    79	function delegatedChildSummaries(message: GeminxMessage): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
    80	  const lineage = message.delegation_lineage;
    81	  if (!lineage) return [];
    82	  const ids = Array.from(new Set([
    83	    ...activeDelegationChildIds(lineage),
    84	    ...(Array.isArray(lineage.attempts)
    85	      ? lineage.attempts.map((attempt) => attempt.child_message_id).filter((id): id is string => Boolean(id))
    86	      : [])
    87	  ])).slice(-8);
    88	  return ids.map((id) => {
    89	    const child = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(id);
    90	    const attempt = [...(lineage.attempts ?? [])].reverse().find((row) => row.child_message_id === id);
    91	    if (!child) {
    92	      return {
    93	        child_message_id: id,
    94	        executor: attempt?.executor ?? null,
    95	        status: attempt?.status ?? "UNKNOWN",
    96	        reason: attempt?.reason ?? null,
    97	        tool: attempt?.tool ?? null,
    98	        missing: true
    99	      };
   100	    }
   101	    const payload = child.payload && typeof child.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(child.payload)
   102	      ? child.payload as Record<string, unknown>
   103	      : {};
   104	    return {
   105	      child_message_id: child.message_id,
   106	      correlation_id: child.correlation_id,
   107	      executor: attempt?.executor ?? child.recipient,
   108	      recipient: child.recipient,
   109	      status: child.status,
   110	      reason: attempt?.reason ?? null,
   111	      tool: attempt?.tool ?? null,
   112	      objective: readOptionalString(payload.objective),
   113	      owned_surface: readOptionalString(payload.owned_surface),
   114	      created_at: child.created_at,
   115	      completed_at: child.completed_at ?? null,
   116	      last_heartbeat_at: child.last_heartbeat_at ?? null,
   117	      receipt_id: child.receipt_id ?? null,
   118	      attempt_count: child.attempt_count ?? 0,
   119	      last_error: child.last_error ?? null,
   120	      activity: Array.isArray(child.activity) ? child.activity.slice(-12) : []
   121	    };
   122	  });
   123	}

   125	function buildCompanionRoutingReceipt(params: {
   126	  intent: string;
   127	  correlation_id: string;
   128	  conversation_id: string | null;
   129	  message_id: string | null;
   130	  chosen_route: string;
   131	  fast_plan_action?: string | null;
   132	  read_only?: boolean;
   133	  durable_dispatched?: boolean;
   134	}) {
   135	  const intentSha256 = createHash("sha256").update(params.intent).digest("hex").slice(0, 16);
   136	  const receipt = {
   137	    intent_sha256: intentSha256,
   138	    intent_length: params.intent.length,
   139	    correlation_id: params.correlation_id,
   140	    conversation_id: params.conversation_id,
   141	    message_id: params.message_id,
   142	    chosen_route: params.chosen_route,
   143	    fast_plan_action: params.fast_plan_action ?? null,
   144	    read_only: params.read_only ?? false,
   145	    durable_dispatched: params.durable_dispatched ?? false,
   146	    at: new Date().toISOString()
   147	  };
   148	  console.log(`[companion-routing] ${JSON.stringify(receipt)}`);
   149	  return receipt;
   150	}

   152	const AUTONOMOUS_FOUNDER_AUTHORITY = "ROB_PREAPPROVED_GEMINX_SELF_MAINTENANCE_V1";

   154	function hasGlobalNoMutationIntent(normalized: string): boolean {
   155	  normalized = normalized.replace(/’/g, "'");
   156	  // A scoped prohibition, e.g. "Do not change production code", must NOT
   157	  // convert an authorised temporary-scratch write/delete task to READ_ONLY.
   158	  // "do not change" is NOT by itself a global safety declaration.
   159	  const globalOnly = [
   160	    /\bread[- ]?only\b/i,
   161	    /\bjust (?:audit|inspect|verify|check|review)\b/i,
   162	    /\b(?:do not|don't) (?:change|modify|fix|edit|repair) anything\b/i,
   163	    /\b(?:do not|don't) (?=[^.!?\n]{0,160}\banything\b)[^.!?\n]{0,160}\b(?:change|modify|fix|edit|repair|deploy|send|publish|spend)\b[^.!?\n]{0,160}\banything\b/i,
   164	    /\bwithout (?:changing|modifying|fixing|repairing) anything\b/i,
   165	    /\b(?:do not|don't) (?:make )?(?:any )?changes?(?=\s*(?:[.!?,;\n]|$))/i,
   166	    /\bno changes?(?=\s*(?:[.!?,;\n]|$))/i,
   167	    /\bwithout (?:changes?|modifications?)(?=\s*(?:[.!?,;\n]|$))/i
   168	  ];
   169	  return globalOnly.some(pattern => pattern.test(normalized));
   170	}
   171	
   172	function hasScopedNoMutationInstruction(normalized: string): boolean {
   173	  normalized = normalized.replace(/’/g, "'");
   174	  return /\b(do not (?:change|modify|fix|edit|deploy|run|repair)|don't (?:change|modify|fix|edit|deploy|run|repair))\b/i.test(normalized);
   175	}
   176	
   177	function hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized: string): boolean {
   178	  normalized = normalized.replace(/’/g, "'");
   179	  return normalized
   180	    .split(/[\n.!?;]+/)
   181	    .map((segment) => segment.trim())
   182	    .filter(Boolean)
   183	    .some((segment) => {
   184	      if (/^(?:do not|don't|never|no)\b/i.test(segment)) return false;
   185	      if (
   186	        /\bread[- ]?only\b/i.test(segment) &&
   187	        !/\b(?:create|write|modify|edit|delete|remove|cancel|terminate|repair|fix|apply|implement|deploy|publish)\b/i.test(segment)
   188	      ) {
   189	        return false;
   190	      }
   191	      if (
   192	        /^(?:go ahead and |please )?(?:check|inspect|verify|audit|review)\b.{0,80}\b(?:status|health|check|receipts?|sha|inbox|queue)\b/i.test(segment) &&
   193	        !/\b(?:run|execute|launch|stage|dispatch|send|deploy|write|create|delete)\b/i.test(segment)
   194	      ) {
   195	        return false;
   196	      }
   197	      return (
   198	        /\b(?:create|write|modify|edit|delete|remove|cancel|terminate|repair|fix|apply|implement|deploy|run|execute|dispatch|stage|approve|call|perform|send|publish|click|type|submit|start|launch)\b/i.test(segment) ||
   199	        /\b(?:run|execute|launch|stage|dispatch)\s+[^.!?\n]{0,40}\b(?:canary|ambush|pipeline|factory)\b/i.test(segment) ||
   200	        /\b(?:canary|ambush)\s+[^.!?\n]{0,40}\b(?:run|execution|dispatch)\b/i.test(segment) ||
   201	        /\b(?:office_digital_ambush_run|office_digital_ambush_stage)\b/i.test(segment)
   202	      );
   203	    });
   204	}
   205	
   206	export function isOperatorStatusInspectionIntent(intent: string): boolean {
   207	  const normalized = intent.trim().toLowerCase();
   208	  if (!normalized) return false;
   209	  if (
   210	    /\b(?:run|execute|launch|stage|dispatch)\s+[^.!?\n]{0,40}\b(?:canary|ambush|pipeline|factory)\b/i.test(normalized) ||
   211	    /\b(?:office_digital_ambush_run|office_digital_ambush_stage)\b/i.test(normalized)
   212	  ) {
   213	    return false;
   214	  }
   215	  return (
   216	    /\bfully operational\b/i.test(normalized)
   217	    || /\b(?:geminx|geminex|operator|runtime|routing|tools?|capabilit(?:y|ies))\b.{0,80}\b(?:operational|available|live|working|healthy|ready|connected|configured|missing|broken|status)\b/i.test(normalized)
   218	    || /\b(?:what|which)\s+(?:tools?|capabilit(?:y|ies))\b/i.test(normalized)
   219	    || /\bwhat can (?:you|geminx|geminex) actually do\b/i.test(normalized)
   220	  );
   221	}

   223	export function isSameObjectiveContinuationIntent(intent: string): boolean {
   224	  const normalized = intent.trim().toLowerCase();
   225	  if (!normalized) return false;
   226	  return /^(?:continue|carry on|keep going|resume|retry|try again|retest|rerun|follow up|continue the task|keep working|go on)(?:\b|[.!?])/i.test(normalized)
   227	    || /\b(?:continue|resume|carry on|keep going)\s+(?:it|that|the same|the task|the job|this)\b/i.test(normalized);
   228	}
   229	
   230	export function isReadOnlyDurableCompanion(intent: string, isFounderAuthorized = false): boolean {
   231	  const normalized = intent.trim().toLowerCase();
   232	  if (!normalized) return false;
   233	  if (isConversationalOnlyIntent(normalized, isFounderAuthorized)) return false;
   234	
   235	  if (hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized)) return false;
   236	
   237	  const mutationSignal =
   238	    (/\b(fix|repair|patch|change|update|edit|write|delete|remove|create|deploy|restart|rebuild|rerun|retest|execute|dispatch|stage|approve|call|perform|continue into the repair|make it work|resolve|schedule|purge|cancel|launch)\b/i.test(normalized) ||
   239	      /\b(?:run|execute|launch|stage|dispatch)\s+[^.!?\n]{0,40}\b(?:canary|ambush|pipeline|factory)\b/i.test(normalized) ||
   240	      /\b(?:office_digital_ambush_run|office_digital_ambush_stage)\b/i.test(normalized)) &&
   241	    !/\b(?:digital[- ]ambush status|status of digital[- ]ambush)\b/i.test(normalized);
   242	  if (mutationSignal && !hasGlobalNoMutationIntent(normalized)) return false;
   243	
   244	  if (isOperatorStatusInspectionIntent(normalized)) {
   245	    if (hasGlobalNoMutationIntent(normalized)) return true;
   246	    return !mutationSignal;
   247	  }
   248	
   249	  const concreteReadInspection =
   250	    /\b(?:read|fetch|inspect|verify|check|audit|investigate|review|diagnose|prove)\b/i.test(normalized)
   251	    && /\b(?:github|railway|boot_here\.md|issue\s*#?\d+|pr\s*#?\d+|pull request|deployment|tool receipt|file sha)\b/i.test(normalized);
   252	
   253	  const positiveExternalMutation =
   254	    /\b(?:geminx_scratch_write|create_or_update_file)\b/i.test(normalized)
   255	    || hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized);
   256	
   257	  const noExternalMutation =
   258	    !mutationSignal
   259	    || hasGlobalNoMutationIntent(normalized)
   260	    || (hasScopedNoMutationInstruction(normalized) && !hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized));
   261	
   262	  if (concreteReadInspection && noExternalMutation && !positiveExternalMutation) {
   263	    return true;
   264	  }
   265	
   266	  const auditSignal = /\b(audit|inspect|investigate|verify|check|review|diagnose)\b/i.test(normalized);
   267	  if (!auditSignal) return false;
   268	
   269	  // Global read-only language is authoritative. A scoped prohibition such as
   270	  // "do not deploy production code" is not: the same Founder task may still
   271	  // explicitly require temporary-file writes, child cancellation, browser
   272	  // actions, or other reversible mutations as part of a qualification.
   273	  if (hasGlobalNoMutationIntent(normalized)) return true;
   274	  if (!hasScopedNoMutationInstruction(normalized)) return false;
   275	  return !hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized);
   276	}
   277	
   278	export function isConversationalOnlyIntent(intent: string, isFounderAuthorized = false): boolean {
   279	  const normalized = String(intent || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   280	  if (!normalized) return true;
   281	  if (hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized)) return false;
   282	  // The phrase "do not just explain what you would do" is NOT an instruction
   283	  // to remain in chat when the Founder explicitly ordered a real tool test.
   284	  // Explicit sandbox execution outranks incidental conversational words.
   285	  const actualToolExecution = /\b(?:run|perform|execute)\s+(?:(?:a|an|the)\s+)?(?:(?:real|full|actual|harmless)\s+)?(?:operator|scratch|tool|integration)\s+(?:test|task|workflow)\b|\b(?:create|write|modify|edit|delete)\b[^.!?\n]{0,100}\b(?:scratch|temporary\s+runtime)\s+(?:file|artifact)\b|\bexecute\s+at\s+least\s+\w+\s+independent\s+tool\s+calls\b/i.test(normalized);
   286	  if (actualToolExecution) return false;
   287	
   288	  // FOUNDER-BYPASS GATE (PART 2):
   289	  // When founder authorization is present, direct technical commands bypass conversational demotion.
   290	  // Destructive operations (dropping DBs, deleting production branches/volumes) and raw credential exposure
   291	  // remain strictly gated by the system security policies and tool execution guardrails.
   292	  if (isFounderAuthorized) {
   293	    const isTechnicalCommand = /\b(?:git|status|diff|log|branch|checkout|pull|push|commit|fetch|test|vitest|tsc|build|preflight|capabilities|inspect|audit|verify|check|probe|sync|harness|diagnose|shell|exec|terminal|process|pm2|railway|runway|metricool|zoho|gmail|mcp|docker|container)\b/i.test(normalized);
   294	    if (isTechnicalCommand) {
   295	      return false;
   296	    }
   297	  }
   298	
   299	  return /\b(?:read\s+and\s+reply|conversational(?:ly)?|in\s+conversation|hypothetical(?:ly)?|walk\s+me\s+through|don't\s+trigger|do\s+not\s+trigger|no\s+background\s+job|without\s+a\s+background\s+job|just\s+answer|just\s+explain|tell\s+me\s+how|what\s+would\s+you\s+do)\b/i.test(normalized)
   300	    || (/^(?:explain|describe)\b/i.test(normalized) && !/\b(?:run|execute|apply|deploy|fix|repair|create|delete)\b/i.test(normalized));
   301	}
   302	
   303	export function isPlainTextArtifactRequest(intent: string): boolean {
   304	  const normalized = String(intent || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   305	  if (!normalized) return false;
   306	  const artifactMatch =
   307	    /\b(?:create|write|draft|make|generate|produce|give\s+me|compose|prepare)\s+(?:(?:me|us)\s+)?(?:(?:a|an|the|another|some)\s+)?(?:test|test\s+prompt|test\s+plan|operator\s+test|checklist|plan|instructions|questions|message|email|description|template|wording|copy)\b/i.test(normalized);
   308	  if (!artifactMatch) return false;
   309	  // If an actual external artifact or action was requested, preserve the governed operator route.
   310	  return !/\b(?:github|repository|repo|railway|deploy|commit|push|publish|execute|run\s+(?:the|this|it|a)|save\s+(?:it|this|the)|file|path|scratch|browser|chrome|device|phone|desktop|system\s+operator|read\s+back|tool\s+receipt|write\s+to|create\s+in|upload|send\s+(?:it|this)\s+to)\b/.test(normalized);
   311	}
   312	
   313	export function shouldDispatchDurableCompanion(intent: string, isFounderAuthorized = false): boolean {
   314	  const normalized = intent.trim().toLowerCase();
   315	  if (!normalized) return false;
   316	  if (isConversationalOnlyIntent(normalized, isFounderAuthorized)) return false;
   317	  if (/\b(?:read\s+and\s+reply|reply\s+only|just\s+talk|just\s+answer)\b/i.test(normalized)) return false;
   318	  // A requested test/prompt in chat is a deliverable, not an autonomous operation.
   319	  // Routing these into the durable worker requires proof of external mutation and
   320	  // caused repeated paid retries with no file/tool objective.
   321	  if (isPlainTextArtifactRequest(intent)) return false;
   322	
   323	  const explicitExecutionOverride =
   324	    (/\b(do it|go ahead|make the changes?|apply the changes?|implement it|deploy it|build it|start it|continue the task)\b/i.test(normalized)
   325	      || /\b(fix it|repair it)\b/i.test(normalized))
   326	    && !/\b(?:so\s+(?:that\s+)?(?:we|i|you)\s+can\s+(?:fix|repair)\s+it|why\s+can['’]?t\s+you\s+(?:fix|repair)\s+it|can\s+we\s+(?:fix|repair)\s+it)\b/i.test(normalized);
   327	  const explicitNoMutation =
   328	    hasGlobalNoMutationIntent(normalized)
   329	    || (hasScopedNoMutationInstruction(normalized) && !hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized));
   330	
   331	  // A request for an explanation is still a live model turn, even when it
   332	  // mentions broken tools or a failed system. Do not hijack it into a paid
   333	  // autonomous repair from a keyword. Explicit action instructions can still
   334	  // dispatch a durable task.
   335	  const explanationLead =
   336	    /^(?:why\b|what(?:'s| is) wrong\b|what (?:caused|went wrong|failed)\b|i need to know\b)/i.test(normalized);
   337	  if (explanationLead && !explicitExecutionOverride &&
   338	      !/\b(?:please|now|go ahead|you must)\s+(?:fix|repair|patch|deploy|implement|execute)\b/i.test(normalized)) {
   339	    return false;
   340	  }
   341	
   342	  // A tool-backed read-only audit is durable work. Mutation authority is controlled
   343	  // separately by the read_only flag and the registry's hard read-only tool guard.
   344	  if (isReadOnlyDurableCompanion(intent, isFounderAuthorized)) return true;
   345	
   346	  // Direct statements that GeminX repaired/fixed nothing are self-repair signals,
   347	  // even when phrased as a report rather than an imperative. Keep the literal
   348	  // fallback as well as the named-agent form so model punctuation/name drift
   349	  // cannot demote a repair objective back to ordinary chat.
   350	  if (
   351	    normalized.includes("fixed nothing")
   352	    || normalized.includes("repaired nothing")
   353	    || normalized.includes("changed nothing")
   354	    || /\b(?:geminx|geminex)\b.{0,48}\b(?:fixed|repaired|changed)\s+nothing\b/i.test(normalized)
   355	    || /\bwhy\s+can['’]?t\s+(?:geminx|geminex)\b/i.test(normalized)
   356	  ) return true;
   357	
   358	  const repairSignal =
   359	    /\b(broken|not working|doesn['’]?t work|won['’]?t work|can['’]?t(?:\s+[a-z0-9_-]+){0,3}\s+fix|cannot fix|unable to fix|nothing (?:was |has been )?fixed|fixed nothing|can['’]?t do (?:it|anything)|unable to do (?:it|anything)|stuck|keeps? (?:failing|breaking)|failed|failure|error|bug|interrupted|needs? attention|not published|blocked|stopped|crashed|regression|same (?:error|problem)|what['’]?s wrong)\b/i.test(normalized);
   360	
   361	  // Other direct no-change requests stay conversational. Do not treat quoted words such
   362	  // as "fix it" inside "do not fix it" as execution authority.
   363	  if (explicitNoMutation) return false;
   364	  const informationalLead =
   365	    /^(what|why|how|which|who|where|when|list|tell me|show me|explain|review|assess|give me|i need to know)\b/i.test(normalized);
   366	  // Diagnostic questions containing "error", "broken" or "failed" must
   367	  // reach a model. Those nouns alone do not authorise a background mutation.
   368	  if (informationalLead && !explicitExecutionOverride && !hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized)) return false;
   369	  if (repairSignal) return true;
   370	
   371	  const isGeneralImageRequest =
   372	    /\b(?:create|make|generate|draw|render|paint|produce|show\s+me)\b[^.!?\n]{0,40}\b(?:image|picture|photo|illustration|drawing|artwork|portrait)\b/i.test(normalized)
   373	    && !/\b(?:digital\s+ambush|outreach|prospect|campaign|website|homepage|mockup|github|railway|deploy|email|send|zoho|gmail)\b/i.test(normalized);
   374	  if (isGeneralImageRequest) return false;
   375	
   376	  const browserExecution = /\b(browser|website|web page|log in|login|sign in|navigate|go to|open the browser|open .*site|click|fill (?:in|out)|submit the form)\b/i.test(normalized);
   377	  const physicalExecution =
   378	    /\b(screenshot|screen ?shot|capture (?:the )?screen|take (?:a )?(?:screen ?shot|picture)|live (?:view|feed)|move (?:the )?(?:mouse|cursor)|mouse|cursor|keyboard|type (?:on|into)|tap|swipe|scroll|launch (?:the )?app|open (?:the )?app|control (?:the )?(?:pc|computer|phone|tablet|device)|take over (?:the )?(?:pc|computer|phone|tablet|device))\b/i.test(normalized);
   379	  return (
   380	    browserExecution ||
   381	    physicalExecution ||
   382	    hasPositiveMutationRequirement(normalized) ||
   383	    /\b(fix|repair|build|deploy|implement|wire|finish|complete|restore|recover|configure|connect|set\s*up|merge|update|apply|create|run|execute|dispatch|stage|publish|send|post|write|remove|delete|continue|resume|retry|retest|rerun|follow\s*up|ambush|canary)\b/i.test(normalized) ||
   384	    /\b(?:office_digital_ambush_run|office_digital_ambush_stage|office_digital_ambush_status)\b/i.test(normalized)
   385	  );
   386	}
   387	
   388	/** Every office agent gets the SAME canonical durable owner for material work.
   389	 * Chat-only turns stay synchronous. Parallel-chat override is an explicit caller mode,
   390	 * not a permission to silently drop an acknowledged long-running objective.
   391	 */
   392	export function shouldDispatchOfficeDurableCompanion(
   393	  officeAgent: OfficeAgentId,
   394	  intent: string,
   395	  isFounderAuthorized = false,
   396	  parallelChatOnly = false
   397	): boolean {
   398	  if (parallelChatOnly || !isOfficeAgentId(officeAgent)) return false;
   399	  return (officeAgent === "neo" && founderAddressedAstra(intent))
   400	    || shouldDispatchDurableCompanion(intent, isFounderAuthorized);
   401	}
   402	
   403	export function shouldDispatchAgLiveDesktop(intent: string): boolean {
   404	  const normalized = intent.trim().toLowerCase();
   405	  if (!normalized) return false;
   406	
   407	  // IMPORTANT: AG's cloud chat is not the desktop motor. A pasted design brief
   408	  // can contain "desktop", "open" and "check" in unrelated sentences. The
   409	  // desktop controller must only receive an actual foreground PC command.
   410	  const firstClause = normalized.split(/[\n.!?](?:\s|$)/, 1)[0]
   411	    .replace(/^(?:(?:ag|listen|hey|please)[,:\s]+)+/i, "")
   412	    .trim();
   413	  const commandLead =
   414	    /^(?:(?:can|could|would|will)\s+you\s+|i\s+(?:want|need)\s+you\s+to\s+|please\s+)?(?:open|launch|start|click|double[- ]?click|type|write|enter|press|move|drag|scroll|focus|select|close|switch|control|use|look at|inspect|check|read|describe|show|capture|take a screenshot|take a screen shot)\b/i.test(firstClause);
   415	  const directVisualQuestion =
   416	    /^(?:what(?:'s| is) on (?:my|the|this) (?:screen|desktop|monitor)|what(?:'s| is) (?:happening|open|visible) on (?:my|the|this) (?:screen|desktop|monitor))\b/i.test(firstClause);
   417	  if (!commandLead && !directVisualQuestion) return false;
   418	
   419	  const desktopSurface =
   420	    /\b(?:desktop|screen|monitor|display|window|this pc|my pc|computer|notepad|calculator|paint|file explorer|explorer|powershell|terminal|cmd|chrome)\b/i.test(firstClause);
   421	  const explicitPhysicalAction =
   422	    /\b(?:move (?:the )?(?:mouse|cursor)|click(?: on)?|double[- ]?click|type (?:this|into|in|on)|press (?:enter|escape|tab|space|ctrl|alt|shift)|scroll|drag|take (?:a )?screen ?shot|capture (?:the )?screen|open notepad|launch notepad|control (?:the )?(?:pc|computer|desktop))\b/i.test(firstClause);
   423	
   424	  // Host availability is NOT part of this predicate. Only an explicitly
   425	  // requested local action may be blocked by a disconnected System Operator.
   426	  return (desktopSurface && (commandLead || directVisualQuestion)) || (commandLead && explicitPhysicalAction);
   427	}
   428	
   429	export function founderLiveAck(intent: string): string {
   430	  // An HTTP 202 is a durable acceptance receipt, not proof that an action ran.
   431	  // Do not promise to test, send, deploy, or repair before any tool executes.
   432	  return "Queued";
   433	}

   435	export function durableFounderJobId(requestMessageId: string | null): string {
   436	  const fallback = String(Date.now()) + "-" + randomUUID();
   437	  const seed = (requestMessageId ?? fallback).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_").slice(0, 140);
   438	  return seed.startsWith("founder-job-") ? seed : "founder-job-" + seed;
   439	}

   441	function enqueueDurableFounderJob(
   442	  intent: string,
   443	  correlationId: string,
   444	  requestMessageId: string | null,
   445	  modelPreference: string,
   446	  attachments: FounderAttachmentRef[],
   447	  conversationId: string,
   448	  readOnly = false,
   449	  clientContext: Record<string, unknown> | null = null,
   450	  officeAgent: OfficeAgentId = "neo",
   451	  companyScope: string | null = null
   452	): GeminxMessage {
   453	  const messageId = durableFounderJobId(requestMessageId);
   454	  const existing = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(messageId);
   455	  if (existing) return existing;
   456	
   457	  const message: GeminxMessage = {
   458	    message_id: messageId,
   459	    correlation_id: correlationId,
   460	    sender: "newton",
   461	    recipient: (officeAgent === "ag" || conversationId.startsWith("ag-")) ? "ag" : "geminx",
   462	    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   463	    priority: "HIGH",
   464	    type: "founder_autonomous_job",
   465	    payload: {
   466	      command: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1",
   467	      authority: AUTONOMOUS_FOUNDER_AUTHORITY,
   468	      model_preference: (officeAgent === "ag" || conversationId.startsWith("ag-")) && (!modelPreference || modelPreference === "deepseek")
   469	        ? "antigravity"
   470	        : modelPreference,
   471	      attachments,
   472	      conversation_id: conversationId,
   473	      objective: intent,
   474	      read_only: readOnly,
   475	      office_agent: (officeAgent === "ag" || conversationId.startsWith("ag-")) ? "ag" : officeAgent,
   476	      company_scope: companyScope,
   477	      acceptance_criteria: ["sophie", "brian", "mark", "kerry", "ads"].includes(officeAgent)
   478	        ? [
   479	            "Use permitted tools directly to inspect or execute the requested task.",
   480	            "Report real tool readbacks and findings honestly without inventing completion.",
   481	            "If a tool or service is blocked, explain the exact error and next step directly to the Founder."
   482	          ]
   483	        : (readOnly
   484	          ? [
   485	              "Inspect current truth using live governed tools wherever the requested source is available.",
   486	              "Perform no mutation, deployment, write, send, publish, navigation, form submission, repair or configuration change.",
   487	              "For current production facts, prefer exact live repository/runtime/provider receipts over Agent Brain or remembered state.",
   488	              "If a requested source cannot be reached live, state that limitation explicitly instead of substituting stale context as current truth.",
   489	              "Return durable tool receipts and never invent completion or freshness."
   490	            ]
   491	          : [
   492	              "Inspect current truth before changing anything.",
   493	              "Fix the smallest durable root cause for each mechanically repairable blocker.",
   494	              "If the task exposes another mechanically repairable GeminX-owned fault while you are working, repair it immediately through the existing governed owner instead of stopping to ask Rob for permission that the standing self-maintenance authority already grants.",
   495	              "Rerun the same failed probe and relevant regressions after each repair.",
   496	              "Continue until the objective is mechanically proven or a real credential, permission, MFA, payment, legal, physical-host or unavailable-runtime boundary remains.",
   497	              "Return durable receipts and never invent completion."
   498	            ]),
   499	      constraints: ["sophie", "brian", "mark", "kerry", "ads"].includes(officeAgent)
   500	        ? [
   501	            "Use only permitted domain tools for this specialist office role.",
   502	            "Do not expose secrets or credentials.",
   503	            "Do not invent completions without live evidence."
   504	          ]
   505	        : (readOnly
   506	          ? [
   507	              "READ_ONLY_MODE: only non-mutating tools are permitted by the runtime registry.",
   508	              "Do not expose secrets or credentials.",
   509	              "Do not claim current production, deployment or provider state from Agent Brain alone.",
   510	              "Preserve exact repository, branch, SHA, deployment and run identities in the report.",
   511	              "If live evidence conflicts with Brain context, report the live evidence and identify the stale context rather than blending them."
   512	            ]
   513	          : [
   514	              "Reuse canonical systems and the existing repair lineage; do not create duplicate queues, controllers or campaigns.",
   515	              "Do not expose secrets or credentials.",
   516	              "Do not weaken protected-action or founder-approval boundaries; login/MFA/CAPTCHA, money movement, purchases, legal acceptance, credential-secret disclosure and account-ownership/security changes still require the appropriate Founder/provider step.",
   517	              "Do not turn a repairable code/runtime/configuration fault into a human blocker merely because it is inconvenient.",
   518	              "Preserve exact run, deployment and repository identity when repairing existing work."
   519	            ]),
   520	      verified_current_state: {
   521	        source: "founder_hq_companion",
   522	        correlation_id: correlationId,
   523	        requested_model: modelPreference,
   524	        dispatched_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   525	        ...(clientContext ? { client_context: clientContext } : {})
   526	      }
   527	    },
   528	    requires_reply: true,
   529	    status: "QUEUED",
   530	    activity: [{
   531	      event_id: randomUUID(),
   532	      at: new Date().toISOString(),
   533	      phase: "queued",
   534	      status: "running",
   535	      label: "Queued in GeminX"
   536	    }],
   537	    followups: [],
   538	    attempt_count: 0,
   539	    max_attempts: 0,
   540	    last_heartbeat_at: new Date().toISOString()
   541	  };
   542	  backgroundCommsStore.saveMessage(message);
   543	  return message;
   544	}

   546	function activeFounderJobForConversation(conversationIdRaw: string | null, explicitRunIdRaw: string | null): GeminxMessage | null {
   547	  const explicitRunId = readOptionalString(explicitRunIdRaw);
   548	  const conversationId = readOptionalString(conversationIdRaw);
   549	  const conversation = conversationId ? conversationStore.get(conversationId) : null;
   550	  const candidateId = explicitRunId ?? readOptionalString(conversation?.active_run_id);
   551	  if (!candidateId) return null;
   552	  const message = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(candidateId);
   553	  if (!message || message.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") return null;
   554	  return message;
   555	}
   556	
   557	function appendFounderFollowup(message: GeminxMessage, text: string): { followup_id: string; status: GeminxMessage["status"] } {
   558	  const resumableBoundary = ["WAITING_HUMAN", "BLOCKED", "FAILED"].includes(message.status);
   559	  if (resumableBoundary) {
   560	    // A founder-authorised continuation stays under the original objective ID.
   561	    // The worker must reconcile any prior unknown tool outcome before mutation.
   562	    message.attempt_count = 0;
   563	    message.status = "QUEUED";
   564	    delete message.completed_at;
   565	    delete message.receipt_id;
   566	    delete message.reply_payload;
   567	    delete message.next_attempt_at;
   568	    message.last_error = undefined;
   569	  }
   570	
   571	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
   572	  const followup = {
   573	    followup_id: randomUUID(),
   574	    text: text.slice(0, 12000),
   575	    created_at: now,
   576	    status: "PENDING" as const
   577	  };
   578	  message.followups = [...(Array.isArray(message.followups) ? message.followups : []), followup].slice(-30);
   579	  message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
   580	  message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
   581	    event_id: randomUUID(),
   582	    at: now,
   583	    phase: "followup",
   584	    status: "running" as const,
   585	    label: resumableBoundary
   586	      ? "Founder supplied continuation input · resuming the same task"
   587	      : "Founder continuation attached to the same task"
   588	  }].slice(-80);
   589	  backgroundCommsStore.saveMessage(message);
   590	  return { followup_id: followup.followup_id, status: message.status };
   591	}

   617	function readOptionalString(value: unknown): string | null {
   618	  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0 ? value.trim() : null;
   619	}
   620	
   621	const FOUNDER_MODEL_PREFERENCES = new Set([
   622	  "antigravity",
   623	  "gemini-pro",
   624	  "deepseek",
   625	  "deepseek-flash",
   626	  "deepseek-v4-pro",
   627	  "gpt-5.6-luna",
   628	  "gpt-5.6-terra",
   629	  "gpt-5.6-sol",
   630	  "astra"
   631	]);
   632	
   633	function readFounderModelPreference(value: unknown, fallback = "deepseek"): string {
   634	  const candidate = (readOptionalString(value) ?? fallback).toLowerCase();
   635	  if (!FOUNDER_MODEL_PREFERENCES.has(candidate)) {
   636	    throw new Error("model_preference must be 'antigravity', 'gemini-pro', 'deepseek', 'deepseek-flash', 'deepseek-v4-pro', 'gpt-5.6-luna', 'gpt-5.6-terra', 'gpt-5.6-sol', or 'astra'");
   637	  }
   638	  return candidate;
   639	}
   640	
   641	export function founderAddressedAstra(intent: string): boolean {
   642	  return /^\s*astra\s*:/i.test(intent);
   643	}
   644	
   645	export function stripFounderAstraAddress(intent: string): string {
   646	  return founderAddressedAstra(intent) ? intent.replace(/^\s*astra\s*:\s*/i, "").trim() : intent;
   647	}
   648	
   649	function resolveFounderClientContext(
   650	  value: unknown,
   651	  req?: { headers?: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined> }
   652	): Record<string, unknown> | null {
   653	  const reqHeaders = req?.headers ?? {};
   654	  const headerClean = (val: string | string[] | undefined) => Array.isArray(val) ? val[0] ?? "" : val ?? "";
   655	  const headerPlatform = headerClean(reqHeaders["x-client-platform"]).trim().toLowerCase();
   656	  const headerDeviceClass = headerClean(reqHeaders["x-client-device"]).trim().toLowerCase();
   657	  const headerWidth = Number(headerClean(reqHeaders["x-viewport-width"])) || 0;
   658	  const headerHeight = Number(headerClean(reqHeaders["x-viewport-height"])) || 0;
   659	  const headerSurface = headerClean(reqHeaders["x-active-view"]).trim();
   660	
   661	  let input: Record<string, unknown>;
   662	  if (value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
   663	    input = { ...(value as Record<string, unknown>) };
   664	    if (!input.platform && headerPlatform) input.platform = headerPlatform;
   665	    if (!input.device_class && headerDeviceClass) input.device_class = headerDeviceClass;
   666	    if (!input.surface && headerSurface) input.surface = headerSurface;
   667	    if (headerWidth || headerHeight) {
   668	      const curVp = input.viewport && typeof input.viewport === "object" && !Array.isArray(input.viewport)
   669	        ? input.viewport as Record<string, unknown>
   670	        : {};
   671	      input.viewport = {
   672	        width: Number(curVp.width) || headerWidth,
   673	        height: Number(curVp.height) || headerHeight
   674	      };
   675	    }
   676	  } else if (headerPlatform || headerDeviceClass || headerWidth || headerSurface) {
   677	    input = {
   678	      platform: headerPlatform,
   679	      device_class: headerDeviceClass,
   680	      surface: headerSurface,
   681	      viewport: { width: headerWidth, height: headerHeight }
   682	    };
   683	  } else {
   684	    return null;
   685	  }
   686	  const clean = (raw: unknown, max = 120) => String(raw ?? "").replace(/[\r\n\0]/g, " ").trim().slice(0, max);
   687	  const platform = clean(input.platform, 40).toLowerCase();
   688	  const deviceClass = clean(input.device_class, 24).toLowerCase();
   689	  const model = clean(input.model, 120);
   690	  const surface = clean(input.surface, 40) || "founder_hq";
   691	  const viewport = input.viewport && typeof input.viewport === "object" && !Array.isArray(input.viewport)
   692	    ? input.viewport as Record<string, unknown>
   693	    : {};
   694	  const context: Record<string, unknown> = {
   695	    surface,
   696	    platform: platform || "unknown",
   697	    device_class: ["phone", "tablet", "desktop"].includes(deviceClass) ? deviceClass : "unknown",
   698	    model: model || null,
   699	    mobile: input.mobile === true,
   700	    viewport: {
   701	      width: Math.max(0, Math.min(10000, Number(viewport.width) || 0)),
   702	      height: Math.max(0, Math.min(10000, Number(viewport.height) || 0))
   703	    }
   704	  };
   705	
   706	  let candidates = normalizeDeviceFleet(getSystemOperatorBroker().listStatuses())
   707	    .filter((row) => row.connected === true);
   708	
   709	  if (platform === "android") {
   710	    candidates = candidates.filter(isAndroidDeviceWorker);
   711	    if (model) {
   712	      const lowerModel = model.toLowerCase();
   713	      candidates = candidates.filter((row) => {
   714	        const metadata = row.metadata && typeof row.metadata === "object" && !Array.isArray(row.metadata)
   715	          ? row.metadata as Record<string, unknown>
   716	          : {};
   717	        return String(metadata.model ?? "").trim().toLowerCase() === lowerModel;
   718	      });
   719	    } else {
   720	      candidates = [];
   721	    }
   722	  } else if (platform.includes("win")) {
   723	    candidates = candidates.filter(isWindowsSystemOperator);
   724	  } else {
   725	    candidates = [];
   726	  }
   727	
   728	  if (candidates.length === 1) {
   729	    const row = candidates[0]!;
   730	    const metadata = row.metadata && typeof row.metadata === "object" && !Array.isArray(row.metadata)
   731	      ? row.metadata as Record<string, unknown>
   732	      : {};
   733	    context.paired_device_id = String(row.host_id ?? "");
   734	    context.paired_device_label = String(metadata.label ?? metadata.model ?? row.host_id ?? "").slice(0, 120);
   735	    context.paired_device_model = String(metadata.model ?? "").slice(0, 120) || null;
   736	    context.binding_status = "exact_live_match";
   737	  } else {
   738	    context.binding_status = candidates.length > 1 ? "ambiguous" : "unbound";
   739	    context.matching_live_devices = candidates.length;
   740	  }
   741	
   742	  return context;
   743	}
   744	
   745	function createCrossLaneTruthService(projectRoot: string): CrossLaneTruthService {
   746	  const fixedNow = readOptionalString(process.env.GEMINIX_SHARED_TRUTH_NOW);
   747	  if (fixedNow) {
   748	    const parsed = new Date(fixedNow);
   749	    if (!Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
   750	      return new CrossLaneTruthService({
   751	        projectRoot,
   752	        now: () => parsed
   753	      });
   754	    }
   755	  }
   756	
   757	  return new CrossLaneTruthService({ projectRoot });
   758	}
   759	
   760	function taskCapabilityPreflight(taskPackage: Partial<GeminxTaskPackage>) {
   761	  const requested = taskPackage.required_executables ?? [];
   762	  if (!Array.isArray(requested) || requested.length > 8) {
   763	    return { valid: false as const, error: "task_package.required_executables must be an array with at most 8 entries" };
   764	  }
   765	
   766	  const normalized: string[] = [];
   767	  for (const value of requested) {
   768	    if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{1,32}$/.test(value.trim())) {
   769	      return { valid: false as const, error: "task_package.required_executables contains an unsafe executable name" };
   770	    }
   771	    normalized.push(value.trim().toLowerCase());
   772	  }
   773	
   774	  const repoRoot = typeof taskPackage.repo_root === "string" ? taskPackage.repo_root : null;
   775	  const preflight = preflightGeminxRuntime(["git", ...normalized], process.env, repoRoot);
   776	  return { valid: true as const, preflight };
   777	}
   778	
   779	async function executeCreatorRobloxRun(body: CreateRunRequestBody) {
   780	  const rawCreatorIntent = readOptionalString(body.raw_creator_intent) ?? readOptionalString(body.intent);
   781	  if (!rawCreatorIntent) {
   782	    return {
   783	      status: 400,
   784	      payload: { ok: false, error: "Creator build is missing the raw game idea", data: null }
   785	    };
   786	  }
   787	
   788	  const publishAuthorized = body.publish_authorized === true
   789	    || (body.developer_testing_mode === true && body.require_fresh_publish === true);
   790	  if (!publishAuthorized) {
   791	    return {
   792	      status: 409,
   793	      payload: { ok: false, error: "Creator publish is not authorised for this request", data: null }
   794	    };
   795	  }
   796	
   797	  const correlationId = readOptionalString(body.correlation_id) ?? `creator:${randomUUID()}`;
   798	  const projectId = `creator-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
   799	  const result = await toolRegistry.execute({
   800	    tool_call_id: `roblox:${randomUUID()}`,
   801	    correlation_id: correlationId,
   802	    tool: "roblox_generate_project",
   803	    args: {
   804	      repo_root: `runtime-builds/${projectId}`,
   805	      project_id: projectId,
   806	      template_id: "roblox-obby",
   807	      style: "custom",
   808	      requested_mechanics: [],
   809	      game_brief: rawCreatorIntent
   810	    }
   811	  });
   812	
   813	  const trace = toolRegistry.trace(correlationId);
   814	  if (!result.ok || result.status !== "succeeded") {
   815	    return {
   816	      status: result.status_code && result.status_code >= 400 ? result.status_code : 502,
   817	      payload: {
   818	        ok: false,
   819	        error: result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? "Roblox creator build failed",
   820	        data: {
   821	          route: "roblox_builder",
   822	          correlation_id: correlationId,
   823	          active_run_id: projectId,
   824	          tool_calls: trace,
   825	          tool_trace: trace
   826	        }
   827	      }
   828	    };
   829	  }
   830	
   831	  const data = result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(result.data)
   832	    ? result.data as Record<string, unknown>
   833	    : {};
   834	  return {
   835	    status: 200,
   836	    payload: ok({
   837	      route: "roblox_builder",
   838	      summary: typeof data.message === "string" ? data.message : "Roblox Creator build completed.",
   839	      message: typeof data.message === "string" ? data.message : "Roblox Creator build completed.",
   840	      correlation_id: correlationId,
   841	      active_run_id: projectId,
   842	      ...data,
   843	      tool_calls: trace,
   844	      tool_trace: trace
   845	    })
   846	  };
   847	}
   848	
   849	export function runsRouter(projectRoot = process.cwd()): Router {
   850	  const router = Router();
   851	  const crossLaneTruthService = createCrossLaneTruthService(projectRoot);
   852	  const taskEngine = new GeminxTaskEngineService();
   853	
   854	
   855	  // Deterministic, non-sending Zoho transport qualification for the existing
   856	  // Digital Ambush lane. The parent /api/v1/runs mount already enforces the
   857	  // Founder/Companion bearer boundary. This route invokes the canonical
   858	  // ToolRegistry directly, so proving SMTP/OAuth authentication never requires
   859	  // a paid model turn and can never send a message.
   860	  router.post("/zoho-mail/status", async (req, res) => {
   861	    const verifyAuth = req.body?.verify_auth === true;
   862	    const correlationId =
   863	      readOptionalString(req.body?.correlation_id) ?? `zoho-status:${randomUUID()}`;
   864	    const receipt = await toolRegistry.execute({
   865	      tool_call_id: `zoho-status:${randomUUID()}`,
   866	      correlation_id: correlationId,
   867	      tool: "zoho_mail_status",
   868	      args: { verify_auth: verifyAuth }
   869	    });
   870	
   871	    const receiptStatus = Number(receipt.status_code ?? (receipt.ok ? 200 : 503));
   872	    const httpStatus = receipt.ok
   873	      ? 200
   874	      : receiptStatus >= 400 && receiptStatus <= 599
   875	        ? receiptStatus
   876	        : 503;
   877	    res.status(httpStatus).json({
   878	      ok: receipt.ok,
   879	      error: receipt.ok
   880	        ? null
   881	        : String(receipt.error ?? receipt.blocked_reason ?? "ZOHO_STATUS_PROBE_FAILED"),
   882	      data: {
   883	        schema_version: "geminx.zoho-status-receipt.v1",
   884	        non_sending: true,
   885	        verify_auth: verifyAuth,
   886	        receipt
   887	      }
   888	    });
   889	  });
   890	
   891	  router.get("/", (_req, res) => {
   892	    const items = taskEngine.listRuns();
   893	    res.json(ok({ items, total: items.length, offset: 0, limit: 50 }));
   894	  });
   895	
   896	  router.get("/background", (req, res) => {
   897	    const conversationId = readOptionalString(req.query.conversation_id);
   898	    const activeOnly = String(req.query.active_only ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   899	    const active = activeOnly === "1" || activeOnly === "true" || activeOnly === "yes";
   900	    const terminal = new Set(["COMPLETED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED"]);
   901	    const items = backgroundCommsStore.listMessages()
   902	      .filter((message) => message.type === "founder_autonomous_job")
   903	      .filter((message) => {
   904	        if (!conversationId) return true;
   905	        const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   906	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
   907	          : {};
   908	        return readOptionalString(payload.conversation_id) === conversationId;
   909	      })
   910	      .filter((message) => !active || !terminal.has(message.status))
   911	      .sort((a, b) => b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at))
   912	      .slice(0, 50)
   913	      .map((message) => {
   914	        const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   915	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
   916	          : {};
   917	        return {
   918	          background_job_id: message.message_id,
   919	          correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
   920	          conversation_id: readOptionalString(payload.conversation_id),
   921	          agent_id: readOptionalString(payload.agent_mode),
   922	          is_active: !terminal.has(message.status),
   923	          status: message.status,
   924	          objective: readOptionalString(payload.objective),
   925	          created_at: message.created_at,
   926	          completed_at: message.completed_at ?? null,
   927	          receipt_id: message.receipt_id ?? null,
   928	          attempt_count: message.attempt_count ?? 0,
   929	          last_heartbeat_at: message.last_heartbeat_at ?? null,
   930	          last_error: message.last_error ?? null,
   931	          delegation_lineage: message.delegation_lineage ?? null,
   932	          delegated_children: delegatedChildSummaries(message),
   933	          activity: Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []
   934	        };
   935	      });
   936	    res.json(ok({ items, total: items.length }));
   937	  });
   938	
   939	  router.get("/background/:id", (req, res) => {
   940	    const message = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(req.params.id);
   941	    if (!message || message.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") {
   942	      res.status(404).json(err("background founder job not found"));
   943	      return;
   944	    }
   945	    // Read-only, canonical server truth for independently verifying same-parent
   946	    // continuation. Never trust a browser-supplied conversation or criterion ID.
   947	    const jobPayload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   948	      ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
   949	    const objectiveContract = message.objective_contract;
   950	    res.json(ok({
   951	      background_job_id: message.message_id,
   952	      conversation_id: readOptionalString(jobPayload.conversation_id),
   953	      objective_contract: objectiveContract ? {
   954	        objective_id: objectiveContract.objective_id,
   955	        acceptance_criteria: objectiveContract.acceptance_criteria.map(item => ({
   956	          id: item.id,
   957	          description: item.description,
   958	          status: item.status,
   959	          evidence_receipt: item.evidence_receipt ?? null
   960	        }))
   961	      } : null,
   962	      correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
   963	      status: message.status,
   964	      created_at: message.created_at,
   965	      completed_at: message.completed_at ?? null,
   966	      receipt_id: message.receipt_id ?? null,
   967	      attempt_count: message.attempt_count ?? 0,
   968	      max_attempts: message.max_attempts ?? 0,
   969	      next_attempt_at: message.next_attempt_at ?? null,
   970	      last_heartbeat_at: message.last_heartbeat_at ?? null,
   971	      last_error: message.last_error ?? null,
   972	      reply_payload: message.reply_payload ?? null,
   973	      delegation_lineage: message.delegation_lineage ?? null,
   974	      delegated_children: delegatedChildSummaries(message),
   975	      activity: Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [],
   976	      followups: Array.isArray(message.followups)
   977	        ? message.followups.map(item => ({
   978	            followup_id: item.followup_id,
   979	            text: item.text,
   980	            created_at: item.created_at,
   981	            status: item.status,
   982	            consumed_at: item.consumed_at ?? null
   983	          }))
   984	        : []
   985	    }));
   986	  });
   987	
   988	  router.post("/background/:id/cancel", (req, res) => {
   989	    const message = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(req.params.id);
   990	    if (!message || message.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") {
   991	      res.status(404).json(err("background founder job not found"));
   992	      return;
   993	    }
   994	    if (["COMPLETED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"].includes(message.status)) {
   995	      res.status(409).json(err("background founder job is already terminal"));
   996	      return;
   997	    }
   998	
   999	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1000	    // Cancelling a parent mission cancels only its currently live children. Sibling
  1001	    // founder jobs and unrelated NEO missions remain untouched.
  1002	    for (const childId of activeDelegationChildIds(message.delegation_lineage ?? { version: "geminx.delegation-lineage.v2", active_child_message_ids: [], attempts: [], reroute_count: 0, model_escalations: [] })) {
  1003	      const child = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(childId);
  1004	      if (!child || ["COMPLETED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"].includes(child.status)) continue;
  1005	      child.status = "CANCELLED";
  1006	      child.completed_at = now;
  1007	      child.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  1008	      delete child.next_attempt_at;
  1009	      child.reply_payload = {
  1010	        status: "CANCELLED",
  1011	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  1012	        route: "parent_cancel",
  1013	        message: "Stopped because the parent NEO mission was cancelled by Rob."
  1014	      };
  1015	      child.activity = [...(Array.isArray(child.activity) ? child.activity : []), {
  1016	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  1017	        at: now,
  1018	        phase: "cancel",
  1019	        status: "blocked" as const,
  1020	        label: "Parent NEO mission cancelled by Rob"
  1021	      }].slice(-80);
  1022	      backgroundCommsStore.saveMessage(child);
  1023	    }
  1024	    message.status = "CANCELLED";
  1025	    message.completed_at = now;
  1026	    message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  1027	    delete message.next_attempt_at;
  1028	    message.last_error = undefined;
  1029	    message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  1030	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  1031	      at: now,
  1032	      phase: "cancel",
  1033	      status: "blocked" as const,
  1034	      label: "Stopped by Rob"
  1035	    }].slice(-80);
  1036	    message.reply_payload = {
  1037	      status: "CANCELLED",
  1038	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  1039	      route: "founder_cancel",
  1040	      message: "Stopped by Rob."
  1041	    };
  1042	    backgroundCommsStore.saveMessage(message);
  1043	
  1044	    const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  1045	      ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  1046	      : {};
  1047	    const conversationId = readOptionalString(payload.conversation_id);
  1048	    if (conversationId) {
  1049	      const record = conversationStore.get(conversationId);
  1050	      if (record) {
  1051	        conversationStore.appendTurn(record, {
  1052	          role: "assistant",
  1053	          content: "Stopped by Rob.",
  1054	          at: now
  1055	        }, {
  1056	          active_run_id: message.message_id,
  1057	          domain: record.domain || "maintenance",
  1058	          summary: record.summary
  1059	        });
  1060	      }
  1061	    }
  1062	
  1063	    res.json(ok({
  1064	      background_job_id: message.message_id,
  1065	      status: "CANCELLED",
  1066	      message: "Stopped by Rob."
  1067	    }));
  1068	  });
  1069	
  1070	  router.post("/background/:id/reset-to-blocked", (req, res) => {
  1071	    const message = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(req.params.id);
  1072	    if (!message || message.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") {
  1073	      res.status(404).json(err("background founder job not found"));
  1074	      return;
  1075	    }
  1076	    const supplied = String(req.headers.authorization || "").replace(/^Bearer\s+/i, "").trim();
  1077	    const companionToken = process.env.GEMINX_COMPANION_TOKEN?.trim();
  1078	    const founderHqToken = process.env.GEMINX_HQ_FOUNDER_TOKEN?.trim();
  1079	    const tokenAuthorized = Boolean((companionToken && supplied === companionToken) || (founderHqToken && supplied === founderHqToken));
  1080	    if (!hasValidFounderSession(req) && !tokenAuthorized) {
  1081	      res.status(403).json(err("founder authority required"));
  1082	      return;
  1083	    }
  1084	    message.status = "BLOCKED";
  1085	    message.last_error = "EXECUTION_FAULT:RECOVERABLE_TOOL_DEGRADATION:step_audit";
  1086	    delete message.completed_at;
  1087	    delete message.receipt_id;
  1088	    delete message.reply_payload;
  1089	    const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  1090	      ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  1091	      : {};
  1092	    payload.founder_reset_fenced = true;
  1093	    delete payload.founder_resume_windows;
  1094	    message.payload = payload;
  1095	    if (message.objective_contract && Array.isArray(message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria)) {
  1096	      message.objective_contract.current_stage = "ACTIVE_EXECUTION";
  1097	      message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria = message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria.map((c: any) => ({
  1098	        ...c,
  1099	        status: "UNVERIFIED",
  1100	        evidence_receipt: null
  1101	      }));
  1102	    }
  1103	    const convId = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object"
  1104	      ? String((message.payload as Record<string, unknown>).conversation_id || "")
  1105	      : "";
  1106	    if (convId.startsWith("ag-") || !message.recipient) {
  1107	      message.recipient = convId.startsWith("ag-") ? "ag" : "geminx";
  1108	    }
  1109	    backgroundCommsStore.saveMessage(message);
  1110	    const originalIntent = String(payload.objective || payload.intent || message.message_id);
  1111	    const originalIntentSha = ConversationStore.intentSha256(originalIntent);
  1112	    conversationStore.saveExecutionCheckpoint({
  1113	      schema_version: "geminx.execution-transcript.v1",
  1114	      objective_id: message.message_id,
  1115	      intent_sha256: originalIntentSha,
  1116	      state: "CLEAN",
  1117	      pending_tool_id: null,
  1118	      messages: [{ role: "user", content: originalIntent }],
  1119	      archived_execution_evidence: [],
  1120	      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
  1121	    });
  1122	    res.json(ok({
  1123	      background_job_id: message.message_id,
  1124	      status: "BLOCKED",
  1125	      conversation_id: convId
  1126	    }));
  1127	  });
  1128	
  1129	  router.post("/background/:id/resume", (req, res) => {
  1130	    const message = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(req.params.id);
  1131	    if (!message || message.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") {
  1132	      res.status(404).json(err("background founder job not found"));
  1133	      return;
  1134	    }
  1135	    if (!["WAITING_HUMAN", "BLOCKED", "FAILED"].includes(message.status)) {
  1136	      res.status(409).json(err("background founder job is not waiting for resume"));
  1137	      return;
  1138	    }
  1139	    // Before making a blocked job runnable, reconcile its exact native tool
  1140	    // checkpoint. A pending/unknown-effect tool MUST NOT be replayed, and an
  1141	    // existing transcript must never be replaced with a synthetic CLEAN one.
  1142	    const originalPayload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  1143	      ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  1144	    const originalIntent = String(originalPayload.objective || originalPayload.intent || message.message_id);
  1145	    const originalIntentSha = ConversationStore.intentSha256(originalIntent);
  1146	    try {
  1147	      const currentCp = conversationStore.getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  1148	      const preflight = inspectFounderResumeCheckpoint(currentCp, message.message_id, originalIntentSha);
  1149	      if (!preflight.allowed) {
  1150	        res.status(409).json(err(preflight.code));
  1151	        return;
  1152	      }
  1153	      if (!currentCp) {
  1154	        conversationStore.saveExecutionCheckpoint({
  1155	          schema_version: "geminx.execution-transcript.v1",
  1156	          objective_id: message.message_id,
  1157	          intent_sha256: originalIntentSha,
  1158	          state: "CLEAN",
  1159	          pending_tool_id: null,
  1160	          messages: [{ role: "user", content: originalIntent }],
  1161	          archived_execution_evidence: [],
  1162	          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
  1163	        });
  1164	      }
  1165	    } catch (cpErr) {
  1166	      console.warn("[runs/resume] checkpoint preflight failed:", cpErr);
  1167	      res.status(503).json(err("EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_PREFLIGHT_UNAVAILABLE"));
  1168	      return;
  1169	    }
  1170	
  1171	    // The semantic fallback budget is a per-resume window, not an excuse to
  1172	    // erase previous failures. Archive the old window and bound explicit resumes.
  1173	    const existingWindows = Array.isArray(originalPayload.founder_resume_windows)
  1174	      ? originalPayload.founder_resume_windows : [];
  1175	    if (message.objective_contract?.recovery_attempts && existingWindows.length >= 3 && !originalPayload.founder_reset_fenced) {
  1176	      res.status(409).json(err("FOUNDER_RESUME_WINDOW_LIMIT_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED"));
  1177	      return;
  1178	    }
  1179	
  1180	    backgroundCommsStore.preserveTerminalReceiptForResume(message);
  1181	    message.status = "QUEUED";
  1182	    message.attempt_count = 0;
  1183	    delete message.completed_at;
  1184	    delete message.receipt_id;
  1185	    delete message.reply_payload;
  1186	    delete message.next_attempt_at;
  1187	    message.last_error = undefined;
  1188	    message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  1189	    if (message.objective_contract) {
  1190	      const normalizedCriteria = Array.isArray(message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria)
  1191	        ? message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria.map((c: any) => ({
  1192	            ...c,
  1193	            status: c.status === "UNVERIFIED" ? ("PENDING" as const) : c.status
  1194	          }))
  1195	        : [];
  1196	      if (message.objective_contract.recovery_attempts > 0) {
  1197	        const oldAttempts = message.objective_contract.recovery_attempts;
  1198	        originalPayload.founder_resume_windows = [...existingWindows, {
  1199	          at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  1200	          previous_recovery_attempts: oldAttempts,
  1201	          window_attempts: 0,
  1202	          previous_stage: message.objective_contract.current_stage
  1203	        }].slice(-3);
  1204	      }
  1205	      // CommsStore monotonically preserves lifetime recovery_attempts by
  1206	      // design. Keep that history; the inbox worker uses window_attempts for
  1207	      // the separate bounded Founder-authorised continuation window.
  1208	      message.objective_contract = {
  1209	        ...message.objective_contract,
  1210	        acceptance_criteria: normalizedCriteria,
  1211	        current_stage: "ACTIVE_EXECUTION",
  1212	        next_action_pointer: "CONTINUE_UNVERIFIED_CRITERIA_AFTER_CHECKPOINT_RECONCILIATION",
  1213	        updated_at: message.last_heartbeat_at
  1214	      };
  1215	    }
  1216	    message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  1217	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  1218	      at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  1219	      phase: "resume",
  1220	      status: "running" as const,
  1221	      label: "Founder resumed reconciled same task"
  1222	    }].slice(-80);
  1223	    message.payload = { ...originalPayload, resume_same_objective: true };
  1224	    const resumeConvId = String(originalPayload.conversation_id || "");
  1225	    if (resumeConvId.startsWith("ag-") || !message.recipient) {
  1226	      message.recipient = resumeConvId.startsWith("ag-") ? "ag" : "geminx";
  1227	    }
  1228	    backgroundCommsStore.saveMessage(message);
  1229	
  1230	    res.status(202).json(ok({
  1231	      background_job_id: message.message_id,
  1232	      status: "QUEUED",
  1233	      message: "Same GeminX task resumed."
  1234	    }));
  1235	  });
  1236	
  1237	  router.post("/background/:id/followup", (req, res) => {
  1238	    const message = backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(req.params.id);
  1239	    if (!message || message.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") {
  1240	      res.status(404).json(err("background founder job not found"));
  1241	      return;
  1242	    }
  1243	    if (!["QUEUED", "RUNNING", "ACKNOWLEDGED", "WAITING_HUMAN", "BLOCKED", "FAILED"].includes(message.status)) {
  1244	      res.status(409).json(err("background founder job cannot accept follow-up in its current state"));
  1245	      return;
  1246	    }
  1247	
  1248	    const text = readOptionalString(req.body?.text);
  1249	    if (!text) {
  1250	      res.status(400).json(err("follow-up text is required"));
  1251	      return;
  1252	    }
  1253	
  1254	    const wasTerminalBoundary = message.status === "BLOCKED" || message.status === "FAILED";
  1255	    const wasResumableBoundary = wasTerminalBoundary || message.status === "WAITING_HUMAN";
  1256	    if (wasResumableBoundary) {
  1257	      backgroundCommsStore.preserveTerminalReceiptForResume(message);
  1258	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  1259	      delete message.completed_at;
  1260	      delete message.receipt_id;
  1261	      delete message.reply_payload;
  1262	      delete message.next_attempt_at;
  1263	      message.last_error = undefined;
  1264	    }
  1265	
  1266	    const followup = {
  1267	      followup_id: randomUUID(),
  1268	      text: text.slice(0, 12000),
  1269	      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1270	      status: "PENDING" as const
  1271	    };
  1272	    message.followups = [...(Array.isArray(message.followups) ? message.followups : []), followup].slice(-30);
  1273	    message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  1274	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  1275	      at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1276	      phase: "followup",
  1277	      status: "running" as const,
  1278	      label: wasResumableBoundary ? "Founder supplied the missing input · resuming the same task" : "Your follow-up is queued"
  1279	    }].slice(-80);
  1280	    backgroundCommsStore.saveMessage(message);
  1281	
  1282	    const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  1283	      ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  1284	      : {};
  1285	    const conversationId = readOptionalString(payload.conversation_id);
  1286	    if (conversationId) {
  1287	      const record = conversationStore.get(conversationId);
  1288	      if (record) {
  1289	        conversationStore.appendTurn(record, {
  1290	          role: "user",
  1291	          content: text.slice(0, 12000),
  1292	          at: new Date().toISOString()
  1293	        }, {
  1294	          active_run_id: message.message_id,
  1295	          domain: record.domain || "maintenance",
  1296	          summary: record.summary
  1297	        });
  1298	      }
  1299	    }
  1300	
  1301	    res.status(202).json(ok({
  1302	      background_job_id: message.message_id,
  1303	      followup_id: followup.followup_id,
  1304	      status: "PENDING",
  1305	      message: "Added to the current GeminX task."
  1306	    }));
  1307	  });
  1308	
  1309	  router.get("/conversations", (req, res, next) => {
  1310	    try {
  1311	      const rawLimit = Number(req.query.limit ?? 20);
  1312	      const limit = Number.isFinite(rawLimit) ? Math.max(1, Math.min(200, Math.floor(rawLimit))) : 20;
  1313	      const activeStatuses = new Set(["QUEUED", "DELIVERED", "ACKNOWLEDGED", "RUNNING",
  1314	        "AWAITING_EXTERNAL_EVENT", "SUSPENDED_INTERNAL", "WAITING_HUMAN"]);
  1315	      const resumableStatuses = new Set(["BLOCKED", "FAILED", "WAITING_HUMAN"]);
  1316	      const byConversation = new Map<string, GeminxMessage[]>();
  1317	      for (const job of backgroundCommsStore.listMessages()) {
  1318	        if (job.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") continue;
  1319	        const payload = job.payload && typeof job.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(job.payload)
  1320	          ? job.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  1321	        const id = readOptionalString(payload.conversation_id);
  1322	        if (!id) continue;
  1323	        const found = byConversation.get(id) ?? [];
  1324	        found.push(job);
  1325	        byConversation.set(id, found);
  1326	      }
  1327	      const allRecords = conversationStore.list();
  1328	      const topRecords = allRecords.slice(0, limit);
  1329	      const includedIds = new Set(topRecords.map(r => r.conversation_id));
  1330	      for (const record of allRecords.slice(limit)) {
  1331	        const jobs = byConversation.get(record.conversation_id);
  1332	        if (jobs && jobs.some(j => activeStatuses.has(j.status) || resumableStatuses.has(j.status))) {
  1333	          topRecords.push(record);
  1334	          includedIds.add(record.conversation_id);
  1335	        }
  1336	      }
  1337	      const items = topRecords.map((record) => {
  1338	        const related = (byConversation.get(record.conversation_id) ?? [])
  1339	          .sort((left, right) => right.created_at.localeCompare(left.created_at));
  1340	        const job = related.find(item => activeStatuses.has(item.status)) ??
  1341	          related.find(item => resumableStatuses.has(item.status)) ??
  1342	          related[0] ?? null;
  1343	        const payload = job?.payload && typeof job.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(job.payload)
  1344	          ? job.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  1345	        const resolvedActiveRunId = record.active_run_id ||
  1346	          (job && (activeStatuses.has(job.status) || resumableStatuses.has(job.status)) ? job.message_id : null);
  1347	        return {
  1348	          conversation_id: record.conversation_id,
  1349	          active_run_id: resolvedActiveRunId,
  1350	          active_task_id: record.active_task_id,
  1351	          domain: record.domain,
  1352	          summary: record.summary,
  1353	          model_preference: record.model_preference ?? null,
  1354	          title: deriveConversationTitle(record),
  1355	          custom_title: record.title ?? null,
  1356	          is_pinned: record.is_pinned === true,
  1357	          pin_updated_at: record.pin_updated_at ?? null,
  1358	          metadata_updated_at: record.metadata_updated_at ?? null,
  1359	          last_activity_at: record.last_activity_at ?? record.updated_at,
  1360	          project_id: record.project_id ?? null,
  1361	          project_name: record.project_name ?? null,
  1362	          active_job: job ? {
  1363	            job_id: job.message_id,
  1364	            background_job_id: job.message_id,
  1365	            conversation_id: record.conversation_id,
  1366	            status: job.status,
  1367	            is_active: activeStatuses.has(job.status),
  1368	            created_at: job.created_at,
  1369	            completed_at: job.completed_at ?? null,
  1370	            last_heartbeat_at: job.last_heartbeat_at ?? null,
  1371	            summary: (readOptionalString(payload.objective) ?? "").slice(0, 140) || null,
  1372	            receipt_id: job.receipt_id ?? null
  1373	          } : null,
  1374	          turn_count: record.turns.length,
  1375	          created_at: record.created_at,
  1376	          updated_at: record.updated_at
  1377	        };
  1378	      });
  1379	      res.json(ok({ items, total: items.length }));
  1380	    } catch (error) { next(error); }
  1381	  });

  1450	  router.patch("/conversations/:id", (req, res, next) => {
  1451	    try {
  1452	      const body = req.body && typeof req.body === "object" && !Array.isArray(req.body)
  1453	        ? req.body as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  1454	      const has = (key: string) => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, key);
  1455	      if (!has("title") && !has("is_pinned") && !has("model_preference") && !has("project_id") && !has("project_name")) {
  1456	        res.status(400).json(err("NO_VALID_PATCH_FIELDS")); return;
  1457	      }
  1458	      if (has("only_if_unset") && typeof body.only_if_unset !== "boolean") {
  1459	        res.status(400).json(err("INVALID_ONLY_IF_UNSET")); return;
  1460	      }
  1461	      if (has("is_pinned") && typeof body.is_pinned !== "boolean") {
  1462	        res.status(400).json(err("INVALID_PIN_STATE")); return;
  1463	      }
  1464	      const modelPreference = has("model_preference")
  1465	        ? readFounderModelPreference(body.model_preference, "deepseek") : undefined;
  1466	      let projectId = has("project_id")
  1467	        ? (body.project_id === null || body.project_id === "" ? null : String(body.project_id))
  1468	        : undefined;
  1469	      let projectName = has("project_name")
  1470	        ? (body.project_name === null ? null : String(body.project_name))
  1471	        : undefined;
  1472	      if (projectId && !projectName) {
  1473	        const found = nativeNeoProjectStore.get(projectId);
  1474	        if (found) projectName = found.name;
  1475	      }
  1476	      const updated = conversationStore.updateMetadata(req.params.id, {
  1477	        ...(has("title") ? { title: body.title as string | null } : {}),
  1478	        ...(has("is_pinned") ? { is_pinned: body.is_pinned as boolean } : {}),
  1479	        ...(has("only_if_unset") ? { only_if_unset: body.only_if_unset as boolean } : {}),
  1480	        ...(modelPreference !== undefined ? { model_preference: modelPreference } : {}),
  1481	        ...(projectId !== undefined ? { project_id: projectId } : {}),
  1482	        ...(projectName !== undefined ? { project_name: projectName } : {})
  1483	      });
  1484	      if (!updated) { res.status(404).json(err("conversation not found")); return; }
  1485	      res.json(ok({
  1486	        ...updated,
  1487	        title: deriveConversationTitle(updated),
  1488	        custom_title: updated.title ?? null,
  1489	        is_pinned: updated.is_pinned === true,
  1490	        pin_updated_at: updated.pin_updated_at ?? null,
  1491	        project_id: updated.project_id ?? null,
  1492	        project_name: updated.project_name ?? null,
  1493	        last_activity_at: updated.last_activity_at ?? updated.updated_at
  1494	      }));
  1495	    } catch (error) {
  1496	      res.status(400).json(err(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)));
  1497	    }
  1498	  });
  1499	
  1500	  router.get("/conversations/:id", (req, res, next) => {
  1501	    try {
  1502	      const record = conversationStore.get(req.params.id);
  1503	      if (!record) {
  1504	        res.status(404).json(err("conversation not found"));
  1505	        return;
  1506	      }
  1507	      let resolvedActiveRunId = record.active_run_id;
  1508	      const currentRunJob = resolvedActiveRunId ? backgroundCommsStore.getMessage(resolvedActiveRunId) : null;
  1509	      const liveStatuses = new Set([
  1510	        "QUEUED", "DELIVERED", "ACKNOWLEDGED", "RUNNING",
  1511	        "AWAITING_EXTERNAL_EVENT", "SUSPENDED_INTERNAL"
  1512	      ]);
  1513	      const resumableStatuses = new Set([
  1514	        "WAITING_HUMAN", "BLOCKED", "FAILED"
  1515	      ]);
  1516	      if (!currentRunJob || (!liveStatuses.has(currentRunJob.status) && !resumableStatuses.has(currentRunJob.status))) {
  1517	        const relatedJobs = backgroundCommsStore.listMessages()
  1518	          .filter(j => {
  1519	            if (j.type !== "founder_autonomous_job") return false;
  1520	            const p = j.payload && typeof j.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(j.payload)
  1521	              ? j.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  1522	            return readOptionalString(p.conversation_id) === record.conversation_id;
  1523	          })
  1524	          .sort((a, b) => b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at));
  1525	        const liveJob = relatedJobs.find(j => liveStatuses.has(j.status));
  1526	        const resumableJob = relatedJobs.find(j => resumableStatuses.has(j.status));
  1527	        const chosen = liveJob ?? resumableJob ?? null;
  1528	        if (chosen) {
  1529	          resolvedActiveRunId = chosen.message_id;
  1530	        }
  1531	      }
  1532	      res.json(ok({
  1533	        ...record,
  1534	        active_run_id: resolvedActiveRunId
  1535	      }));
  1536	    } catch (error) {
  1537	      next(error);
  1538	    }
  1539	  });
