   910	function retryFingerprintToolCalls(trace: ReturnType<ToolRegistry["trace"]>): Array<{
   911	  tool: string;
   912	  status: string;
   913	  error?: string | null;
   914	  args?: Record<string, unknown>;
   915	}> {
   916	  return trace.map((entry) => ({
   917	    tool: entry.tool,
   918	    status: entry.status,
   919	    error: entry.error,
   920	    args: entry.args,
   921	  }));
   922	}
   923	
   924	export function buildDisprovenApproachRecord(
   925	  input: {
   926	    code: string;
   927	    failure_class?: "TRANSIENT" | "LOGIC" | "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY" | "CAPABILITY" | "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME" | "UNKNOWN";
   928	    tool_calls?: Array<{ tool: string; status: string; error?: string | null; args?: Record<string, unknown> }>;
   929	  },
   930	  now = new Date()
   931	): DisprovenApproachRecord {
   932	  const failureClass = input.failure_class ?? "UNKNOWN";
   933	  const relevantCalls = (input.tool_calls ?? []).filter((call) => call.status !== "succeeded");
   934	  const calls = relevantCalls.length > 0 ? relevantCalls : (input.tool_calls ?? []);
   935	  const approachFamilies = [...new Set(calls.map((call) => approachFamily(call.tool)))].slice(0, 12);
   936	  const targetResources = [...new Set(calls.map((call) => normalizedFailureTarget(call.tool, call.args)))].slice(0, 12);
   937	  const errorShape = calls
   938	    .map((call) => normalizeFailureFingerprintText(call.error))
   939	    .filter(Boolean)
   940	    .slice(0, 8);
   941	
   942	  const fingerprint = createHash("sha256")
   943	    .update(JSON.stringify({
   944	      code: input.code,
   945	      failure_class: failureClass,
   946	      approach_families: approachFamilies,
   947	      target_resources: targetResources,
   948	      error_shape: errorShape,
   949	    }))
   950	    .digest("hex")
   951	    .slice(0, 24);
   952	
   953	  const observedAt = now.toISOString();
   954	  const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000).toISOString();
   955	  return {
   956	    fingerprint,
   957	    failure_class: failureClass,
   958	    code: input.code,
   959	    approach_families: approachFamilies,
   960	    first_observed_at: observedAt,
   961	    last_observed_at: observedAt,
   962	    occurrences: 1,
   963	    expires_at: expiresAt,
   964	    expires_when: "Material target/runtime/code identity changes, or the failure fingerprint no longer reproduces.",
   965	  };
   966	}
   967	
   968	function mergeDisprovenApproach(
   969	  existing: unknown,
   970	  next: DisprovenApproachRecord,
   971	  now = Date.now()
   972	): { records: DisprovenApproachRecord[]; repeated: boolean } {
   973	  const records = Array.isArray(existing)
   974	    ? existing.filter((item): item is DisprovenApproachRecord => {
   975	        if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || Array.isArray(item)) return false;
   976	        const record = item as Partial<DisprovenApproachRecord>;
   977	        const expiry = Date.parse(String(record.expires_at ?? ""));
   978	        return typeof record.fingerprint === "string" && (!Number.isFinite(expiry) || expiry > now);
   979	      })
   980	    : [];
   981	
   982	  const previous = records.find((item) => item.fingerprint === next.fingerprint);
   983	  const merged = previous
   984	    ? {
   985	        ...next,
   986	        first_observed_at: previous.first_observed_at,
   987	        occurrences: Math.max(1, Number(previous.occurrences ?? 1)) + 1,
   988	      }
   989	    : next;
   990	
   991	  return {
   992	    records: [...records.filter((item) => item.fingerprint !== next.fingerprint), merged].slice(-16),
   993	    repeated: merged.occurrences > 1,
   994	  };
   995	}
   996	
   997	function heartbeatJob(store: CommsStore, messageId: string): void {
   998	  const latest = store.getMessage(messageId);
   999	  if (!latest || latest.status !== "RUNNING") return;
  1000	  latest.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  1001	  store.saveMessage(latest);
  1002	}

  1004	function taskPackageFromPayload(payload: unknown): GeminxTaskPackage | null {
  1005	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  1006	  const record = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  1007	  const candidate = record.task_package ?? (record.command === "RUN_TASK_PACKAGE" ? record.task : null);
  1008	  if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object" || Array.isArray(candidate)) return null;
  1009	  return candidate as GeminxTaskPackage;
  1010	}

  1013

  1015	  command: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1";
  1016	  authority: string;
  1017	  objective: string;
  1018	  acceptance_criteria: string[];
  1019	  constraints: string[];

  1037	const GENERIC_AUTONOMOUS_ROUTING_BOILERPLATE = [
  1038	  /^Inspect current truth before changing anything\.$/i,
  1039	  /^Fix the smallest durable root cause for each mechanically repairable blocker\.$/i,
  1040	  /^If the task exposes another mechanically repairable GeminX-owned fault while you are working,/i,
  1041	  /^Rerun the same failed probe and relevant regressions after each repair\.$/i,
  1042	  /^Continue until the objective is mechanically proven or a real credential,/i,
  1043	  /^Return durable receipts and never invent completion\.$/i,
  1044	  /^Reuse canonical systems and the existing repair lineage;/i,
  1045	  /^Do not expose secrets or credentials\.$/i,
  1046	  /^Do not weaken protected-action or founder-approval boundaries;/i,
  1047	  /^Do not turn a repairable code\/runtime\/configuration fault into a human blocker/i,
  1048	  /^Preserve exact run, deployment and repository identity when repairing existing work\.$/i,
  1049	];
  1050	
  1051	function taskRoutingSupplement(lines: readonly string[]): string {
  1052	  return lines
  1053	    .map((line) => String(line ?? "").trim())
  1054	    .filter(Boolean)
  1055	    .filter((line) => !GENERIC_AUTONOMOUS_ROUTING_BOILERPLATE.some((pattern) => pattern.test(line)))
  1056	    .join("\n");
  1057	}
  1058	
  1059	export function inferAutonomousTaskType(command: {
  1060	  objective: string;
  1061	  acceptance_criteria: string[];
  1062	  constraints: string[];
  1063	}) {
  1064	  // The Founder objective owns task classification. Generic autonomous safety
  1065	  // boilerplate contains words such as "create", "repair" and "deployment";
  1066	  // letting those words classify every job turned simple Notepad/device actions
  1067	  // into engineering builds and polluted their tool surface with GitHub/Railway.
  1068	  const objectiveType = inferDeepSeekTaskType(command.objective);
  1069	  if (objectiveType !== "chat") return objectiveType;
  1070	
  1071	  // Custom, task-specific acceptance criteria may still promote an otherwise
  1072	  // terse objective, but the stock governance contract must never do so.
  1073	  const supplemental = taskRoutingSupplement([
  1074	    ...command.acceptance_criteria,
  1075	    ...command.constraints,
  1076	  ]);
  1077	  return supplemental ? inferDeepSeekTaskType(supplemental) : objectiveType;
  1078	}
  1079	
  1080	function autonomousTaskCommand(payload: unknown): GeminxAutonomousTaskCommand | null {
  1081	  if (!payload) return null;
  1082	  let value: Record<string, unknown>;
  1083	  if (typeof payload === "string") {
  1084	    const trimmed = payload.trim();
  1085	    if (!trimmed) return null;
  1086	    value = {
  1087	      command: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1",
  1088	      authority: GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_AUTHORITY,
  1089	      objective: trimmed,
  1090	    };
  1091	  } else if (typeof payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload)) {
  1092	    value = { ...(payload as Record<string, unknown>) };
  1093	    const rawObjective = String(
  1094	      value.objective ??
  1095	      value.intent ??
  1096	      value.task ??
  1097	      value.prompt ??
  1098	      value.message ??
  1099	      value.content ??
  1100	      value.instruction ??
  1101	      ""
  1102	    ).trim();
  1103	    if (!rawObjective) return null;
  1104	    if (String(value.command ?? "") !== "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1") {
  1105	      value.command = "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1";
  1106	    }
  1107	    if (!value.authority) {
  1108	      value.authority = GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_AUTHORITY;
  1109	    }
  1110	    value.objective = rawObjective;
  1111	  } else {
  1112	    return null;
  1113	  }
  1114	
  1115	  const strings = (input: unknown) =>
  1116	    Array.isArray(input)
  1117	      ? input.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.trim().length > 0).map(item => item.trim())
  1118	      : [];
  1119	
  1120	  const verifiedCurrentState =
  1121	    value.verified_current_state && typeof value.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(value.verified_current_state)
  1122	      ? value.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>
  1123	      : {};
  1124	  // Legacy durable jobs did not persist the selected Office identity and were
  1125	  // executed as Neo. Preserve that replay behavior, while new jobs bind the
  1126	  // authenticated HQ selection explicitly through this envelope.
  1127	  const officeAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(value.office_agent, "neo");
  1128	
  1129	  return {
  1130	    command: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1",
  1131	    authority: String(value.authority ?? GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_AUTHORITY),
  1132	    objective: String(value.objective),
  1133	    acceptance_criteria: strings(value.acceptance_criteria),
  1134	    constraints: strings(value.constraints),
  1135	    verified_current_state: verifiedCurrentState,
  1136	    model_preference: officeAgent === "brian" ? "antigravity" : (String(value.model_preference ?? (officeAgent === "ag" ? "antigravity" : "deepseek")).trim().toLowerCase() || "deepseek"),
  1137	    allow_free_model: value.allow_free_model === true,
  1138	    data_classification: ["public", "internal", "confidential", "secret"].includes(String(value.data_classification ?? "").toLowerCase())
  1139	      ? String(value.data_classification).toLowerCase() as GeminxDataClassification
  1140	      : "internal",
  1141	    requires_tools: value.requires_tools !== false,
  1142	    read_only: value.read_only === true,
  1143	    allowed_tools: strings(value.allowed_tools).slice(0, 64),
  1144	    credential_scope: strings(value.credential_scope).slice(0, 64),
  1145	    attachments: parseFounderAttachments(value.attachments),
  1146	    conversation_id: typeof value.conversation_id === "string" && value.conversation_id.trim()
  1147	      ? value.conversation_id.trim()
  1148	      : null,
  1149	    office_agent: officeAgent,
  1150	    company_scope: normalizeCompanyScope(value.company_scope, officeAgent),
  1151	    cancel_message_ids: strings(value.cancel_message_ids).slice(0, 100),
  1152	    depends_on_message_ids: strings(value.depends_on_message_ids).slice(0, 100),
  1153	    evidence_refs: strings(value.evidence_refs).slice(0, 100),
  1154	  };
  1155	}
  1156	
  1157	type NewtonConnectorResultCommand = {
  1158	  command: "CHATGPT_CONNECTOR_RESULT_V1";
  1159	  connector_message_id: string;
  1160	  original_message_id: string | null;
  1161	  original_correlation_id: string;
  1162	  terminal_status: string;
  1163	  receipt_path: string;
  1164	  summary: string;
  1165	  proof: unknown[];
  1166	  founder_action_required: boolean;
  1167	  resume_cancelled_objective: boolean;
  1168	};
  1169	
  1170	function newtonConnectorResultCommand(payload: unknown): NewtonConnectorResultCommand | null {
  1171	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  1172	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  1173	  if (String(value.command ?? "") !== "CHATGPT_CONNECTOR_RESULT_V1") return null;
  1174	  const connectorMessageId = String(value.connector_message_id ?? "").trim();
  1175	  const originalCorrelationId = String(value.original_correlation_id ?? "").trim();
  1176	  if (!/^geminx-newton-connector-[a-f0-9]{16}$/.test(connectorMessageId) || !originalCorrelationId) return null;
  1177	  return {
  1178	    command: "CHATGPT_CONNECTOR_RESULT_V1",
  1179	    connector_message_id: connectorMessageId,
  1180	    original_message_id: typeof value.original_message_id === "string" && value.original_message_id.trim()
  1181	      ? value.original_message_id.trim()
  1182	      : null,
  1183	    original_correlation_id: originalCorrelationId,
  1184	    terminal_status: String(value.terminal_status ?? "UNKNOWN").trim().toUpperCase().slice(0, 40),
  1185	    receipt_path: String(value.receipt_path ?? "").trim().slice(0, 500),
  1186	    summary: String(value.summary ?? "").trim().slice(0, 4_000),
  1187	    proof: Array.isArray(value.proof) ? value.proof.slice(0, 40) : [],
  1188	    founder_action_required: value.founder_action_required === true,
  1189	    resume_cancelled_objective: value.resume_cancelled_objective === true,
  1190	  };
  1191	}
  1192	
  1193	function connectorAuthorisesCancelledObjectiveResume(command: NewtonConnectorResultCommand): boolean {
  1194	  if (!command.resume_cancelled_objective || !command.original_message_id || command.terminal_status !== "COMPLETED") {
  1195	    return false;
  1196	  }
  1197	  return command.proof.some(item => {
  1198	    if (!item || typeof item !== "object" || Array.isArray(item)) return false;
  1199	    const proof = item as Record<string, unknown>;
  1200	    return String(proof.type ?? "").trim() === "material_state_change" &&
  1201	      String(proof.from_status ?? "").trim().toUpperCase() === "CANCELLED" &&
  1202	      String(proof.reason ?? "").trim().length > 0;
  1203	  });
  1204	}
  1205	
  1206	function applyNewtonConnectorResult(
  1207	  message: GeminxMessage,
  1208	  command: NewtonConnectorResultCommand,
  1209	  commsStore: CommsStore
  1210	): GeminxMessage {
  1211	  const complete = (status: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED", reply: Record<string, unknown>) => {
  1212	    message.status = status;
  1213	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  1214	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  1215	    message.reply_payload = {
  1216	      status,
  1217	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  1218	      route: "newton-chatgpt-connector-result",
  1219	      ...reply,
  1220	    };
  1221	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  1222	    return message;
  1223	  };
  1224	
  1225	  if (message.sender.toLowerCase() !== "newton") {
  1226	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  1227	      code: "NEWTON_CONNECTOR_RESULT_SENDER_FORBIDDEN",
  1228	      output: "Only Newton may return a ChatGPT connector result into the GeminX continuation path.",
  1229	    });
  1230	  }
  1231	
  1232	  const candidates = commsStore.listMessages()
  1233	    .filter(candidate =>
  1234	      candidate.message_id !== message.message_id &&
  1235	      isPrimaryAgentRecipient(candidate.recipient) &&
  1236	      candidate.correlation_id === command.original_correlation_id &&
  1237	      autonomousTaskCommand(candidate.payload) !== null
  1238	    )
  1239	    .sort((a, b) => b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at));
  1240	  const original = command.original_message_id
  1241	    ? candidates.find(candidate => candidate.message_id === command.original_message_id)
  1242	    : candidates[0];
  1243	
  1244	  if (!original) {
  1245	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  1246	      code: "NEWTON_CONNECTOR_ORIGINAL_OBJECTIVE_NOT_FOUND",
  1247	      connector_message_id: command.connector_message_id,
  1248	      correlation_id: command.original_correlation_id,
  1249	      output: "The connector receipt is validly shaped, but no matching canonical GeminX autonomous objective exists to resume.",
  1250	    });
  1251	  }
  1252	
  1253	  if (original.status === "CANCELLED" && !connectorAuthorisesCancelledObjectiveResume(command)) {
  1254	    return complete("COMPLETED", {
  1255	      code: "NEWTON_CONNECTOR_ORIGINAL_OBJECTIVE_CANCELLED",
  1256	      connector_message_id: command.connector_message_id,
  1257	      original_message_id: original.message_id,
  1258	      resumed: false,
  1259	    });
  1260	  }
  1261	
  1262	  const originalPayload = original.payload && typeof original.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(original.payload)
  1263	    ? { ...(original.payload as Record<string, unknown>) }
  1264	    : {};
  1265	  const verifiedState = originalPayload.verified_current_state &&
  1266	    typeof originalPayload.verified_current_state === "object" &&
  1267	    !Array.isArray(originalPayload.verified_current_state)
  1268	      ? { ...(originalPayload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
  1269	      : {};
  1270	  const existingResults = Array.isArray(verifiedState.connector_results)
  1271	    ? verifiedState.connector_results.filter(item => item && typeof item === "object" && !Array.isArray(item)) as Record<string, unknown>[]
  1272	    : [];
  1273	
  1274	  if (existingResults.some(result => String(result.connector_message_id ?? "") === command.connector_message_id)) {
  1275	    return complete("COMPLETED", {
  1276	      code: "NEWTON_CONNECTOR_RESULT_ALREADY_APPLIED",
  1277	      connector_message_id: command.connector_message_id,
  1278	      original_message_id: original.message_id,
  1279	      resumed: false,
  1280	    });
  1281	  }
  1282	
  1283	  verifiedState.connector_results = [...existingResults, {
  1284	    connector_message_id: command.connector_message_id,
  1285	    terminal_status: command.terminal_status,
  1286	    receipt_path: command.receipt_path,
  1287	    summary: command.summary,
  1288	    proof: command.proof,
  1289	    founder_action_required: command.founder_action_required,
  1290	    resume_cancelled_objective: command.resume_cancelled_objective,
  1291	    returned_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1292	  }].slice(-12);
  1293	  originalPayload.verified_current_state = verifiedState;
  1294	  original.payload = originalPayload;
  1295	
  1296	  if (original.status !== "RUNNING" && original.status !== "QUEUED") {
  1297	    original.status = "QUEUED";
  1298	    // A terminal connector receipt is a material state change: the same objective
  1299	    // now has new evidence/capability available. Reset only this objective's bounded
  1300	    // attempt counter so the retry circuit grants a fresh execution window without
  1301	    // creating a duplicate job or allowing an unbounded retry loop.
  1302	    original.attempt_count = 0;
  1303	    delete original.completed_at;
  1304	    delete original.receipt_id;
  1305	    delete original.reply_payload;
  1306	    delete original.next_attempt_at;
  1307	    delete original.last_error;
  1308	  }
  1309	  original.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  1310	  original.activity = [...(Array.isArray(original.activity) ? original.activity : []), {
  1311	    event_id: randomUUID(),
  1312	    at: original.last_heartbeat_at,
  1313	    phase: "connector-return",
  1314	    status: "running" as const,
  1315	    label: "Newton connector result returned · resuming the same task",
  1316	  }].slice(-80);
  1317	  commsStore.saveMessage(original);
  1318	
  1319	  return complete("COMPLETED", {
  1320	    code: "NEWTON_CONNECTOR_RESULT_APPLIED",
  1321	    connector_message_id: command.connector_message_id,
  1322	    original_message_id: original.message_id,
  1323	    original_correlation_id: original.correlation_id,
  1324	    resumed: original.status === "QUEUED",
  1325	    founder_action_required: command.founder_action_required,
  1326	    terminal_status: command.terminal_status,
  1327	    receipt_path: command.receipt_path,
  1328	  });
  1329	}
  1330	
  1331	export function founderVisibleAutonomousMessage(text: string): string {
  1332	  return String(text ?? "")
  1333	    .replace(/^\s*GEMINX_CONTEXT_PRESSURE:[^\n]*(?:\n+)?/i, "")
  1334	    .replace(/\s*<geminx_learning>[\s\S]*?<\/geminx_learning>\s*/gi, "\n")
  1335	    .replace(/\s*<geminx_terminal>[\s\S]*?<\/geminx_terminal>\s*/gi, "\n")
  1336	    .replace(/<\/?(?:details|summary)>/gi, "")
  1337	    .replace(/^\s*(?:COMPLETED_PROVEN|BLOCKED_REAL_BOUNDARY|FAILED_VERIFICATION)\s*$/gim, "")
  1338	    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
  1339	    .trim();
  1340	}

  1342	const CONTEXT_PRESSURE_REPAIR_GENERATION = "context-pressure-v4";
  1343	const MAX_CONTEXT_PRESSURE_AUTO_RESUMES = 3;

  1345	export type GeminxAutonomousTerminalAdjudication = {
  1346	  status: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED" | "FAILED" | "RETRY";
  1347	  code:
  1348	    | "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN"
  1349	    | "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_REAL_BOUNDARY"
  1350	    | "NEO_IMAGE_PROVIDER_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED"
  1351	    | "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_NO_PROGRESS_CIRCUIT_OPEN"
  1352	    | "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_INTERNAL_CONTEXT_PRESSURE"
  1353	    | "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_VERIFICATION_REPAIR_REQUIRED"
  1354	    | "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_RECOVERABLE_TOOL_DEGRADATION"
  1355	    | "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TERMINAL_PROOF_MISSING"
  1356	    | "GEMINX_DURABLE_CRITERIA_PROOF_MISSING"
  1357	    | "GEMINX_SCRATCH_LIFECYCLE_PROOF_MISSING";
  1358	  output?: string;
  1359	  failure_class?: "TRANSIENT" | "LOGIC" | "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY" | "CAPABILITY" | "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME" | "UNKNOWN";
  1360	  degraded_tool_calls: Array<{ tool: string; status: string; error: string | null }>;
  1361	};

  1363	export type AutonomousTerminalToolCall = {

  1366	  error?: string | null;
  1367	  started_at?: string | null;
  1368	  finished_at?: string | null;

  1370	  args?: Record<string, unknown> | null;
