  3436	          (exitCode === 0 && /\b(?:vitest|test)\b/i.test(callArgs))
  3437	        );

  3485	      const ghToken = process.env.GEMINX_GITHUB_TOKEN || process.env.GITHUB_TOKEN || process.env.GEMINX_REPO_ACCESS_TOKEN;

  3500	          }

  3537	      office_agent: command.office_agent,
  3538	      objective: [command.objective, ...(command.acceptance_criteria ?? [])].join("\n"),
  3539	      tool_calls: (reply.tool_calls ?? []).map((call: any) => ({
  3540	        tool: call.tool,
  3541	        status: call.status,
  3542	        error: call.error ?? null,
  3543	        args: call.args,
  3544	        result: call.result ?? null,
  3545	      })),
  3546	    });
  3547	
  3548	    // Only the native tool registry's actual result objects may advance a
  3549	    // durable criterion. Model speech, returned text and naked success flags
  3550	    // cannot manufacture VERIFIED. Persist before terminal adjudication.
  3551	    message = refreshMessage();
  3552	    if (message.objective_contract) {
  3553	      const promotion = promoteDurableCriteriaFromToolReceipts(
  3554	        message.objective_contract,
  3555	        (reply.tool_calls ?? []).map((call: any) => ({
  3556	          tool: call.tool,
  3557	          status: call.status,
  3558	          args: call.args,
  3559	          result: call.result ?? null
  3560	        }))
  3561	      );
  3562	      if (promotion.newly_verified.length) {
  3563	        message.objective_contract = promotion.contract;
  3564	        message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  3565	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  3566	          at: new Date().toISOString(),
  3567	          phase: "criterion-verification",
  3568	          status: "done" as const,
  3569	          label: "Evidence-backed criterion receipts: " + promotion.newly_verified.join(",")
  3570	        }].slice(-80);
  3571	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3572	      }
  3573	      const isOfficeSpecialist = ["sophie", "brian", "mark", "kerry", "ads"].includes(String(command.office_agent ?? ""));
  3574	      if (!isOfficeSpecialist && !command.read_only && adjudication.status === "COMPLETED" && promotion.remaining.length) {
  3575	        adjudication = {
  3576	          status: "RETRY",
  3577	          code: "GEMINX_DURABLE_CRITERIA_PROOF_MISSING",
  3578	          failure_class: "LOGIC",
  3579	          output: "Acceptance criteria lack independent typed proof: " + promotion.remaining.join(",") +
  3580	            ". Preserve the same objective and collect criterion_id-linked tool readbacks before closing.",
  3581	          degraded_tool_calls: adjudication.degraded_tool_calls
  3582	        };
  3583	      } else if (
  3584	        isOfficeSpecialist &&
  3585	        adjudication.status === "COMPLETED" &&
  3586	        message.objective_contract &&
  3587	        message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria.length > 0
  3588	      ) {
  3589	        // Specialist office agents complete on plain language report; mark criteria verified so the contract closes cleanly
  3590	        message.objective_contract = {
  3591	          ...message.objective_contract,
  3592	          acceptance_criteria: message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria.map((c) => ({
  3593	            ...c,
  3594	            status: "VERIFIED",
  3595	            evidence_receipt: c.evidence_receipt || `specialist_verified:${command.office_agent}:turn_completed`
  3596	          }))
  3597	        };
  3598	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3599	      } else if (
  3600	        message.objective_contract.acceptance_criteria.length > 0 &&
  3601	        promotion.remaining.length === 0 &&
  3602	        adjudication.status !== "BLOCKED" &&
  3603	        (!adjudication.degraded_tool_calls || adjudication.degraded_tool_calls.length === 0)
  3604	      ) {
  3605	        adjudication = {
  3606	          status: "COMPLETED",
  3607	          code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  3608	          degraded_tool_calls: adjudication.degraded_tool_calls ?? []
  3609	        };
  3610	      }
  3611	    }
  3612	
  3613	    if (adjudication.code === "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_INTERNAL_CONTEXT_PRESSURE") {
  3614	      // The old route sent a second paid run to QUEUED with an incomplete prose
  3615	      // checkpoint. Fail closed until a replay-safe native checkpoint exists.
  3616	      const trace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  3617	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  3618	        code: "GEMINX_INTERNAL_CONTEXT_PRESSURE_CHECKPOINT_REQUIRED",
  3619	        founder_action_required: false,
  3620	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  3621	        output: "The context window closed before verification. No paid execution was requeued.",
  3622	        tool_trace: compactRetryToolTrace(trace)
  3623	      });
  3624	    }
  3625	
  3626	    if (adjudication.status !== "COMPLETED" && adjudication.status !== "BLOCKED") {
  3627	      const availabilityRaw = executorAvailabilityFromEnv();
  3628	      const availability = astraUnavailableForObjective(command)
  3629	        ? { ...availabilityRaw, openai: false }
  3630	        : availabilityRaw;
  3631	      const trace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  3632	      const agTool =
  3633	        !creatorFrontierOnly && availability.ag
  3634	          ? selectAgToolFailover(trace, lineage)
  3635	          : null;
  3636	
  3637	      // AG is only used where its cloud worker has a mechanically proven executor
  3638	      // for the exact failed tool family. Arbitrary objectives are never "delegated"
  3639	      // to AG merely to make the graph look autonomous.
  3640	      if (agTool) {
  3641	        const child = buildAgDelegatedToolMessage(
  3642	          message,
  3643	          agTool,
  3644	          command.objective,
  3645	          command.acceptance_criteria
  3646	        );
  3647	        liveStore.saveMessage(child);
  3648	        lineage = recordDelegationDispatch(
  3649	          lineage,
  3650	          "ag",
  3651	          `failed_tool:${agTool.tool}:${adjudication.code}`,
  3652	          child.message_id,
  3653	          agTool.tool
  3654	        );
  3655	        lineage.last_failure_code = adjudication.code;
  3656	        message = refreshMessage();
  3657	        message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  3658	        message.status = "QUEUED";
  3659	        delete message.completed_at;
  3660	        delete message.receipt_id;
  3661	        delete message.reply_payload;
  3662	        message.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 60_000).toISOString();
  3663	        message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  3664	        message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  3665	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  3666	          at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  3667	          phase: "delegate",
  3668	          status: "running" as const,
  3669	          label: `GeminX rerouted failed ${agTool.tool} execution to AG · same task/evidence lineage`,
  3670	          tool: agTool.tool,
  3671	        }].slice(-80);
  3672	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3673	        return message;
  3674	      }
  3675	
  3676	
  3677	    }
  3678	
  3679	    if (adjudication.status === "RETRY") {
  3680	      const trace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  3681	      const mutationObjective = objectiveRequiresMutationEvidence(command.objective, command.read_only);
  3682	      const scratchObjective = mutationObjective && /\b(?:scratch|temporary file|temp file)\b/i.test(command.objective);
  3683	      const mutationProgress = scratchObjective
  3684	        ? trace.some((entry) => entry.status === "succeeded" && /^(?:geminx_scratch_write|geminx_scratch_delete)$/.test(entry.tool))
  3685	        : hasMaterialMutationProgress(trace);
  3686	      const failedMutation = hasFailedMutationAttempt(trace);
  3687	      // Read-only qualification/recovery work is still real progress when it
  3688	      // discovers a genuinely new verified target. Do not gate that progress
  3689	      // on one adjudication code or on every unrelated tool being healthy.
  3690	      // A failed mutation remains sticky so a "read sandwich" cannot hide a
  3691	      // broken write and mint an unlimited retry window.
  3692	      const observationProgress = !mutationObjective && !mutationProgress && !failedMutation;
  3693	      if (mutationProgress || observationProgress) {
  3694	        // Always refresh BEFORE mutating the durable retry state. Refreshing
  3695	        // after recordRetryCircuitObservationProgress would discard its
  3696	        // in-memory update and make novel verified reads look stagnant again.
  3697	        message = refreshMessage();
  3698	        const progressed = mutationProgress
  3699	          ? (recordRetryCircuitMaterialProgress(message), true)
  3700	          : recordRetryCircuitObservationProgress(message, trace);
  3701	        if (progressed) {
  3702	          liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3703	          if (message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)) {
  3704	            const progressedVerified = (message.payload as Record<string, unknown>).verified_current_state;
  3705	            if (progressedVerified && typeof progressedVerified === "object" && !Array.isArray(progressedVerified)) {
  3706	              command.verified_current_state = { ...(progressedVerified as Record<string, unknown>) };
  3707	            }
  3708	          }
  3709	        }
  3710	      }
  3711	      const verified = command.verified_current_state && typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  3712	        ? { ...command.verified_current_state }
  3713	        : {};
  3714	      const repairs = Array.isArray(verified.self_repair_receipts)
  3715	        ? verified.self_repair_receipts.filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object")
  3716	        : [];
  3717	      verified.self_repair_receipts = [...repairs, {
  3718	        attempt: Number(message.attempt_count ?? 1),
  3719	        provider: reply.provider,
  3720	        model: reply.model,
  3721	        code: adjudication.code,
  3722	        failure_class: adjudication.failure_class ?? "UNKNOWN",
  3723	        degraded_tool_calls: adjudication.degraded_tool_calls,
  3724	        tool_trace: compactRetryToolTrace(trace),
  3725	        last_attempt_summary: visibleMessage.slice(0, 4_000),
  3726	        observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  3727	      }].slice(-12);
  3728	      const disproven = buildDisprovenApproachRecord({
  3729	        code: adjudication.code,
  3730	        failure_class: adjudication.failure_class,
  3731	        tool_calls: retryFingerprintToolCalls(trace),
  3732	      });
  3733	      const graveyard = mergeDisprovenApproach(verified.disproven_approaches, disproven);
  3734	      verified.disproven_approaches = graveyard.records;
  3735	      if (graveyard.repeated) {
  3736	        verified.repeated_failure_fingerprint = {
  3737	          fingerprint: disproven.fingerprint,
  3738	          observed_at: disproven.last_observed_at,
  3739	          instruction: "Do not repeat a functionally equivalent approach unchanged. Change hypothesis/tool path or use the existing escalation route.",
  3740	        };
  3741	        verified.next_action = "CHANGE_STRATEGY_OR_ESCALATE_SAME_OBJECTIVE_FROM_DISPROVEN_APPROACH";
  3742	      } else {
  3743	        delete verified.repeated_failure_fingerprint;
  3744	        verified.next_action = "SELF_REPAIR_SAME_OBJECTIVE_FROM_PRESERVED_EVIDENCE";
  3745	      }
  3746	      command.verified_current_state = verified;
  3747	      message = refreshMessage();
  3748	      message.payload = {
  3749	        ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  3750	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  3751	          : {}),
  3752	        verified_current_state: verified,
  3753	      };
  3754	      message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  3755	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  3756	        at: new Date().toISOString(),
  3757	        phase: "self-repair",
  3758	        status: "running" as const,
  3759	        label: adjudication.failure_class === "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME"
  3760	          ? `External runtime unavailable · attempting bounded canonical recovery (${adjudication.code})`
  3761	          : `Recoverable internal failure · preserving evidence and retrying same objective (${adjudication.code})`,
  3762	      }].slice(-80);
  3763	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3764	
  3765	      const existingProDiagnosis = verified.self_heal_pro_diagnostic;
  3766	      const eligibleForPro = shouldRequestSelfHealProDiagnosis({
  3767	        objective: command.objective,
  3768	        read_only: command.read_only,
  3769	        creator_only: creatorFrontierOnly,
  3770	        previously_attempted: Boolean(existingProDiagnosis),
  3771	        observed_tool_steps: trace.length,
  3772	        degraded_tool_count: adjudication.degraded_tool_calls.length,
  3773	        failure_code: adjudication.code,
  3774	        enabled: process.env.GEMINX_SELF_HEAL_PRO_ENABLED?.trim() !== "0",
  3775	      });
  3776	      if (eligibleForPro) {
  3777	        // Persist the attempt BEFORE the provider request. On provider timeout or
  3778	        // worker restart we must never buy a second Pro diagnosis for this job.
  3779	        const at = new Date().toISOString();
  3780	        verified.self_heal_pro_diagnostic = {
  3781	          status: "ATTEMPTED",
  3782	          at,
  3783	          failure_code: adjudication.code,
  3784	          failure_fingerprint: disproven.fingerprint,
  3785	        };
  3786	        command.verified_current_state = verified;
  3787	        message = refreshMessage();
  3788	        message.payload = {
  3789	          ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  3790	            ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  3791	            : {}),
  3792	          verified_current_state: verified,
  3793	        };
  3794	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3795	        console.log(`[SELF-HEAL] phase=pro-diagnosis status=ATTEMPTED job_id=${message.message_id} fingerprint=${disproven.fingerprint.slice(0, 18)}`);
  3796	        try {
  3797	          const toolEvidence = String(redactSensitive(JSON.stringify(compactRetryToolTrace(trace))) ?? "").slice(0, 6_000);
  3798	          const diagnosis = await generateCompanionReplyWithTools(
  3799	            [
  3800	              "You are the one-shot DeepSeek V4 Pro DIAGNOSTIC tier for a failed GeminX code repair.",
  3801	              "This is THINK ONLY. You have no execution tools and may not claim you patched, tested or deployed anything.",
  3802	              "Give a concise root-cause hypothesis, exact likely source locations, minimal fix, tests to run, deployment proof, and rollback criteria.",
  3803	              "Distinguish source-proven findings from hypotheses; do not suggest bypassing authentication, spending limits or protected approval.",
  3804	              `CANONICAL JOB: ${message.message_id}`,
  3805	              `OBJECTIVE: ${command.objective.slice(0, 3_000)}`,
  3806	              `FAILURE: ${adjudication.code}`,
  3807	              `VERIFIED TOOL TRACE: ${toolEvidence}`,
  3808	            ].join("\n"),
  3809	            `${correlationId}:self-heal-pro:${disproven.fingerprint.slice(0, 18)}`,
  3810	            null,
  3811	            undefined,
  3812	            { modelPreference: "deepseek-v4-pro" },
  3813	            "reasoning",
  3814	          );
  3815	          verified.self_heal_pro_diagnostic = {
  3816	            status: "COMPLETED",
  3817	            at,
  3818	            model: diagnosis.model,
  3819	            failure_code: adjudication.code,
  3820	            failure_fingerprint: disproven.fingerprint,
  3821	            diagnosis: diagnosis.message.slice(0, 5_000),
  3822	            correlation_id: diagnosis.correlation_id ?? null,
  3823	          };
  3824	          verified.next_action = "APPLY_PRO_DIAGNOSIS_USING_EXISTING_FLASH_TOOLS_AND_VERIFY";
  3825	          command.verified_current_state = verified;
  3826	          message = refreshMessage();
  3827	          message.payload = {
  3828	            ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  3829	              ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  3830	              : {}),
  3831	            verified_current_state: verified,
  3832	            // The diagnostic tier has no write tools. Flash remains executor.
  3833	            model_preference: "deepseek",
  3834	          };
  3835	          message.status = "QUEUED";
  3836	          delete message.completed_at;
  3837	          delete message.receipt_id;
  3838	          delete message.reply_payload;
  3839	          message.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + 1000).toISOString();
  3840	          message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  3841	          message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  3842	            event_id: randomUUID(),
  3843	            at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  3844	            phase: "self-heal",
  3845	            status: "running" as const,
  3846	            label: "DeepSeek V4 Pro diagnosed one runtime fault; Flash will apply and verify under the same job",
  3847	          }].slice(-80);
  3848	          liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3849	          console.log(`[SELF-HEAL] phase=flash-executor status=QUEUED job_id=${message.message_id} diagnostic_model=${diagnosis.model} fingerprint=${disproven.fingerprint.slice(0, 18)}`);
  3850	          await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  3851	            console.warn(`[SELF-HEAL] phase=receipt status=FAILED job_id=${message.message_id} error_class=${error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"}`);
  3852	          });
  3853	          return message;
  3854	        } catch (error) {
  3855	          verified.self_heal_pro_diagnostic = {
  3856	            status: "UNAVAILABLE",
  3857	            at,
  3858	            failure_code: adjudication.code,
  3859	            failure_fingerprint: disproven.fingerprint,
  3860	            error_class: error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown",
  3861	          };
  3862	          command.verified_current_state = verified;
  3863	          message = refreshMessage();
  3864	          message.payload = {
  3865	            ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  3866	              ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  3867	              : {}),
  3868	            verified_current_state: verified,
  3869	          };
  3870	          liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3871	          console.warn(`[SELF-HEAL] phase=pro-diagnosis status=UNAVAILABLE job_id=${message.message_id} paid_retry=false`);
  3872	        }
  3873	      }
  3874	      if (message.objective_contract) {
  3875	        // The ordinary adjudication path must use the same bounded recovery
  3876	        // contract as thrown provider/runtime failures.
  3877	        return retrySameJob(new Error(
  3878	          "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SEMANTIC_STALL:" + adjudication.code
  3879	        ), disproven.fingerprint);
  3880	      }
  3881	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  3882	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SEMANTIC_STALL",
  3883	        original_code: adjudication.code,
  3884	        failure_fingerprint: disproven.fingerprint,
  3885	        founder_action_required: false,
  3886	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  3887	        // A semantic stall is a failure to complete the action, not a reason
  3888	        // to conceal the model's evidence-based explanation from the Founder.
  3889	        message: visibleMessage && !isInternalEngineLeak(visibleMessage)
  3890	          ? visibleMessage
  3891	          : "I could not verify the requested repair. The available tool results and failure are retained, and no change is being claimed as complete.",
  3892	        output: adjudication.output ?? "The run stalled and produced no usable output. Execution stopped without completing the requested objective; no additional paid retries are authorized.",
  3893	        tool_trace: compactRetryToolTrace(trace)
  3894	      });
  3895	    }
  3896	
  3897	    const adjudicationOutput = adjudication.output
  3898	      ? normalizeAutonomousRecoveryClaims(String(adjudication.output), recoveryTruth)
  3899	      : undefined;
  3900	
  3901	    message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  3902	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3903	    const noProgressFuse =
  3904	      adjudication.status === "BLOCKED" &&
  3905	      adjudication.code === "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_NO_PROGRESS_CIRCUIT_OPEN";
  3906	    const protectedHumanBoundary =
  3907	      adjudication.status === "BLOCKED" &&
  3908	      adjudication.failure_class === "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY";
  3909	    const externalRuntimeBoundary =
  3910	      adjudication.status === "BLOCKED" &&
  3911	      adjudication.failure_class === "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME";
  3912	    return complete(adjudication.status, {
  3913	      code: adjudication.code,
  3914	      ...(noProgressFuse ? {
  3915	        founder_action_required: false,
  3916	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  3917	        internal_safety_circuit: true,
  3918	      } : {}),
  3919	      ...(protectedHumanBoundary ? {
  3920	        founder_action_required: true,
  3921	        blocker_category: "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY",
  3922	      } : {}),
  3923	      ...(externalRuntimeBoundary ? {
  3924	        founder_action_required: false,
  3925	        blocker_category: "EXTERNAL_RUNTIME_BOUNDARY",
  3926	        external_runtime_boundary: true,
  3927	        suspension_class: "HOST_UNREACHABLE_AFTER_CANONICAL_RECOVERY",
  3928	      } : {}),
  3929	      ...(adjudicationOutput ? { output: adjudicationOutput } : {}),
  3930	      degraded_tool_calls: adjudication.degraded_tool_calls,
  3931	      delegation_lineage: lineage,
  3932	      ...safe,
  3933	    });
  3934	  } catch (error) {
  3935	    const cancelledJob = cancelled();
  3936	    if (cancelledJob || (error instanceof Error && error.message === "GEMINX_JOB_CANCELLED")) {
  3937	      return cancelledJob ?? refreshMessage();
  3938	    }
  3939	    if (error instanceof BrowserHumanTakeoverRequired) {
  3940	      message = refreshMessage();
  3941	      message.status = "WAITING_HUMAN";
  3942	      delete message.completed_at;
  3943	      delete message.receipt_id;
  3944	      delete message.next_attempt_at;
  3945	      message.last_error = undefined;
  3946	      message.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  3947	      message.reply_payload = {
  3948	        status: "WAITING_HUMAN",
  3949	        code: error.code,
  3950	        founder_action_required: true,
  3951	        blocker_category: "PROTECTED_HUMAN_BOUNDARY",
  3952	        session_id: error.session_id,
  3953	        challenge: error.challenge ?? null,
  3954	        url: error.url ?? null,
  3955	        output: error.challenge
  3956	          ? "A protected browser challenge was mechanically detected. The same task/session is preserved for resume after the protected human step."
  3957	          : "Browser human control is active, but no protected challenge detail was supplied."
  3958	      };
  3959	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  3960	      return message;
  3961	    }
  3962	
  3963	    // DeepSeek's explicit 402/429 is a provider boundary, not assistant speech.
  3964	    // Switch the SAME durable objective to live Gemini only if no native tool
  3965	    // action may have occurred. Do not replay an unknown external mutation.
  3966	    const errorDetail = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  3967	    const priorModel = String(command.model_preference ?? "deepseek").toLowerCase();
  3968	    const checkpointForFailover = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot })
  3969	      .getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  3970	    const checkpointMessages = checkpointForFailover?.messages ?? [];
  3971	    const verifiedBeforeFailover: Record<string, unknown> = command.verified_current_state &&
  3972	      typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  3973	      ? { ...command.verified_current_state } : {};
  3974	    const googleConfigured = Boolean(
  3975	      process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_API_KEY ||
  3976	      process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY ||
  3977	      process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY ||
  3978	      process.env.GEMINX_GEMINI_LIVE_API_KEY
  3979	    );
  3980	    const failoverPlan = planDeepSeekGoogleFailover({
  3981	      failure: errorDetail,
  3982	      googleConfigured,
  3983	      selectedDeepSeek: ["deepseek", "deepseek-flash", "deepseek-v4-pro"].includes(priorModel),
  3984	      googleAlreadyAttempted: Boolean(verifiedBeforeFailover.provider_failover_google_attempted_at),
  3985	      toolReceipts: registry.trace(correlationId).length,
  3986	      checkpointToolMessages: checkpointMessages.filter(row =>
  3987	        row.role === "tool" || Boolean(row.tool_calls?.length)).length,
  3988	      unresolvedToolCalls: unresolvedNativeToolCallIds(checkpointMessages).length
  3989	    });
  3990	    if (failoverPlan === "REROUTE_GOOGLE" && !creatorFrontierOnly) {
  3991	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  3992	      verifiedBeforeFailover.provider_failover_google_attempted_at = now;
  3993	      verifiedBeforeFailover.provider_failover_source = "deepseek";
  3994	      verifiedBeforeFailover.provider_failover_failure = errorDetail.slice(0, 600);
  3995	      verifiedBeforeFailover.next_action = "CONTINUE_SAME_OBJECTIVE_ON_GOOGLE_WITHOUT_TOOL_REPLAY";
  3996	      command.model_preference = "gemini-pro";
  3997	      command.verified_current_state = verifiedBeforeFailover;
  3998	      message = refreshMessage();
  3999	      message.payload = {
  4000	        ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  4001	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {}),
  4002	        model_preference: "gemini-pro",
  4003	        verified_current_state: verifiedBeforeFailover,
  4004	      };
  4005	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  4006	      delete message.completed_at;
  4007	      delete message.receipt_id;
  4008	      delete message.reply_payload;
  4009	      message.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + 1000).toISOString();
  4010	      message.last_error = errorDetail.slice(0, 800);
  4011	      message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  4012	      message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  4013	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  4014	        at: now,
  4015	        phase: "model-failover",
  4016	        status: "running" as const,
  4017	        label: "DeepSeek provider boundary · continuing the same objective on live Gemini (no tool replay)"
  4018	      }].slice(-80);
  4019	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4020	      return message;
  4021	    }
  4022	    if (failoverPlan === "RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED") {
  4023	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4024	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4025	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4026	        code: "GEMINX_PROVIDER_FAILOVER_TOOL_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4027	        founder_action_required: false,
  4028	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4029	        output: errorDetail,
  4030	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4031	      });
  4032	    }
  4033	    if (deepSeekCreditExhausted(error)) {
  4034	      return retrySameJob(error);
  4035	    }
  4036	
  4037	    if (isOpenAiCompanionModelPreference(command.model_preference) && astraProviderUnavailable(error)) {
  4038	      const observedAt = new Date();
  4039	      const creditExhausted = astraCreditExhausted(error);
  4040	      const verified = command.verified_current_state && typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  4041	        ? { ...command.verified_current_state }
  4042	        : {};
  4043	      verified.astra_unavailable_reason = creditExhausted
  4044	        ? "credit_balance_exhausted"
  4045	        : "provider_temporarily_unavailable";
  4046	      verified.astra_unavailable_at = observedAt.toISOString();
  4047	      delete verified.astra_reprobe_after;
  4048	      verified.astra_provider_boundary = creditExhausted
  4049	        ? "FOUNDER_PAYMENT_OR_TOPUP_MAY_BE_REQUIRED_TO_RESTORE_ASTRA"
  4050	        : "OPTIONAL_PROVIDER_TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE";
  4051	      verified.next_action = "CONTINUE_WITH_DEEPSEEK_FLASH";
  4052	      command.verified_current_state = verified;
  4053	      command.model_preference = "deepseek";
  4054	      message = refreshMessage();
  4055	      message.payload = {
  4056	        ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  4057	          ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  4058	          : {}),
  4059	        model_preference: command.model_preference,
  4060	        verified_current_state: verified,
  4061	      };
  4062	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  4063	      delete message.completed_at;
  4064	      delete message.receipt_id;
  4065	      delete message.reply_payload;
  4066	      delete message.next_attempt_at;
  4067	      message.last_error = undefined;
  4068	      message.last_heartbeat_at = observedAt.toISOString();
  4069	      message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  4070	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  4071	        at: message.last_heartbeat_at,
  4072	        phase: "model-reroute",
  4073	        status: "running" as const,
  4074	        label: creditExhausted
  4075	          ? "Astra credits unavailable · continuing same objective with DeepSeek Flash · no automatic paid re-probe"
  4076	          : "Astra temporarily unavailable · continuing same objective with DeepSeek Flash · no automatic paid re-probe"
  4077	      }].slice(-80);
  4078	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4079	      return message;
  4080	    }
  4081	
  4082	    const executionErrorText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
  4083	    // Flash can exhaust a turn after fully recorded tool receipts without
  4084	    // producing founder-visible text. A CLEAN, receipt-complete native transcript
  4085	    // can continue without replaying its past actions. Pending/unknown tool
  4086	    // outcomes still fail closed rather than duplicating a possible mutation.
  4087	    if (executionErrorText.includes("MODEL_DID_NOT_PRODUCE_A_USABLE_REPLY")) {
  4088	      let evidence: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  4089	      try {
  4090	        const parsed = JSON.parse(executionErrorText);
  4091	        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
  4092	          evidence = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
  4093	        }
  4094	      } catch {
  4095	        // Old/unstructured exceptions are never eligible for automated replay.
  4096	      }
  4097	      const checkpoint = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot })
  4098	        .getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  4099	      if (canResumeNoReplyFromNativeCheckpoint(checkpoint, command.objective)) {
  4100	        const failures = Array.isArray(evidence.tool_failures)
  4101	          ? evidence.tool_failures.filter(row => row && typeof row === "object") as Record<string, unknown>[]
  4102	          : [];
  4103	        const railwayDenied = failures.some(row =>
  4104	          String(row.tool ?? "").startsWith("railway_") &&
  4105	          /Not Authorized|RAILWAY_HTTP_(?:401|403)|RAILWAY_AUTH_REJECTED/i.test(String(row.error ?? "")));
  4106	        const githubMissing = failures.some(row =>
  4107	          row.tool === "github_read_file_range" &&
  4108	          /owner, repo and path are required/.test(String(row.error ?? "")));
  4109	        const verified = command.verified_current_state && typeof command.verified_current_state === "object"
  4110	          ? { ...command.verified_current_state } : {};
  4111	        verified.no_reply_native_checkpoint_recovery = {
  4112	          original_correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
  4113	          model: evidence.model ?? "unknown",
  4114	          tool_failures: failures.slice(-8).map(row => ({
  4115	            tool: String(row.tool ?? ""),
  4116	            status: String(row.status ?? ""),
  4117	            error: String(row.error ?? "").slice(0, 180)
  4118	          })),
  4119	          next_action: railwayDenied
  4120	            ? "Railway read-only auth was rejected: do not retry identical Railway credentials or repeat the same Railway reads. Continue authorised GitHub/browser/preview code work and report exact pending Railway access after other phases."
  4121	            : githubMissing
  4122	              ? "Use explicit owner, repo, valid file path and exact ref. Discover actual file paths with github_repo_map first; never replay an empty github_read_file_range call."
  4123	              : "Continue from confirmed native tool receipts without replaying past actions. Choose a different executable path; never repeat an ambiguous write.",
  4124	          observed_at: new Date().toISOString()
  4125	        };
  4126	        command.verified_current_state = verified;
  4127	        message = refreshMessage();
  4128	        message.payload = {
  4129	          ...(message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
  4130	            ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {}),
  4131	          verified_current_state: verified
  4132	        };
  4133	        message.activity = [...(message.activity ?? []), {
  4134	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  4135	          at: new Date().toISOString(),
  4136	          phase: "model-recovery",
  4137	          status: "running" as const,
  4138	          label: "Model returned no usable final text · native tool receipts were complete; continuing same job from clean checkpoint"
  4139	        }].slice(-80);
  4140	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4141	        return retrySameJob(error, "MODEL_NO_REPLY_CLEAN_CHECKPOINT");
  4142	      }
  4143	      // A pending or unmatched tool has an unknown side effect. Never replay it.
  4144	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4145	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4146	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4147	        code: "GEMINX_MODEL_NO_REPLY_NATIVE_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4148	        founder_action_required: false,
  4149	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4150	        output: "The model returned no usable text. The native checkpoint has a pending, mismatched or incomplete tool receipt; the possible external action was NOT repeated.",
  4151	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4152	      });
  4153	    }
  4154	
  4155	    if (executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_NOT_CONFIGURED") || executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_MCP_NOT_VERIFIED")) {
  4156	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4157	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4158	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4159	        code: executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_NOT_CONFIGURED")
  4160	          ? "AGX_HARNESS_NOT_CONFIGURED"
  4161	          : "AGX_HARNESS_MCP_NOT_VERIFIED",
  4162	        founder_action_required: false,
  4163	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_CREDENTIAL_MISSING",
  4164	        output: executionErrorText,
  4165	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4166	      });
  4167	    }
  4168	
  4169	    // Legacy Gemini no-reply reconciliation handling
  4170	    if (executionErrorText.includes("GEMINX_GEMINI_NO_REPLY_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED")) {
  4171	      const store = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot });
  4172	      const checkpoint = store.getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  4173	      const toolTrace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  4174	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4175	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4176	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4177	        code: "GEMINX_GEMINI_NO_REPLY_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4178	        founder_action_required: false,
  4179	        internal_safety_circuit: true,
  4180	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4181	        output: `Google Gemini returned no usable final answer after executing tools. Tool receipts: ${toolTrace.length}. Replay suppressed to prevent duplicating completed actions.`,
  4182	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4183	      });
  4184	    }
  4185	
  4186	    // A local office-role policy rejection happens BEFORE any Google Interactions
  4187	    // request. Its preflight/read receipt is not uncertain mutating work, and
  4188	    // it must not be reported as an absent provider interaction ID.
  4189	    if (executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_LOCAL_PREPROVIDER_DENIED")) {
  4190	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4191	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4192	      const localCode = executionErrorText.match(/AGX_HARNESS_LOCAL_PREPROVIDER_DENIED:\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)/i)?.[1]
  4193	        || "LOCAL_ROLE_TOOL_DENIED";
  4194	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4195	        code: "AGX_HARNESS_LOCAL_PREPROVIDER_DENIED",
  4196	        founder_action_required: false,
  4197	        internal_safety_circuit: true,
  4198	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4199	        output: `Google managed dispatch was blocked by local policy before contacting Google (${localCode}). No provider interaction exists, no paid inference was made and no mutations were replayed.`,
  4200	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4201	      });
  4202	    }
  4203	
  4204	    // For Google managed Antigravity: distinguish pending/incomplete interactions or
  4205	    // potential mutations from clean terminal failures. Never replay uncertain mutating work.
  4206	    // Preserve the original job ID, interaction ID, checkpoint, receipts and lease.
  4207	    const modelPref = String(command.model_preference || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  4208	    const isManagedRoute = modelPref === "antigravity" ||
  4209	      executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_") ||
  4210	      executionErrorText.includes("Google managed Antigravity");
  4211	    if (isManagedRoute && (
  4212	        executionErrorText.includes("AGX_HARNESS_NOT_COMPLETED") ||
  4213	        executionErrorText.includes("Google managed Antigravity did not complete") ||
  4214	        (error instanceof AntigravityCompanionError && modelPref === "antigravity"))) {
  4215	      const store = new ConversationStore({ root: liveStore.runtimeRoot });
  4216	      const conversationRecord = command.conversation_id ? store.get(command.conversation_id) : null;
  4217	      const managedSession = conversationRecord?.managed_antigravity_session;
  4218	      const checkpoint = store.getExecutionCheckpoint(message.message_id);
  4219	      const toolTrace = registry.trace(correlationId);
  4220	      const externalToolTrace = toolTrace.filter(
  4221	        t => t.tool !== "antigravity_harness_status" && t.tool !== "antigravity_harness_execute"
  4222	      );
  4223	      const remoteToolCallsMatch = executionErrorText.match(/remote_tool_calls=(\d+)/i);
  4224	      const remoteToolCount = remoteToolCallsMatch ? Number(remoteToolCallsMatch[1]) : 0;
  4225	      const hasToolCalls = externalToolTrace.length > 0 || remoteToolCount > 0 ||
  4226	        Boolean(checkpoint?.messages?.some(m => m.role === "tool" || Boolean(m.tool_calls?.length)));
  4227	      const statusMatch = executionErrorText.match(/status=([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/i);
  4228	      const idMatch = executionErrorText.match(/interaction_id=([^\s]+)/i);
  4229	      const sessionStatus = statusMatch?.[1] ?? managedSession?.status ?? "unknown";
  4230	      const effectiveInteractionId = (idMatch?.[1] && idMatch[1] !== "none" ? idMatch[1] : undefined) ?? managedSession?.interaction_id;
  4231	      const isPendingOrIncomplete = ["in_progress", "incomplete", "requires_action", "pending", "unknown"].includes(sessionStatus) ||
  4232	        /in_progress|incomplete|requires_action|pending/i.test(executionErrorText);
  4233	
  4234	      const validInteractionId = Boolean(
  4235	        effectiveInteractionId &&
  4236	        !effectiveInteractionId.startsWith("err-") &&
  4237	        !effectiveInteractionId.startsWith("fail-")
  4238	      );
  4239	      if (hasToolCalls || (isPendingOrIncomplete && validInteractionId)) {
  4240	        message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4241	        liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4242	        let llmExplanation = "";
  4243	        try {
  4244	          const diag = await generateAntigravityCompanionReplyWithTools(
  4245	            `Summarize in 2-3 direct sentences for Rob what just happened on this turn and what the current status is, without corporate fluff or asterisks. Objective: ${String(command.objective || "").slice(0, 500)}. Provider status: ${sessionStatus}. Interaction ID: ${effectiveInteractionId ?? "none"}. External tool calls recorded: ${externalToolTrace.length + remoteToolCount}. Diagnostic detail: ${executionErrorText.slice(0, 700)}`,
  4246	            correlationId,
  4247	            null
  4248	          );
  4249	          llmExplanation = String(diag.message || "").trim();
  4250	        } catch {
  4251	          llmExplanation = "";
  4252	        }
  4253	        const technicalFooter = `Google managed Antigravity ${sessionStatus === "completed" ? "completed without a usable final answer" : `did not complete (status=${sessionStatus})`}. External tool receipts: ${externalToolTrace.length + remoteToolCount}. Uncertain mutating work was not replayed. Original job ID, interaction ID (${effectiveInteractionId ?? "none"}), checkpoint, and receipts preserved for reconciliation.`;
  4254	        return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4255	          code: "GEMINX_MANAGED_INTERACTION_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4256	          founder_action_required: false,
  4257	          internal_safety_circuit: true,
  4258	          blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4259	          output: llmExplanation ? `${llmExplanation}\n\n[${technicalFooter}]` : `${executionErrorText}\n\n[${technicalFooter}]`,
  4260	          interaction_id: effectiveInteractionId,
  4261	          environment_id: managedSession?.environment_id,
  4262	          provider_status: sessionStatus,
  4263	          delegation_lineage: lineage
  4264	        });
  4265	      }
  4266	
  4267	      // Clean terminal failure with zero tool calls: route through retry circuit under failure fingerprint
  4268	      return retrySameJob(error, "AGX_HARNESS_TERMINAL_FAILURE");
  4269	    }
  4270	    if (executionErrorText.includes("BLOCKED_TURN_0_UNBOUND_CONNECTOR")) {
  4271	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4272	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4273	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4274	        code: "BLOCKED_TURN_0_UNBOUND_CONNECTOR",
  4275	        founder_action_required: false,
  4276	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_CREDENTIAL_MISSING",
  4277	        llm_calls: 0,
  4278	        output: executionErrorText,
  4279	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4280	      });
  4281	    }
  4282	    if (/GEMINX_(?:TOOL_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED|EXECUTION_ALREADY_TERMINAL|EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT_[A-Z_]+)/i.test(executionErrorText)) {
  4283	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4284	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4285	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4286	        code: "GEMINX_NATIVE_TRANSCRIPT_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED",
  4287	        founder_action_required: false,
  4288	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4289	        output: executionErrorText,
  4290	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4291	      });
  4292	    }
  4293	    if (/DEEPSEEK_(?:OBJECTIVE_LIFETIME_BUDGET_REACHED|TASK_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED|PROVIDER_REQUEST_BUDGET_REACHED|OBJECTIVE_LEDGER_LOCK_UNAVAILABLE|OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_FUSE_TRIPPED)|GEMINX_(?:WORKFLOW_SCHEMA_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED|CONTEXT_PRESSURE_PREFLIGHT)/i.test(executionErrorText)) {
  4294	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4295	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4296	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4297	        code: "GEMINX_INTERNAL_FINITE_BUDGET_OR_CAPACITY_BOUNDARY",
  4298	        founder_action_required: false,
  4299	        blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4300	        output: executionErrorText,
  4301	        delegation_lineage: lineage
  4302	      });
  4303	    }
  4304	    if (deepSeekDailySpendReservationBusy(error)) {
  4305	      return retrySameJob(error);
  4306	    }
  4307	    if (/DEEPSEEK_DAILY_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED|DeepSeek daily spend budget reached/i.test(executionErrorText)) {
  4308	      message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4309	      liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4310	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4311	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED",
  4312	        founder_action_required: false,
  4313	        mutable_policy_boundary: true,
  4314	        recoverable_after_material_budget_change: true,
  4315	        delegation_lineage: lineage,
  4316	        output: executionErrorText
  4317	      });
  4318	    }
  4319	
  4320	    if (transientExecutionError(error)) {
  4321	      return retrySameJob(error);
  4322	    }
  4323	
  4324	    // Any unclassified internal failure is not permission to switch paid models
  4325	    // and requeue a fresh execution from scratch. Preserve the failure receipt.
  4326	    message.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  4327	    liveStore.saveMessage(message);
  4328	    return complete("FAILED", {
  4329	      code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_EXECUTION_ERROR_NO_AUTORETRY",
  4330	      founder_action_required: false,
  4331	      blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4332	      delegation_lineage: lineage,
  4333	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
  4334	    });
  4335	  }
  4336	}
  4337	
  4338	
  4339	function frontierReasonCommand(payload: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  4340	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4341	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4342	  if (String(value.command ?? "") !== "GEMINX_FRONTIER_REASON") return null;
  4343	  return value;
  4344	}
  4345	
  4346	async function executeFrontierReason(message: GeminxMessage, command: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  4347	  const complete = (status: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED" | "FAILED", reply: Record<string, unknown>) => {
  4348	    message.status = status;
  4349	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  4350	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  4351	    message.reply_payload = {
  4352	      status,
  4353	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  4354	      route: "frontier-reasoning",
  4355	      ...reply,
  4356	    };
  4357	    return message;
  4358	  };
  4359	
  4360	  if (message.sender.toLowerCase() !== "newton") {
  4361	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4362	      code: "FRONTIER_SENDER_FORBIDDEN",
  4363	      output: "Only the governed Newton controller may invoke the frontier reasoning command.",
  4364	    });
  4365	  }
  4366	
  4367	  const authority = String(command.authority ?? "");
  4368	  if (authority !== "ROB_PREAPPROVED_FRONTIER_REASONING_V1") {
  4369	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4370	      code: "FRONTIER_AUTHORITY_INVALID",
  4371	      output: "Frontier reasoning requires the founder-approved authority contract.",
  4372	    });
  4373	  }
  4374	
  4375	  const taskType = String(command.task_type ?? "reasoning") as any;
  4376	  let route;
  4377	  try {
  4378	    route = routeGeminxFrontier({
  4379	      task_type: taskType,
  4380	      prior_verified_failures: Number(command.prior_verified_failures ?? 0),
  4381	      behavioral_score: typeof command.behavioral_score === "number" ? command.behavioral_score : null,
  4382	      conflicting_evidence: command.conflicting_evidence === true,
  4383	      unfamiliar_problem: command.unfamiliar_problem === true,
  4384	      protected_surface_proposal: command.protected_surface_proposal === true,
  4385	      force_frontier: command.force_frontier === true,
  4386	      deepseek_api_key_present: Boolean(env("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY")),
  4387	      openai_api_key_present: openAiSpendEnabled() && Boolean(env("OPENAI_API_KEY")),
  4388	      deepseek_available: true,
  4389	      openai_available: openAiSpendEnabled(),
  4390	      astra_effort: typeof command.astra_effort === "string" ? command.astra_effort as any : undefined,
  4391	    });
  4392	  } catch (error) {
  4393	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4394	      code: "FRONTIER_TASK_TYPE_UNSUPPORTED",
  4395	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unsupported frontier task type.",
  4396	      task_type: taskType,
  4397	    });
  4398	  }
  4399	
  4400	  if (route.provider !== "openai" || route.model !== "gpt-6-astra") {
  4401	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4402	      code: "FRONTIER_ESCALATION_NOT_SELECTED",
  4403	      model_route: route,
  4404	      output: route.provider === "none"
  4405	        ? route.reason
  4406	        : "Canonical routing kept this objective on DeepSeek; use the normal task path instead of spending frontier budget.",
  4407	    });
  4408	  }
  4409	
  4410	  const objective = String(command.objective ?? "").trim();
  4411	  if (!objective) {
  4412	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4413	      code: "FRONTIER_OBJECTIVE_REQUIRED",
  4414	      output: "A non-empty objective is required.",
  4415	    });
  4416	  }
  4417	
  4418	  const brainSnapshot = await configuredAgentBrainContextRetriever().retrieve(objective, {
  4419	    activeDomain: /roblox|creator|game/i.test(objective) ? "roblox" : "geminx",
  4420	    activeGoal: objective,
  4421	  });
  4422	
  4423	  if (
  4424	    brainSnapshot.status !== "attached" ||
  4425	    brainSnapshot.revision_verified !== true ||
  4426	    !brainSnapshot.revision ||
  4427	    !brainSnapshot.content_sha256 ||
  4428	    brainSnapshot.truncated === true
  4429	  ) {
  4430	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4431	      code: "GEMINX_AGENT_BRAIN_BOOT_UNVERIFIED",
  4432	      blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  4433	      founder_action_required: false,
  4434	      nonterminal: true,
  4435	      output: "Canonical Geminex Brain boot is unavailable, revision-unverified, integrity-unbound or truncated. Governed mutation remains fail-closed until the same objective can prove an exact Brain revision and full-content digest.",
  4436	      evidence: {
  4437	        status: brainSnapshot.status,
  4438	        repository: brainSnapshot.repository ?? null,
  4439	        requested_ref: brainSnapshot.requested_ref ?? null,
  4440	        revision: brainSnapshot.revision ?? null,
  4441	        revision_verified: brainSnapshot.revision_verified === true,
  4442	        content_sha256: brainSnapshot.content_sha256 ?? null,
  4443	        truncated: brainSnapshot.truncated === true,
  4444	        truncated_paths: brainSnapshot.truncated_paths ?? [],
  4445	        missing_required_paths: brainSnapshot.missing_required_paths ?? [],
  4446	        sources: brainSnapshot.sources,
  4447	      },
  4448	    });
  4449	  }
  4450	
  4451	  const suppliedVerifiedState = command.verified_current_state;
  4452	  const verifiedState = {
  4453	    supplied: suppliedVerifiedState ?? {},
  4454	    shared_agent_brain: {
  4455	      status: brainSnapshot.status,
  4456	      sources: brainSnapshot.sources,
  4457	      context: brainSnapshot.context,
  4458	      repository: brainSnapshot.repository,
  4459	      requested_ref: brainSnapshot.requested_ref,
  4460	      revision: brainSnapshot.revision,
  4461	      revision_verified: brainSnapshot.revision_verified,
  4462	      content_sha256: brainSnapshot.content_sha256,
  4463	      truncated: brainSnapshot.truncated,
  4464	      truncated_paths: brainSnapshot.truncated_paths,
  4465	      missing_required_paths: brainSnapshot.missing_required_paths,
  4466	    },
  4467	  };
  4468	
  4469	  const arrayOfStrings = (value: unknown): string[] =>
  4470	    Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && Boolean(item.trim())).map(item => item.trim()) : [];
  4471	
  4472	  const context = compileFrontierContext({
  4473	    objective,
  4474	    acceptance_criteria: arrayOfStrings(command.acceptance_criteria),
  4475	    verified_current_state: verifiedState,
  4476	    relevant_failures: Array.isArray(command.relevant_failures) ? command.relevant_failures : [],
  4477	    relevant_receipts: Array.isArray(command.relevant_receipts) ? command.relevant_receipts : [],
  4478	    repo_system_map: command.repo_system_map ?? null,
  4479	    permissions: arrayOfStrings(command.permissions),
  4480	    doctrine: arrayOfStrings(command.doctrine),
  4481	    tools: arrayOfStrings(command.tools),
  4482	    budget: command.budget && typeof command.budget === "object" && !Array.isArray(command.budget)
  4483	      ? command.budget as { max_objective_usd?: number; max_output_tokens?: number }
  4484	      : undefined,
  4485	  });
  4486	
  4487	  try {
  4488	    const budget = command.budget && typeof command.budget === "object" && !Array.isArray(command.budget)
  4489	      ? command.budget as Record<string, unknown>
  4490	      : {};
  4491	    const objectiveId = String(command.objective_id ?? message.correlation_id ?? message.message_id);
  4492	    const astra = await invokeAstra({
  4493	      context,
  4494	      reasoning_effort: route.reasoning_effort as any,
  4495	      objective_id: objectiveId,
  4496	      max_output_tokens: typeof budget.max_output_tokens === "number" ? budget.max_output_tokens : undefined,
  4497	      max_objective_usd: typeof budget.max_objective_usd === "number" ? budget.max_objective_usd : undefined,
  4498	    });
  4499	
  4500	    const learning = astra.result.learning_result;
  4501	    const learningPersistence = learning.should_persist
  4502	      ? await persistFrontierLearningCandidate({
  4503	          provider: astra.provider,
  4504	          model: astra.model,
  4505	          objective_id: objectiveId,
  4506	          source_receipt_id: `receipt-${message.message_id}`,
  4507	          task_status: astra.result.task_result.status,
  4508	          lesson_type: learning.lesson_type,
  4509	          title: learning.title,
  4510	          summary: learning.summary,
  4511	          reusable_when: learning.reusable_when,
  4512	          invalidation_conditions: learning.invalidation_conditions,
  4513	          evidence_required: learning.evidence_required,
  4514	          task_evidence: astra.result.task_result.evidence,
  4515	          observed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  4516	        }).catch((error) => ({
  4517	          status: "failed" as const,
  4518	          path: null,
  4519	          fingerprint: null,
  4520	          promotion_state: "CANDIDATE_UNVERIFIED" as const,
  4521	          error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  4522	        }))
  4523	      : {
  4524	          status: "skipped" as const,
  4525	          path: null,
  4526	          fingerprint: null,
  4527	          promotion_state: "NOT_APPLICABLE" as const,
  4528	        };
  4529	
  4530	    return complete("COMPLETED", {
  4531	      code: "ASTRA_FRONTIER_RESULT",
  4532	      model_route: route,
  4533	      frontier_receipt: astra,
  4534	      learning_persistence: learningPersistence,
  4535	    });
  4536	  } catch (error) {
  4537	    return complete("FAILED", {
  4538	      code: "ASTRA_FRONTIER_EXECUTION_FAILED",
  4539	      model_route: route,
  4540	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  4541	    });
  4542	  }
  4543	}

  4546	type PennyPodBridgeAction = "MOLTBOOK_POST" | "MOLTBOOK_COMMENT";
  4547	
  4548	function pennyPodBridgeCommand(payload: unknown): {
  4549	  action: PennyPodBridgeAction;
  4550	  authority: string;
  4551	  created_at: string;
  4552	  reason: string;
  4553	  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  4554	} | null {
  4555	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4556	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4557	  const command = String(value.command ?? "");
  4558	  const action: PennyPodBridgeAction | null =
  4559	    command === "PENNY_POD_MOLTBOOK_POST"
  4560	      ? "MOLTBOOK_POST"
  4561	      : command === "PENNY_POD_MOLTBOOK_COMMENT"
  4562	        ? "MOLTBOOK_COMMENT"
  4563	        : null;
  4564	  if (!action) return null;
  4565	  if (!value.payload || typeof value.payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(value.payload)) return null;
  4566	  return {
  4567	    action,
  4568	    authority: String(value.authority ?? ""),
  4569	    created_at: String(value.created_at ?? ""),
  4570	    reason: String(value.reason ?? ""),
  4571	    payload: value.payload as Record<string, unknown>,
  4572	  };
  4573	}

  4575	async function executePennyPodBridge(message: GeminxMessage, command: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof pennyPodBridgeCommand>>) {
  4576	  const token = env("PENNY_POD_BRIDGE_TOKEN");
  4577	  const baseUrl = env("PENNY_POD_INTERNAL_URL") || "https://penny-pod-a2a-production.up.railway.app";
  4578	  if (!token) throw new Error("PENNY_POD_BRIDGE_TOKEN_MISSING");
  4579	
  4580	  const response = await fetch(baseUrl.replace(/\/$/, "") + "/api/internal/social-command", {
  4581	    method: "POST",
  4582	    headers: {
  4583	      "authorization": "Bearer " + token,
  4584	      "content-type": "application/json",
  4585	      "accept": "application/json",
  4586	    },
  4587	    body: JSON.stringify({
  4588	      message_id: message.message_id,
  4589	      action: command.action,
  4590	      authority: command.authority,
  4591	      created_at: command.created_at,
  4592	      reason: command.reason,
  4593	      payload: command.payload,
  4594	    }),
  4595	    signal: typeof AbortSignal.timeout === "function" ? AbortSignal.timeout(20_000) : undefined,
  4596	  });
  4597	  const raw = await response.text();
  4598	  let body: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  4599	  try {
  4600	    body = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, unknown> : {};
  4601	  } catch {
  4602	    body = { raw: raw.slice(0, 500) };
  4603	  }
  4604	  return { response, body };
  4605	}

  4608	type SmartSiteProspectBusAction = "GET" | "UPSERT";
  4609	const DIGITAL_AMBUSH_AUTHORITY = "ROB_PREAPPROVED_DIGITAL_AMBUSH_V1";
  4610	const BUSINESS_SOLUTIONS_SITE_ID = "entreprenuity-business-solutions";
  4611	
  4612	function smartSiteProspectBusCommand(payload: unknown): {
  4613	  action: SmartSiteProspectBusAction;
  4614	  authority: string;
  4615	  site_id: string;
  4616	  slug: string;
  4617	  prospect: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  4618	} | null {
  4619	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4620	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4621	  const command = String(value.command ?? "");
  4622	  const action: SmartSiteProspectBusAction | null =
  4623	    command === "ENTREPRENUITY_SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_GET"
  4624	      ? "GET"
  4625	      : command === "ENTREPRENUITY_SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_UPSERT"
  4626	        ? "UPSERT"
  4627	        : null;
  4628	  if (!action) return null;
  4629	
  4630	  const prospect = value.prospect && typeof value.prospect === "object" && !Array.isArray(value.prospect)
  4631	    ? value.prospect as Record<string, unknown>
  4632	    : null;
  4633	
  4634	  return {
  4635	    action,
  4636	    authority: String(value.authority ?? ""),
  4637	    site_id: String(value.site_id ?? ""),
  4638	    slug: String(value.slug ?? prospect?.slug ?? ""),
  4639	    prospect,
  4640	  };
  4641	}
  4642	
  4643	async function executeSmartSiteProspectBus(
  4644	  message: GeminxMessage,
  4645	  command: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof smartSiteProspectBusCommand>>,
  4646	  store: SmartSiteStore
  4647	): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  4648	  const complete = async (
  4649	    status: "COMPLETED" | "BLOCKED" | "FAILED",
  4650	    replyPayload: Record<string, unknown>
  4651	  ): Promise<GeminxMessage> => {
  4652	    message.status = status;
  4653	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  4654	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  4655	    message.reply_payload = {
  4656	      status,
  4657	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  4658	      route: "smart-site-prospect-bus",
  4659	      ...replyPayload,
  4660	    };
  4661	    return message;
  4662	  };
  4663	
  4664	  if (message.sender.toLowerCase() !== "newton") {
  4665	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4666	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_SENDER_FORBIDDEN",
  4667	      output: "Only the governed Newton controller may invoke the Digital Ambush prospect bus.",
  4668	    });
  4669	  }
  4670	  if (command.authority !== DIGITAL_AMBUSH_AUTHORITY) {
  4671	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4672	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_AUTHORITY_INVALID",
  4673	      output: "Digital Ambush prospect mutation authority is missing or invalid.",
  4674	    });
  4675	  }
  4676	  if (command.site_id !== BUSINESS_SOLUTIONS_SITE_ID) {
  4677	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4678	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_SITE_FORBIDDEN",
  4679	      output: "The prospect bus is restricted to the canonical Business Solutions site.",
  4680	    });
  4681	  }
  4682	  if (!command.slug || !/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,119}$/i.test(command.slug)) {
  4683	    return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4684	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_SLUG_INVALID",
  4685	      output: "A valid canonical prospect slug is required.",
  4686	    });
  4687	  }
  4688	
  4689	  try {
  4690	    if (command.action === "GET") {
  4691	      const prospect = store.getProspect(command.site_id, command.slug);
  4692	      if (!prospect) {
  4693	        return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4694	          code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_NOT_FOUND",
  4695	          found: false,
  4696	          site_id: command.site_id,
  4697	          slug: command.slug,
  4698	        });
  4699	      }
  4700	      return complete("COMPLETED", {
  4701	        code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_FOUND",
  4702	        found: true,
  4703	        prospect,
  4704	      });
  4705	    }
  4706	
  4707	    if (!command.prospect) {
  4708	      return complete("BLOCKED", {
  4709	        code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_PAYLOAD_REQUIRED",
  4710	        output: "UPSERT requires a bounded prospect object.",
  4711	      });
  4712	    }
  4713	
  4714	    const prospect = store.upsertProspect(
  4715	      { ...command.prospect, slug: command.slug },
  4716	      command.site_id,
  4717	      { allowVerifiedIdentityCorrection: true }
  4718	    );
  4719	    return complete("COMPLETED", {
  4720	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_UPSERTED",
  4721	      found: true,
  4722	      prospect,
  4723	    });
  4724	  } catch (error) {
  4725	    return complete("FAILED", {
  4726	      code: "SMART_SITE_PROSPECT_BUS_ERROR",
  4727	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  4728	    });
  4729	  }
  4730	}
  4731	
  4732	function githubHeaders(token: string) {
  4733	  return {
  4734	    Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
  4735	    Accept: "application/vnd.github+json",
  4736	    "X-GitHub-Api-Version": GITHUB_API_VERSION,
  4737	    "User-Agent": "GeminX-Inbox-Worker/1.0"
  4738	  };
  4739	}
  4740	
  4741	function contentsUrl(filePath: string, ref?: string): string {
  4742	  const encodedPath = filePath.split("/").map(part => encodeURIComponent(part)).join("/");
  4743	  const base = `https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/contents/${encodedPath}`;
  4744	  return ref ? `${base}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}` : base;
  4745	}
  4746	
  4747	function redactReceiptValue(value: unknown): unknown {
  4748	  if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  4749	  try {
  4750	    const raw = JSON.stringify(value);
  4751	    const redacted = redactSensitive(raw);
  4752	    return typeof redacted === "string" ? JSON.parse(redacted) : value;
  4753	  } catch {
  4754	    return "[UNSERIALIZABLE]";
  4755	  }
  4756	}
  4757	
  4758	type ReceiptSnapshot = Record<string, unknown>;
  4759	
  4760	function receiptTerminalStatus(value: unknown): boolean {
  4761	  return new Set(["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED", "WAITING_HUMAN"])
  4762	    .has(String(value ?? "").trim().toUpperCase());
  4763	}
  4764	
  4765	function receiptAttempt(value: ReceiptSnapshot): number {
  4766	  const parsed = Number(value.attempt_count ?? 0);
  4767	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 ? parsed : 0;
  4768	}
  4769	
  4770	function receiptCompletedAt(value: ReceiptSnapshot): number | null {
  4771	  const parsed = Date.parse(String(value.completed_at ?? ""));
  4772	  return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
  4773	}
  4774	
  4775	function receiptActivityLength(value: ReceiptSnapshot): number {
  4776	  return Array.isArray(value.activity) ? value.activity.length : 0;
  4777	}
  4778	
  4779	export function receiptSnapshotSupersedesLocal(
  4780	  canonical: ReceiptSnapshot,
  4781	  local: ReceiptSnapshot
  4782	): boolean {
  4783	  if (String(canonical.message_id ?? "") !== String(local.message_id ?? "")) return false;
  4784	
  4785	  const canonicalCorrelation = String(canonical.correlation_id ?? "").trim();
  4786	  const localCorrelation = String(local.correlation_id ?? "").trim();
  4787	  if (canonicalCorrelation && localCorrelation && canonicalCorrelation !== localCorrelation) return false;
  4788	
  4789	  const canonicalAttempt = receiptAttempt(canonical);
  4790	  const localAttempt = receiptAttempt(local);
  4791	  if (canonicalAttempt > localAttempt) return true;
  4792	  if (canonicalAttempt < localAttempt) return false;
  4793	
  4794	  const canonicalTerminal = receiptTerminalStatus(canonical.status);
  4795	  const localTerminal = receiptTerminalStatus(local.status);
  4796	  if (canonicalTerminal && !localTerminal) return true;
  4797	  if (!canonicalTerminal && localTerminal) return false;
  4798	
  4799	  const canonicalCompleted = receiptCompletedAt(canonical);
  4800	  const localCompleted = receiptCompletedAt(local);
  4801	  if (canonicalCompleted !== null && localCompleted !== null) {
  4802	    if (canonicalCompleted > localCompleted) return true;
  4803	    if (canonicalCompleted < localCompleted) return false;
  4804	  } else if (canonicalCompleted !== null && localCompleted === null) {
  4805	    return true;
  4806	  } else if (canonicalCompleted === null && localCompleted !== null) {
  4807	    return false;
  4808	  }
  4809	
  4810	  return String(canonical.status ?? "") === String(local.status ?? "")
  4811	    && receiptActivityLength(canonical) >= receiptActivityLength(local);
  4812	}
  4813	
  4814	function decodeGithubReceipt(content: unknown): ReceiptSnapshot | null {
  4815	  const encoded = String(content ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
  4816	  if (!encoded) return null;
  4817	  try {
  4818	    const decoded = Buffer.from(encoded, "base64").toString("utf8");
  4819	    const parsed = JSON.parse(decoded);
  4820	    return parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
  4821	      ? parsed as ReceiptSnapshot
  4822	      : null;
  4823	  } catch {
  4824	    return null;
  4825	  }
  4826	}
  4827	
  4828	async function pushReceiptToBrain(message: GeminxMessage): Promise<void> {
  4829	  const token = env("GEMINX_REPO_ACCESS_TOKEN");
  4830	  if (!token) {
  4831	    console.warn(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_not_persisted message_id=${message.message_id} reason=repo_access_missing`);
  4832	    return;
  4833	  }
  4834	
  4835	  const receiptPath = `Agents/GEMINX/Outbox/ToNewton/${message.message_id}/RECEIPT.json`;
  4836	  const receiptContent = {
  4837	    receipt_id: message.receipt_id || `receipt-${message.message_id}`,
  4838	    message_id: message.message_id,
  4839	    correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
  4840	    sender: message.sender,
  4841	    recipient: message.recipient,
  4842	    status: message.status,
  4843	    priority: message.priority,
  4844	    type: message.type,
  4845	    created_at: message.created_at,
  4846	    acknowledged_at: message.acknowledged_at,
  4847	    completed_at: message.completed_at,
  4848	    attempt_count: message.attempt_count,
  4849	    last_error: redactReceiptValue(message.last_error),
  4850	    activity: redactReceiptValue(message.activity),
  4851	    delegation_lineage: redactReceiptValue(message.delegation_lineage),
  4852	    reply_payload: redactReceiptValue(message.reply_payload)
  4853	  };
  4854	  const encodedContent = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(receiptContent, null, 2)).toString("base64");
  4855	
  4856	  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 5; attempt += 1) {
  4857	    let existingSha: string | undefined;
  4858	    const existing = await fetch(contentsUrl(receiptPath, GITHUB_BRANCH), {
  4859	      headers: githubHeaders(token)
  4860	    });
  4861	
  4862	    if (existing.ok) {
  4863	      const metadata = await existing.json() as { sha?: string };
  4864	      existingSha = metadata.sha;
  4865	    } else if (existing.status !== 404) {
  4866	      throw new Error(`RECEIPT_LOOKUP_FAILED_${existing.status}`);
  4867	    }
  4868	
  4869	    const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
  4870	      message: `geminx: persist execution receipt to Newton for ${message.message_id} [skip ci]`,
  4871	      content: encodedContent,
  4872	      branch: GITHUB_BRANCH
  4873	    };
  4874	    if (existingSha) body.sha = existingSha;
  4875	
  4876	    const response = await fetch(contentsUrl(receiptPath), {
  4877	      method: "PUT",
  4878	      headers: {
  4879	        ...githubHeaders(token),
  4880	        "Content-Type": "application/json"
  4881	      },
  4882	      body: JSON.stringify(body)
  4883	    });
  4884	
  4885	    if (response.ok) return;
  4886	    if (response.status !== 409) {
  4887	      throw new Error(`RECEIPT_WRITE_FAILED_${response.status}`);
  4888	    }
  4889	
  4890	    // A concurrent webhook/worker may have persisted this exact terminal receipt
  4891	    // between our GET and PUT. Reconcile before writing again: identical content
  4892	    // is success, while genuinely different content is rebased on the next loop.
  4893	    const reconciled = await fetch(contentsUrl(receiptPath, GITHUB_BRANCH), {
  4894	      headers: githubHeaders(token)
  4895	    });
  4896	    if (reconciled.ok) {
  4897	      const current = await reconciled.json() as { content?: string; sha?: string };
  4898	      const currentContent = String(current.content ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, "");
  4899	      if (currentContent && currentContent === encodedContent.replace(/\s+/g, "")) {
  4900	        console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_reconciled message_id=${message.message_id} attempt=${attempt} action=ALREADY_PERSISTED`);
  4901	        return;
  4902	      }
  4903	
  4904	      const canonicalReceipt = decodeGithubReceipt(current.content);
  4905	      if (canonicalReceipt && receiptSnapshotSupersedesLocal(canonicalReceipt, receiptContent)) {
  4906	        console.log(
  4907	          `[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_reconciled message_id=${message.message_id} attempt=${attempt} action=CANONICAL_NEWER canonical_attempt=${receiptAttempt(canonicalReceipt)} local_attempt=${receiptAttempt(receiptContent)} canonical_status=${String(canonicalReceipt.status ?? "UNKNOWN")} local_status=${String(receiptContent.status ?? "UNKNOWN")}`
  4908	        );
  4909	        return;
  4910	      }
  4911	    } else if (reconciled.status !== 404) {
  4912	      throw new Error(`RECEIPT_RECONCILE_FAILED_${reconciled.status}`);
  4913	    }
  4914	
  4915	    if (attempt === 5) {
  4916	      throw new Error("RECEIPT_WRITE_FAILED_409");
  4917	    }
  4918	    console.warn(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_conflict message_id=${message.message_id} attempt=${attempt} action=REFRESH_AND_RETRY_RECEIPT`);
  4919	    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, attempt * 125));
  4920	  }
  4921	}
  4922	
  4923	function scheduledFounderMessageCommand(payload: unknown): {
  4924	  text: string;
  4925	  title: string;
  4926	  conversation_id: string | null;
  4927	} | null {
  4928	  if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || Array.isArray(payload)) return null;
  4929	  const value = payload as Record<string, unknown>;
  4930	  if (String(value.command ?? "") !== "GEMINX_SCHEDULED_MESSAGE_V1") return null;
  4931	  const text = String(value.text ?? "").trim().slice(0, 2000);
  4932	  if (!text) return null;
  4933	  const title = String(value.title ?? "GeminX").trim().slice(0, 120) || "GeminX";
  4934	  const conversationId = typeof value.conversation_id === "string" && value.conversation_id.trim()
  4935	    ? value.conversation_id.trim()
  4936	    : null;
  4937	  return { text, title, conversation_id: conversationId };
  4938	}
  4939	
  4940	async function executeScheduledFounderMessage(
  4941	  message: GeminxMessage,
  4942	  command: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof scheduledFounderMessageCommand>>,
  4943	  commsStore: CommsStore
  4944	): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  4945	  const deliveredAt = new Date().toISOString();
  4946	  let conversationWritten = false;
  4947	
  4948	  if (command.conversation_id) {
  4949	    try {
  4950	      const record = conversationStore.get(command.conversation_id);
  4951	      if (record) {
  4952	        conversationStore.appendTurn(record, {
  4953	          role: "assistant",
  4954	          message_id: message.message_id,
  4955	          content: command.text,
  4956	          at: deliveredAt
  4957	        });
  4958	        conversationWritten = true;
  4959	      }
  4960	    } catch {
  4961	      conversationWritten = false;
  4962	    }
  4963	  }
  4964	
  4965	  // A successful timer firing is not successful Founder delivery. If the
  4966	  // conversation record is absent/unwritable, report the failure truthfully.
  4967	  const deliveryStatus = conversationWritten ? "COMPLETED" : "BLOCKED";
  4968	  const deliveryCode = conversationWritten
  4969	    ? "GEMINX_SCHEDULED_MESSAGE_DELIVERED"
  4970	    : "GEMINX_SCHEDULED_CONVERSATION_DELIVERY_UNVERIFIED";
  4971	  const push = await notifyFounder({
  4972	    title: conversationWritten ? command.title : "GeminX follow-up delivery failed",
  4973	    body: conversationWritten ? command.text : "The scheduled message ran but could not be written to its Founder HQ conversation. The original message and receipt remain preserved.",
  4974	    job_id: message.message_id,
  4975	    status: deliveryStatus,
  4976	    url: command.conversation_id ? "/hq/?conversation=" + encodeURIComponent(command.conversation_id) : "/hq/",
  4977	    tag: "geminx-scheduled-" + message.message_id
  4978	  }).catch(() => ({ sent: 0, failed: 0, removed: 0 }));
  4979	
  4980	  message.status = deliveryStatus;
  4981	  message.completed_at = deliveredAt;
  4982	  message.last_heartbeat_at = deliveredAt;
  4983	  message.receipt_id = "receipt-" + message.message_id;
  4984	  message.reply_payload = {
  4985	    status: deliveryStatus,
  4986	    code: deliveryCode,
  4987	    worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  4988	    delivered_at: deliveredAt,
  4989	    conversation_written: conversationWritten,
  4990	    push
  4991	  };
  4992	  message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  4993	    event_id: randomUUID(),
  4994	    at: deliveredAt,
  4995	    phase: "scheduled-delivery",
  4996	    status: conversationWritten ? "done" as const : "blocked" as const,
  4997	    label: conversationWritten ? "Scheduled Founder follow-up delivered" : "Scheduled Founder follow-up delivery unverified"
  4998	  }].slice(-80);
  4999	  commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5000	  console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] scheduled_delivery message_id=${message.message_id} conversation_written=${conversationWritten} push_sent=${push.sent} push_failed=${push.failed} push_removed=${push.removed}`);
  5001	  return message;
  5002	}
  5003	
  5004	
  5005	type AutonomousDependencyGate =
  5006	  | { state: "READY"; completed: string[] }
  5007	  | { state: "WAITING"; pending: Array<{ message_id: string; status: GeminxMessage["status"] }> }
  5008	  | { state: "BLOCKED"; reason: string; failed: Array<{ message_id: string; status: string }> };
  5009	
  5010	function autonomousDependencyGate(
  5011	  command: GeminxAutonomousTaskCommand,
  5012	  commsStore: CommsStore
  5013	): AutonomousDependencyGate {
  5014	  const ids = [...new Set(command.depends_on_message_ids.map((id) => id.trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  5015	  if (ids.length === 0) return { state: "READY", completed: [] };
  5016	
  5017	  const completed: string[] = [];
  5018	  const pending: Array<{ message_id: string; status: GeminxMessage["status"] }> = [];
  5019	  const failed: Array<{ message_id: string; status: string }> = [];
  5020	
  5021	  for (const id of ids) {
  5022	    const dependency = commsStore.getMessage(id);
  5023	    if (!dependency) {
  5024	      failed.push({ message_id: id, status: "MISSING" });
  5025	      continue;
  5026	    }
  5027	    if (dependency.status === "COMPLETED") {
  5028	      completed.push(id);
  5029	      continue;
  5030	    }
  5031	    if (["BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"].includes(dependency.status)) {
  5032	      failed.push({ message_id: id, status: dependency.status });
  5033	      continue;
  5034	    }
  5035	    pending.push({ message_id: id, status: dependency.status });
  5036	  }
  5037	
  5038	  if (failed.length > 0) {
  5039	    return {
  5040	      state: "BLOCKED",
  5041	      reason: "One or more declared dependencies are missing or terminally failed.",
  5042	      failed,
  5043	    };
  5044	  }
  5045	  if (pending.length > 0) return { state: "WAITING", pending };
  5046	  return { state: "READY", completed };
  5047	}
  5048
