  5051	  return [
  5052	    "Creator inventory is owned by this production backend. For current Roblox target/run capacity truth use creator_target_inventory directly; do not ask Rob for a Railway token merely to inspect GeminX's own Creator pool. When Rob has explicitly authorised failed-test cleanup and free capacity is below the readiness threshold of 2, use creator_reclaim_disposable_capacity with founder_authorized_cleanup=true instead of repeatedly rereading unchanged inventory. That deterministic tool releases at most one exact terminal disposable binding and independently fails closed on permanent/reserved, validated/published, Founder-test/public, historically published, mismatched or actively leased targets. Use creator_release_unpublished_target only when the Founder objective names one exact binding to release.",
  5053	    "Creator founder-journey loop: when the objective is to test, repair or finish GeminX Game Creator, do not treat backend health, API success, a green deployment or an internal stage receipt as sufficient acceptance. Use the browser lane to open the real Founder-facing Creator, select/open the exact persisted game/run under test, attempt the same Play/Open-in-Roblox journey Rob would perform, and observe the actual end-user result. If that journey fails, preserve the SAME run/game/artifact/universe/place/version identity, diagnose from browser/runtime/tool evidence, make the smallest repair in the existing canonical owner, then repeat the SAME founder journey. Continue this diagnose -> repair -> retest loop until exact receipts prove the game opens/playability succeeds or a genuine protected human/provider boundary remains. Do not create a replacement game/run merely to make the test pass.",
  5054	    "Creator model lane: autonomous Creator repair stays on DeepSeek Flash. Spend more effort on live tools, exact evidence, compact retries and durable self-repair rather than self-promoting to Pro or Astra. Keep the SAME durable task and accumulated evidence, do not fork a duplicate repair campaign, and after any repair return to the exact same browser/user journey and test again.",

Immediately inspect current system/Studio capability and continue the SAME objective through system_* / roblox_studio_* / ROBLOX_AI tools when connected. Preserve run/game/universe/place/version identity across the handoff. Ask: what would Rob actually click, what exact game should visibly open, what identity would prove it is the intended game, and could this test pass while Rob still sees the same failure? If yes, the probe is insufficient. Prefer outcome proof over mechanism proof. After every repair, rerun the real Founder journey, not merely the easiest API/unit check.",
  5057	  ];

  5059	
  5060	/** A Cloud AG CommsStore envelope must only be claimed by the Railway Cloud AG worker.
  5061	 * Any envelope addressed to AG (via recipient, office_agent, or ag- conversation)
  5062	 * is owned exclusively by the dedicated Cloud AG worker.
  5063	 */
  5064	export function isDedicatedCloudAgDelivery(
  5065	  message: Pick<GeminxMessage, "recipient" | "type" | "payload">
  5066	): boolean {
  5067	  const recipient = String(message.recipient ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  5068	  const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : null;
  5069	  const officeAgent = String(payload?.office_agent ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  5070	  const convId = String((message as any).conversation_id ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  5071	
  5072	  return recipient === "ag" || officeAgent === "ag" || convId.startsWith("ag-");
  5073	}
  5074	
  5075	export async function processGeminxInboxMessage(
  5076	  message: GeminxMessage,
  5077	  commsStore = new CommsStore(),
  5078	  taskEngine = new GeminxTaskEngineService(),
  5079	  smartSiteStore = new SmartSiteStore()
  5080	): Promise<GeminxMessage> {
  5081	  if (!isPrimaryAgentRecipient(message.recipient)) return message;
  5082	  if (isDedicatedCloudAgDelivery(message)) return message; // Cloud worker owns ACK+reply; no DeepSeek race.
  5083	
  5084	  // A sweep works from a queue snapshot. Another worker/recovery path can make
  5085	  // that same logical message terminal before this snapshot starts executing.
  5086	  // Always re-read canonical state before admission so a stale QUEUED object can
  5087	  // never resurrect a BLOCKED/FAILED/COMPLETED objective or emit a duplicate
  5088	  // Founder-facing reply.
  5089	  const canonicalAtAdmission = commsStore.getMessage(message.message_id);
  5090	  if (canonicalAtAdmission) {
  5091	    const canonicalStatus = String(canonicalAtAdmission.status ?? "").toUpperCase();
  5092	    if (["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED", "WAITING_HUMAN"].includes(canonicalStatus)) {
  5093	      console.log(
  5094	        `[geminx-inbox-worker] stale_admission_suppressed message_id=${message.message_id} stale_status=${message.status} canonical_status=${canonicalStatus}`
  5095	      );
  5096	      return canonicalAtAdmission;
  5097	    }
  5098	    // Continue from the freshest persisted state rather than an older sweep copy.
  5099	    message = canonicalAtAdmission;
  5100	  }
  5101	
  5102	  if (message.status === "CANCELLED") return message;
  5103	  if (message.status !== "QUEUED" || !dueForAttempt(message)) return message;
  5104	
  5105	  const autonomousPreflight = autonomousTaskCommand(message.payload);
  5106	  if (autonomousPreflight && autonomousPreflight.depends_on_message_ids.length > 0) {
  5107	    const dependencyGate = autonomousDependencyGate(autonomousPreflight, commsStore);
  5108	    const now = new Date();
  5109	
  5110	    if (dependencyGate.state === "BLOCKED") {
  5111	      const at = now.toISOString();
  5112	      message.status = "BLOCKED";
  5113	      message.completed_at = at;
  5114	      message.last_heartbeat_at = at;
  5115	      delete message.next_attempt_at;
  5116	      message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5117	      message.reply_payload = {
  5118	        status: "BLOCKED",
  5119	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5120	        route: "geminx-autonomous-companion",
  5121	        code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_DEPENDENCY_FAILED",
  5122	        output: dependencyGate.reason,
  5123	        dependencies: dependencyGate.failed,
  5124	      };
  5125	      message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  5126	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  5127	        at,
  5128	        phase: "dependency-gate",
  5129	        status: "blocked" as const,
  5130	        label: "Dependency failed or is missing · parent objective held closed"
  5131	      }].slice(-80);
  5132	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5133	      await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5134	      return message;
  5135	    }
  5136	
  5137	    if (dependencyGate.state === "WAITING") {
  5138	      const at = now.toISOString();
  5139	      message.status = "QUEUED";
  5140	      message.next_attempt_at = new Date(now.getTime() + 30_000).toISOString();
  5141	      message.last_heartbeat_at = at;
  5142	      const label = `Waiting for ${dependencyGate.pending.length} declared dependenc${dependencyGate.pending.length === 1 ? "y" : "ies"} · no model call`;
  5143	      const currentActivity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
  5144	      const last = currentActivity.at(-1);
  5145	      if (!(last?.phase === "dependency-gate" && last?.label === label)) {
  5146	        message.activity = [...currentActivity, {
  5147	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  5148	          at,
  5149	          phase: "dependency-gate",
  5150	          status: "running" as const,
  5151	          label
  5152	        }].slice(-80);
  5153	      }
  5154	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5155	      return message;
  5156	    }
  5157	  }
  5158	
  5159	  const contextResumeState =
  5160	    autonomousPreflight?.verified_current_state && typeof autonomousPreflight.verified_current_state === "object"
  5161	      ? autonomousPreflight.verified_current_state
  5162	      : {};
  5163	  const boundedContextResumePending =
  5164	    autonomousPreflight
  5165	    && contextResumeState.context_pressure_resume_pending === true
  5166	    && contextResumeState.force_compact_context === true;
  5167	
  5168	  const retryState = autonomousPreflight ? retryCircuitProgressState(message) : null;
  5169	  const retryStop =
  5170	    autonomousPreflight && retryState && retryState.provider_transient_failures >= geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()
  5171	      ? {
  5172	          code: "GEMINX_PROVIDER_TRANSIENT_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
  5173	          suspension_class: "PROVIDER_TRANSIENT_CIRCUIT_OPEN",
  5174	          detail: `Provider transient circuit already open (${retryState.provider_transient_failures}/${geminxAutonomousRetryLimit()}).`
  5175	        }
  5176	      : autonomousPreflight
  5177	        && retryState
  5178	        && retryState.turns_without_progress >= geminxNoProgressTurnLimit()
  5179	        && retryState.strategy_change_count >= geminxStrategyChangeLimit()
  5180	        ? {
  5181	            code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_STRATEGY_EXHAUSTED",
  5182	            suspension_class: "STRATEGY_EXHAUSTED",
  5183	            detail: `No-progress remained after ${retryState.strategy_change_count} materially different recovery strategies.`
  5184	          }
  5185	        : autonomousPreflight && retryState && retryState.total_objective_turns >= geminxObjectiveTurnLimitForCommand(autonomousPreflight)
  5186	          ? {
  5187	              code: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS",
  5188	              suspension_class: "MAX_OBJECTIVE_TURNS",
  5189	              detail: `Emergency run-loop ceiling reached (${retryState.total_objective_turns}/${geminxObjectiveTurnLimitForCommand(autonomousPreflight)}). This is a last-resort runaway guard, not a normal objective budget.`
  5190	            }
  5191	          : null;
  5192	  if (retryStop) {
  5193	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
  5194	    message.status = "BLOCKED";
  5195	    message.completed_at = now;
  5196	    delete message.next_attempt_at;
  5197	    message.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  5198	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5199	    message.reply_payload = {
  5200	      status: "BLOCKED",
  5201	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5202	      route: "geminx-autonomous-companion",
  5203	      code: retryStop.code,
  5204	      founder_action_required: false,
  5205	      blocker_category: "INTERNAL_RUNTIME_FAULT",
  5206	      internal_safety_circuit: true,
  5207	      suspension_class: retryStop.suspension_class,
  5208	      output: `${retryStop.detail} No model/provider call was made. This is an internal maintenance suspension, never a protected Founder boundary.`
  5209	    };
  5210	    message.activity = [...(Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : []), {
  5211	      event_id: randomUUID(),
  5212	      at: now,
  5213	      phase: "retry-circuit",
  5214	      status: "blocked" as const,
  5215	      label: `Internal safety circuit opened · ${retryStop.suspension_class} · Founder action not required`
  5216	    }].slice(-80);
  5217	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5218	    try {
  5219	      emitSeamFault({
  5220	        commsStore,
  5221	        correlation_id: message.correlation_id,
  5222	        parent_job_id: message.message_id,
  5223	        step_id: "RETRY_CIRCUIT_STOP",
  5224	        code: retryStop.code,
  5225	        message: retryStop.detail,
  5226	        intent: autonomousPreflight?.objective ?? null,
  5227	        serialized_payload: message.payload,
  5228	        attempt_count: message.attempt_count
  5229	      });
  5230	    } catch {}
  5231	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5232	    console.warn(`[geminx-inbox-worker] internal_safety_circuit message_id=${message.message_id} code=${retryStop.code} model_call_suppressed=true`);
  5233	    return message;
  5234	  }
  5235	
  5236	  message.status = "RUNNING";
  5237	  message.attempt_count = Math.max(0, Number(message.attempt_count ?? 0)) + 1;
  5238	  message.acknowledged_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5239	  message.last_heartbeat_at = message.acknowledged_at;
  5240	  delete message.next_attempt_at;
  5241	
  5242	  // Context-pressure retry-ceiling bypass is a one-shot permit. Consume it before
  5243	  // execution so an unrelated later failure cannot inherit an unlimited retry path.
  5244	  if (boundedContextResumePending && message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)) {
  5245	    const payload = { ...(message.payload as Record<string, unknown>) };
  5246	    const verified =
  5247	      payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
  5248	        ? { ...(payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown>) }
  5249	        : {};
  5250	    delete verified.context_pressure_resume_pending;
  5251	    payload.verified_current_state = verified;
  5252	    message.payload = payload;
  5253	  }
  5254	  commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5255	
  5256	  const scheduledMessage = scheduledFounderMessageCommand(message.payload);
  5257	  if (scheduledMessage) {
  5258	    const result = await executeScheduledFounderMessage(message, scheduledMessage, commsStore);
  5259	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5260	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] scheduled_receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5261	    });
  5262	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=founder-scheduled-message`);
  5263	    return result;
  5264	  }
  5265	
  5266	  const connectorResult = newtonConnectorResultCommand(message.payload);
  5267	  if (connectorResult) {
  5268	    const result = applyNewtonConnectorResult(message, connectorResult, commsStore);
  5269	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5270	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5271	    });
  5272	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=newton-chatgpt-connector-result`);
  5273	    return result;
  5274	  }
  5275	
  5276	  const frontierCommand = frontierReasonCommand(message.payload);
  5277	  if (frontierCommand) {
  5278	    const result = await executeFrontierReason(message, frontierCommand);
  5279	    commsStore.saveMessage(result);
  5280	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5281	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5282	    });
  5283	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=frontier-reasoning`);
  5284	    return result;
  5285	  }
  5286	
  5287	  const pennyPodCommand = pennyPodBridgeCommand(message.payload);
  5288	  if (pennyPodCommand) {
  5289	    try {
  5290	      const { response, body } = await executePennyPodBridge(message, pennyPodCommand);
  5291	      const ok = response.ok && body.ok === true;
  5292	      message.status = ok ? "COMPLETED" : "BLOCKED";
  5293	      message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5294	      message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5295	      message.reply_payload = {
  5296	        status: message.status,
  5297	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5298	        route: "penny-pod-social-bridge",
  5299	        provider_http_status: response.status,
  5300	        result: body,
  5301	      };
  5302	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5303	      await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  5304	        console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${message.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5305	      });
  5306	      console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${message.message_id} status=${message.status} route=penny-pod-social-bridge`);
  5307	      return message;
  5308	    } catch (error) {
  5309	      message.status = "FAILED";
  5310	      message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5311	      message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5312	      message.reply_payload = {
  5313	        status: "FAILED",
  5314	        code: "PENNY_POD_BRIDGE_ERROR",
  5315	        worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5316	        output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
  5317	      };
  5318	      commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5319	      await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5320	      return message;
  5321	    }
  5322	  }
  5323	
  5324	
  5325	  const smartSiteCommand = smartSiteProspectBusCommand(message.payload);
  5326	  if (smartSiteCommand) {
  5327	    const result = await executeSmartSiteProspectBus(message, smartSiteCommand, smartSiteStore);
  5328	    commsStore.saveMessage(result);
  5329	    await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5330	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5331	    });
  5332	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=smart-site-prospect-bus`);
  5333	    return result;
  5334	  }
  5335	
  5336	  const autonomousCommand = autonomousTaskCommand(message.payload);
  5337	  if (autonomousCommand) {
  5338	    const result = await executeAutonomousTask(message, autonomousCommand, commsStore);
  5339	    commsStore.saveMessage(result);
  5340	    if (!["QUEUED", "RUNNING", "CANCELLED"].includes(result.status)) {
  5341	      const terminalCode = String(result.reply_payload?.code ?? "UNKNOWN").replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ").slice(0, 120);
  5342	      const terminalDetailRaw = String(
  5343	        result.reply_payload?.output ??
  5344	        result.reply_payload?.message ??
  5345	        result.last_error ??
  5346	        ""
  5347	      ).replace(/[\r\n]+/g, " ").slice(0, 900);
  5348	      const terminalDetail = String(redactSensitive(terminalDetailRaw) ?? "").slice(0, 900);
  5349	      console.log(
  5350	        `[geminx-inbox-worker] terminal_evidence message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} code=${terminalCode} detail=${JSON.stringify(terminalDetail)}`
  5351	      );
  5352	      await pushReceiptToBrain(result).catch((error) => {
  5353	        console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${result.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5354	      });
  5355	    }
  5356	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${result.message_id} status=${result.status} route=geminx-autonomous-companion attempt=${result.attempt_count ?? 0}`);
  5357	    return result;
  5358	  }
  5359	
  5360	  const taskPackage = taskPackageFromPayload(message.payload);
  5361	  if (!taskPackage) {
  5362	    message.status = "BLOCKED";
  5363	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5364	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5365	    message.reply_payload = {
  5366	      status: "BLOCKED",
  5367	      code: "GEMINX_TASK_PACKAGE_REQUIRED",
  5368	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5369	      output: "Message reached GeminX but did not contain a bounded task_package, so no work was fabricated."
  5370	    };
  5371	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5372	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  5373	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${message.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5374	    });
  5375	    return message;
  5376	  }
  5377	
  5378	  try {
  5379	    const result = await taskEngine.executeTaskPackage(taskPackage);
  5380	    const status = result.receipt.status === "completed"
  5381	      ? "COMPLETED"
  5382	      : result.receipt.status === "blocked"
  5383	        ? "BLOCKED"
  5384	        : "FAILED";
  5385	
  5386	    message.status = status;
  5387	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5388	    message.receipt_id = result.receipt.id;
  5389	    message.reply_payload = {
  5390	      status,
  5391	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5392	      run_id: result.run.id,
  5393	      receipt_id: result.receipt.id,
  5394	      summary: result.receipt.summary,
  5395	      changed_files: result.receipt.changed_files,
  5396	      protected_action_refused: result.receipt.protected_action_refused,
  5397	      blockers: result.receipt.blockers,
  5398	      commands: result.receipt.commands,
  5399	      head_sha: result.receipt.head_sha,
  5400	      commit_sha: result.receipt.commit_sha
  5401	    };
  5402	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5403	
  5404	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch((error) => {
  5405	      console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] receipt_write_failed message_id=${message.message_id} error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5406	    });
  5407	
  5408	    console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${message.message_id} status=${status} run_id=${result.run.id}`);
  5409	    return message;
  5410	  } catch (error) {
  5411	    message.status = "FAILED";
  5412	    message.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5413	    message.receipt_id = `receipt-${message.message_id}`;
  5414	    message.reply_payload = {
  5415	      status: "FAILED",
  5416	      code: "GEMINX_TASK_EXECUTION_ERROR",
  5417	      worker: "geminx-inbox-worker",
  5418	      output: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
  5419	    };
  5420	    commsStore.saveMessage(message);
  5421	    await pushReceiptToBrain(message).catch(() => undefined);
  5422	    console.error(`[geminx-inbox-worker] message_id=${message.message_id} status=FAILED error=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5423	    return message;
  5424	  }
  5425	}
  5426	
  5427	async function sweep(): Promise<void> {
  5428	  if (sweepRunning) return;
  5429	  sweepRunning = true;
  5430	  lastSweepStartedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  5431	  try {
  5432	    const commsStore = new CommsStore();
  5433	    if (Date.now() - lastDurableMemoryMirrorFlushAt >= DURABLE_MEMORY_MIRROR_RETRY_MS) {
  5434	      lastDurableMemoryMirrorFlushAt = Date.now();
  5435	      void durableMemoryStore.flushBrainMirror(20)
  5436	        .then((result) => {
  5437	          if (result.pending > 0 || result.attempted > 0 || result.status !== "synced") {
  5438	            console.log(
  5439	              `[geminx-durable-memory] mirror status=${result.status} attempted=${result.attempted} synced=${result.synced} pending=${result.pending} brain_ref=${result.brain_ref} last_http_status=${result.last_http_status ?? "none"} last_error_class=${result.last_error_class ?? "none"} last_error_detail=${result.last_error_detail ?? "none"} cooldown_until=${result.cooldown_until ?? "none"}`
  5440	            );
  5441	          }
  5442	        })
  5443	        .catch((error) => {
  5444	          console.warn(`[geminx-durable-memory] mirror retry failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5445	        });
  5446	    }
  5447	    const availableSlots = Math.max(0, maxConcurrentJobs() - activeJobs.size);
  5448	    if (availableSlots <= 0) return;
  5449	
  5450	    const queued = commsStore.listMessages()
  5451	      .filter(message =>
  5452	        isPrimaryAgentRecipient(message.recipient) &&
  5453	        !isDedicatedCloudAgDelivery(message) &&
  5454	        message.status === "QUEUED" &&
  5455	        dueForAttempt(message) &&
  5456	        !activeJobs.has(message.message_id)
  5457	      )
  5458	      .sort(compareGeminxInboxQueue)
  5459	      .slice(0, availableSlots);
  5460	
  5461	    for (const message of queued) {
  5462	      activeJobs.add(message.message_id);
  5463	      const heartbeat = setInterval(() => heartbeatJob(commsStore, message.message_id), heartbeatMs());
  5464	      void processGeminxInboxMessage(message, commsStore, new GeminxTaskEngineService())
  5465	        .catch((error) => {
  5466	          const latest = commsStore.getMessage(message.message_id) ?? message;
  5467	          if (latest.status === "CANCELLED") {
  5468	            commsStore.saveMessage(latest);
  5469	          } else if (transientExecutionError(error)) {
  5470	            const attempt = Math.max(1, Number(latest.attempt_count ?? 1));
  5471	            const delayMs = retryDelayMs(attempt);
  5472	            latest.status = "QUEUED";
  5473	            latest.next_attempt_at = new Date(Date.now() + delayMs).toISOString();
  5474	            latest.last_error = error instanceof Error ? error.message.slice(0, 800) : String(error).slice(0, 800);
  5475	            latest.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5476	            commsStore.saveMessage(latest);
  5477	          } else {
  5478	            latest.status = "FAILED";
  5479	            latest.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  5480	            latest.last_error = error instanceof Error ? error.message.slice(0, 800) : String(error).slice(0, 800);
  5481	            latest.reply_payload = {
  5482	              status: "FAILED",
  5483	              code: "GEMINX_INBOX_UNHANDLED_EXECUTION_ERROR",
  5484	              output: latest.last_error
  5485	            };
  5486	            commsStore.saveMessage(latest);
  5487	          }
  5488	        })
  5489	        .finally(() => {
  5490	          clearInterval(heartbeat);
  5491	          activeJobs.delete(message.message_id);
  5492	        });
  5493	    }
  5494	  } finally {
  5495	    sweepRunning = false;
  5496	    lastSweepFinishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  5497	  }
  5498	}
  5499	
  5500	export function geminxInboxWorkerStatus(): {
  5501	  started: boolean;
  5502	  sweep_running: boolean;
  5503	  active_jobs: number;
  5504	  max_concurrent_jobs: number;
  5505	  poll_interval_ms: number;
  5506	  heartbeat_interval_ms: number;
  5507	  last_sweep_started_at: string | null;
  5508	  last_sweep_finished_at: string | null;
  5509	} {
  5510	  return {
  5511	    started,
  5512	    sweep_running: sweepRunning,
  5513	    active_jobs: activeJobs.size,
  5514	    max_concurrent_jobs: maxConcurrentJobs(),
  5515	    poll_interval_ms: intervalMs(),
  5516	    heartbeat_interval_ms: heartbeatMs(),
  5517	    last_sweep_started_at: lastSweepStartedAt,
  5518	    last_sweep_finished_at: lastSweepFinishedAt,
  5519	  };
  5520	}
  5521	
  5522	export function startGeminxInboxWorker(): void {
  5523	  if (started) return;
  5524	  started = true;
  5525	  console.log(`[geminx-inbox-worker] started interval_ms=${intervalMs()} heartbeat_ms=${heartbeatMs()} concurrency=${maxConcurrentJobs()}`);
  5526	  const memoryDiagnostics = durableMemoryStore.diagnostics();
  5527	  console.log(
  5528	    `[geminx-durable-memory] root=${memoryDiagnostics.memory_root} task_streams=${memoryDiagnostics.task_stream_count} conversation_streams=${memoryDiagnostics.conversation_stream_count} pending_mirror=${memoryDiagnostics.mirror_outbox_count} brain_ref=${memoryDiagnostics.brain_ref} mirror_armed=${memoryDiagnostics.mirror_armed}`
  5529	  );
  5530	  void sweep();
  5531	  setInterval(() => void sweep(), intervalMs());
  5532	}

    36	function objectOf(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
    37	  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : {};
    38	}
    39	function nonEmpty(value: unknown): string {
    40	  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
    41	}
    42	function evidenceFields(call: DurableToolReceipt): Record<string, unknown>[] {
    43	  const top = objectOf(call.result);
    44	  const data = objectOf(top.data);
    45	  return [top, data, objectOf(top.receipt), objectOf(top.proof), objectOf(data.receipt), objectOf(data.proof)];
    46	}
    47	function field(fields: Record<string, unknown>[], keys: string[]): unknown {
    48	  for (const part of fields) for (const key of keys) if (part[key] !== undefined && part[key] !== null) return part[key];
    49	  return undefined;
    50	}
    51	function validSha(raw: unknown, min = 40): string {
    52	  const str = nonEmpty(raw);
    53	  return new RegExp("^[a-fA-F0-9]{" + min + ",64}$").test(str) ? str : "";
    54	}
    55	function explicitCriterionIds(call: DurableToolReceipt): string[] {
    56	  const fields = evidenceFields(call);
    57	  const ids = [objectOf(call.args).criterion_id, field(fields, ["criterion_id"]), field(fields, ["criterion_ids"])];
    58	  return ids.flatMap(value => Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value])
    59	    .filter((id): id is string => typeof id === "string" && id.length > 0);
    60	}

    62	function isMutatingToolName(toolName: string): boolean {
    63	  return /\b(?:write|edit|patch|create_or_update|delete|remove|deploy|send|publish|submit|open|close|cancel|reset)\b/i.test(toolName) &&
    64	    !/\b(?:workspace_create_isolated|read|status|list|get|search|fetch|verify|inspect|health|scan)\b/i.test(toolName);
    65	}

    67	function receiptForCriterion(
    68	  call: DurableToolReceipt,
    69	  criterion: DurableCriterion,
    70	  allCalls: readonly DurableToolReceipt[] = []
    71	): string | null {
    72	  if (call.status !== "succeeded") return null;
    73	  const boundIds = explicitCriterionIds(call);
    74	  // An explicit criterion id must match. Otherwise only a concrete named
    75	  // evidence category may be automatically matched, never a generic task claim.
    76	  if (boundIds.length && !boundIds.includes(criterion.id)) return null;
    77	  const fields = evidenceFields(call);
    78	  const top = objectOf(call.result);
    79	  if (top.ok === false || top.connected === false || field(fields, ["degraded"]) === true ||
    80	    field(fields, ["error"]) || field(fields, ["failure"])) return null;
    81	  const tool = nonEmpty(call.tool);
    82	  if (!tool) return null;
    83	  const callSuffix = nonEmpty(call.tool_call_id)
    84	    ? ":" + nonEmpty(call.tool_call_id)
    85	    : ":" + createHash("sha256").update(tool + ":" + JSON.stringify(call.args ?? {})).digest("hex").slice(0, 12);
    86	  const description = criterion.description.toLowerCase();
    87	  // A model-provided criterion_id is routing metadata, not proof. Only an
    88	  // actual successful tool readback with a matching independently typed receipt
    89	  // can advance the state. A write/POST alone never certifies its own effect.
    90	  const sha = validSha(field(fields, ["commit_sha", "commitHash", "revision", "sha"])) ||
    91	    validSha(objectOf(top.commit).sha);
    92	  const deploymentId = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["deployment_id", "deploymentId"]));
    93	  const deploymentState = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["deployment_status", "status"])).toUpperCase();
    94	  const artifactHash = validSha(field(fields, ["artifact_sha256", "sha256"]), 64);
    95	  const artifactPath = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["artifact_path", "path", "url"]));
    96	  const exitCode = field(fields, ["exit_code", "exitCode"]);
    97	  const command = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["command", "test_command", "suite"]));
    98	  const statusRaw = field(fields, ["http_status", "status_code", "statusCode"]);
    99	  const httpStatus = typeof statusRaw === "number" ? statusRaw : Number(statusRaw);
   100	  const url = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["url", "request_url", "endpoint"]));
   101	  const readMethod = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["method"]) ?? objectOf(call.args).method).toUpperCase() || "GET";
   102	
   103	  const wantsReadOnlyConstraint = /\b(?:perform no mutation|no mutation|read[- ]only)\b/.test(description);
   104	  if (wantsReadOnlyConstraint) {
   105	    const anyMutationExecuted = allCalls.some(c => isMutatingToolName(nonEmpty(c.tool)));
   106	    if (anyMutationExecuted || isMutatingToolName(tool)) return null;
   107	    return "readonly_verified:" + tool + callSuffix;
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  const wantsDeploy = /\b(?:deploy|railway|production rollout|release to production)\b/.test(description);
   111	  const wantsTests = /\b(?:test|regression|typecheck|probe|vitest|compile|tsc)\b/.test(description);
   112	  const wantsArtifact = /\b(?:artifact|image|screenshot|video|file hash|sha256)\b/.test(description);
   113	  const wantsHttp = /\b(?:http|status code|live endpoint|website|api route|reachable)\b/.test(description);
   114	  const wantsGit = /\b(?:git|commit|source sha|repository revision)\b/.test(description);
   115	  const wantsInspection = /\b(?:inspect|truth|audit|verify|check)\b/.test(description);
   116	  const wantsRepair = /\b(?:fix|repair|remedy)\b/.test(description);
   117	  const wantsReceipts = /\b(?:receipts?|proven|completion|evidence)\b/.test(description);
   118	  const wantsContinue = /\b(?:continue|standing|self-maintenance)\b/.test(description);
   119	  const evidence: Record<string, string> = {};
   120	  if (/railway.*(?:status|deployment|describe|get)|deployment.*status/i.test(tool) &&
   121	      sha && deploymentId && deploymentState === "SUCCESS") {
   122	    evidence.deploy = "railway:" + deploymentId + ":" + sha;
   123	  }
   124	  if ((/test|shell|command|exec|compile/i.test(tool)) &&
   125	      Number(exitCode) === 0 && exitCode !== undefined &&
   126	      /\b(?:vitest|jest|pytest|npm\s+test|npx\s+tsc|typecheck|unittest)\b/i.test(command)) {
   127	    evidence.test = "test:" + tool + ":" + command.slice(0, 120) + ":exit0";
   128	  }
   129	  const isWorkspaceClean = field(fields, ["clean"]) === true || field(fields, ["status"]) === "VERIFIED_CLEAN";
   130	  const hasTestsPassed = field(fields, ["tests_passed"]) === true;
   131	  if (/workspace_run_verification/i.test(tool) && isWorkspaceClean && hasTestsPassed) {
   132	    const wsSha = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["receipt_sha256"])) || "passed";
   133	    evidence.test = "test:workspace_run_verification:" + wsSha.slice(0, 32);
   134	  }
   135	  if (/antigravity_harness_execute/i.test(tool)) {
   136	    evidence.test = "test:antigravity_harness:verified";
   137	  }
   138	  const ambushOutcome = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["outcome", "status"]));
   139	  const ambushPreviewUrl = nonEmpty(field(fields, ["preview_url", "concept_url"]));

   141	  if (/office_digital_ambush_run|office_digital_ambush_stage/i.test(tool) &&
   142	      (top.ok === true || ambushOutcome === "DOM_QA_PASSED_AWAITING_FOUNDER_APPROVAL" || Boolean(ambushPreviewUrl))) {
   143	    const ambushRef = (ambushProspectId || ambushPreviewUrl || "verified").slice(0, 120);
   144	    evidence.ambush = "ambush:" + tool + ":" + ambushRef;

   146	    evidence.artifact = evidence.artifact || ("artifact:ambush_concept:" + ambushRef);
   147	    evidence.http = evidence.http || ("http:ambush_preview:" + (ambushPreviewUrl || ambushRef).slice(0, 160));
   148	  }
   149	  if (/office_digital_ambush_status/i.test(tool) && (field(fields, ["found"]) === true || Boolean(ambushPreviewUrl))) {
   150	    const ambushRef = (ambushProspectId || ambushPreviewUrl || "status").slice(0, 120);

   152	    evidence.http = evidence.http || ("http:ambush_status:" + ambushRef);
   153	  }

   157	  const isBrowserCapture = /^(?:browser_take_screenshot|chrome_screenshot)$/.test(tool);

   162	    evidence.artifact = evidence.artifact || ("artifact:" + tool + ":" + imgRef);
   163	    evidence.test = evidence.test || ("test:" + tool + ":" + imgRef);
   164	  }

   166	  // speech and even successful POST/write results are not independent proof.
   167	  // Only a *separate* successful readback from the existing durable V7 CRM
   168	  // with the matching persisted ID, scope, and provenance may pass this gate.
   169	  if (/\b(?:crm|test[- ]?lead|lead capture|lead submission|submit (?:a )?lead)\b/i.test(description)) {
   170	    const id=nonEmpty(field(fields,["lead_id"]));
   171	    const proof=validSha(field(fields,["receipt_sha256"]),64);
   172	    if (tool !== "office_crm_verify_test_lead" ||
   173	        top.ok !== true || field(fields,["readback_verified"]) !== true ||
   174	        field(fields,["persisted"]) !== true ||

   176	        !/^lead-[a-z0-9-]{6,90}$/i.test(id) || !proof) return null;
   177	    return "crm:persisted:readback:"+id+":"+proof;
   178	  }
   179	  if (/read|inspect|status|verify|head|fetch/i.test(tool) && artifactHash && artifactPath) {
   180	    evidence.artifact = "artifact:" + artifactHash + ":" + artifactPath.slice(0, 120);
   181	  }
   182	  if (/read|status|fetch|verify|request|browse/i.test(tool) && httpStatus === 200 && url &&
   183	      readMethod === "GET") {
   184	    evidence.http = "http:get:200:" + url.slice(0, 180);
   185	  }
   186	  if (/github.*(?:fetch|read|status|commit|get|create_or_update|patch)|git.*(?:read|status|show)/i.test(tool) && sha) {
   187	    evidence.git = "git:" + sha;
   188	  }
   189	  if (wantsDeploy) return evidence.deploy ?? null;
   190	  if (wantsTests) return evidence.test || evidence.ambush || evidence.artifact || evidence.http || null;
   191	  if (wantsArtifact) return evidence.artifact || evidence.ambush || null;
   192	  if (wantsHttp) return evidence.http || evidence.ambush || null;
   193	  if (wantsGit) return evidence.git ?? null;
   194	  if (wantsInspection) return evidence.artifact || evidence.http || evidence.git || evidence.test || evidence.ambush || ("inspection:" + tool + callSuffix);
   195	  if (wantsRepair) return evidence.git || evidence.test || evidence.artifact || evidence.ambush || ("repair:" + tool + callSuffix);
   196	  if (wantsReceipts) return evidence.test || evidence.artifact || evidence.git || evidence.deploy || evidence.http || evidence.ambush || ("receipt:" + tool + callSuffix);
   197	  if (wantsContinue) return evidence.git || evidence.test || evidence.artifact || evidence.ambush || ("continue:" + tool + callSuffix);
   198	  // General/process criteria cannot be completed by a vaguely successful
   199	  // tool. They need an explicit criterion-specific acceptance workflow.
   200	  return boundIds.length
   201	    ? evidence.deploy || evidence.test || evidence.artifact || evidence.http || evidence.git || evidence.ambush || null
   202	    : evidence.ambush || null;
   203	}
   204	
   205	export function promoteDurableCriteriaFromToolReceipts(
   206	  contract: DurableObjectiveContract,
   207	  toolCalls: readonly DurableToolReceipt[]
   208	): CriterionPromotionResult {
   209	  const newly_verified: string[] = [];
   210	  const criteria = contract.acceptance_criteria.map(criterion => {
   211	    if (criterion.status === "VERIFIED" && criterion.evidence_receipt) return criterion;
   212	    // A FAILED criterion cannot be converted to VERIFIED without explicitly
   213	    // restarting that criterion through the owning acceptance workflow.
   214	    if (criterion.status !== "PENDING") return criterion;
   215	    for (const call of toolCalls) {
   216	      const evidence = receiptForCriterion(call, criterion, toolCalls);
   217	      if (evidence) {
   218	        newly_verified.push(criterion.id);
   219	        return { ...criterion, status: "VERIFIED" as const, evidence_receipt: evidence };
   220	      }
   221	    }
   222	    return criterion;
   223	  });
   224	  const remaining = criteria.filter(c => c.status !== "VERIFIED" || !c.evidence_receipt).map(c => c.id);
   225	  return {
   226	    contract: {
   227	      ...contract,
   228	      acceptance_criteria: criteria,
   229	      current_stage: remaining.length ? "VERIFYING" : "CRITERIA_VERIFIED",
   230	      next_action_pointer: remaining.length ? "VERIFY_CRITERIA:" + remaining.join(",") : "RECONCILE_TERMINAL_PROOF",
   231	      updated_at: newly_verified.length ? new Date().toISOString() : contract.updated_at
   232	    },
   233	    newly_verified,
   234	    remaining
   235	  };
   236	}
   237	
   238	export function classifyDurableArchetype(rawIntent: string, readOnly: boolean): DurableArchetype {
   239	  if (readOnly) return "AUDIT_OR_RESEARCH";
   240	  if (/\b(?:deploy|deployment|railway|vercel|production rollout|ship(?:ping)? to production)\b/i.test(rawIntent))
   241	    return "PRODUCTION_DEPLOYMENT";
   242	  if (/\b(?:outreach|campaign|email sequence|prospect|marketing|social post|social blitz|digital ambush|content|newsletter)\b/i.test(rawIntent))
   243	    return "CONTENT_OR_OUTREACH";
   244	  return "INFRASTRUCTURE_REPAIR";
   245	}
   246	export function ensureDurableObjective(
   247	  existing: Partial<DurableObjectiveContract> | undefined,
   248	  rawIntent: string,
   249	  criteria: string[] = [],
   250	  readOnly = false,
   251	  explicitSpendCeilingCents?: number | null
   252	): DurableObjectiveContract {
   253	  const previous = Array.isArray(existing?.acceptance_criteria) ? existing.acceptance_criteria : [];
   254	  const acceptance_criteria: DurableCriterion[] = previous.length
   255	    ? previous
   256	    : (criteria.length ? criteria : [rawIntent]).map((description, i) => ({
   257	      id: "criterion_" + (i + 1), description, status: "PENDING" as const
   258	    }));
   259	  const spend = existing?.spend_ceiling_cents ?? explicitSpendCeilingCents;
   260	  return {
   261	    objective_id: existing?.objective_id || randomUUID(),
   262	    raw_founder_intent: existing?.raw_founder_intent || rawIntent,
   263	    archetype: existing?.archetype || classifyDurableArchetype(existing?.raw_founder_intent || rawIntent, readOnly),
   264	    acceptance_criteria,
   265	    current_stage: existing?.current_stage || "ACTIVE_EXECUTION",
   266	    next_action_pointer: existing?.next_action_pointer || "EXECUTE_NEXT_UNVERIFIED_CRITERION",
   267	    recovery_attempts: Math.min(2, Math.max(0, Math.floor(existing?.recovery_attempts ?? 0))),
   268	    max_recovery_attempts: 2,
   269	    spend_ceiling_cents: typeof spend === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(spend) && spend >= 0 ? spend : null,
   270	    updated_at: existing?.updated_at || new Date().toISOString()
   271	  };
   272	}
   273	export function resolveDurableRecovery(
   274	  contract: DurableObjectiveContract,
   275	  failedToolName?: string
   276	): { action: "ATTEMPT_FALLBACK_CAPABILITY" | "ESCALATE_TO_WARDEN_INBOX"; contract: DurableObjectiveContract } {
   277	  const attempts = Math.max(0, Math.min(2, contract.recovery_attempts));
   278	  const canRecover = attempts < 2;
   279	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
   280	  return {
   281	    action: canRecover ? "ATTEMPT_FALLBACK_CAPABILITY" : "ESCALATE_TO_WARDEN_INBOX",
   282	    contract: {
   283	      ...contract, recovery_attempts: canRecover ? attempts + 1 : attempts, max_recovery_attempts: 2,
   284	      current_stage: canRecover ? "ATTEMPT_FALLBACK_CAPABILITY" : "WARDEN_CORRECTION_REQUIRED",
   285	      next_action_pointer: canRecover
   286	        ? failedToolName === "shell" ? "USE_GITHUB_OR_FS_DIRECT_TOOLS" : "RETRY_WITH_DIFFERENT_AUTHORISED_TOOL_STRATEGY"
   287	        : "ESCALATE_STRUCTURED_BLOCKER_WITH_EVIDENCE",
   288	      updated_at: now
   289	    }
   290	  };
   291	}
   292	
   293	/** Idempotent per (job, failure fingerprint), with a suffix never truncated away. */
   294	export function wardenCorrectionMessageId(jobId: string, failureFingerprint: string): string {
   295	  const safe = jobId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_").slice(0, 112);
   296	  const digest = createHash("sha256").update(jobId + "\\0" + failureFingerprint).digest("hex").slice(0, 24);
   297	  return "warden-correction-" + safe + "-" + digest;
   298	}

     2	  | "HEALTHY"

     9	export interface OpenClawDiagnosticSnapshot {
    10	  /**
    11	   * OpenClaw is optional to GeminX-native NEO. In native_neo mode the warden
    12	   * must not resurrect a stopped/missing OpenClaw gateway as though NEO were down.
    13	   */
    14	  control_plane_mode?: "legacy_gateway" | "native_neo";
    15	  gateway_service_installed: boolean;
    16	  gateway_running: boolean;
    17	  health_ready: boolean;
    18	  expected_worker_count: number;
    19	  active_worker_count: number;
    20	  duplicate_process_count?: number;
    21	  browser_required?: boolean;
    22	  browser_available?: boolean;
    23	  provider_auth_ok?: boolean;
    24	  provider_reachable?: boolean;
    25	  protected_policy_mismatch?: boolean;
    26	}
    27	
    28	export interface OpenClawDiagnosticDecision {
    29	  schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1";
    30	  incident_class: OpenClawIncidentClass;
    31	  safe_to_auto_repair: boolean;
    32	  reasons: string[];
    33	}
    34	
    35	function invalidProcessCount(label: string, value: number): string | null {
    36	  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) {
    37	    return `${label} must be a non-negative integer before GeminX can trust the OpenClaw snapshot.`;
    38	  }
    39	  return null;
    40	}
    41	
    42	function contradictoryRuntimeEvidence(snapshot: OpenClawDiagnosticSnapshot): string[] {
    43	  const reasons: string[] = [];
    44	  if (snapshot.control_plane_mode === "native_neo") return reasons;
    45	
    46	  if (!snapshot.gateway_service_installed && snapshot.gateway_running) {
    47	    reasons.push("OpenClaw telemetry claims the canonical gateway is running while its service is not installed.");
    48	  }
    49	  if (!snapshot.gateway_running && snapshot.health_ready) {
    50	    reasons.push("OpenClaw telemetry claims readiness while the canonical gateway is not running.");
    51	  }
    52	
    53	  return reasons;
    54	}
    55	
    56	export function diagnoseOpenClawSnapshot(snapshot: OpenClawDiagnosticSnapshot): OpenClawDiagnosticDecision {
    57	  const reasons: string[] = [];
    58	
    59	  if (snapshot.protected_policy_mismatch) {
    60	    return {
    61	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
    62	      incident_class: "PROTECTED_BOUNDARY",
    63	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
    64	      reasons: ["Protected OpenClaw policy/configuration differs from declared authority; automatic mutation is refused."]
    65	    };
    66	  }
    67	
    68	  const invalidCounts = [
    69	    invalidProcessCount("expected_worker_count", snapshot.expected_worker_count),
    70	    invalidProcessCount("active_worker_count", snapshot.active_worker_count),
    71	    snapshot.duplicate_process_count === undefined
    72	      ? null
    73	      : invalidProcessCount("duplicate_process_count", snapshot.duplicate_process_count)
    74	  ].filter((reason): reason is string => reason !== null);
    75	
    76	  if (invalidCounts.length > 0) {
    77	    return {
    78	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
    79	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
    80	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
    81	      reasons: invalidCounts
    82	    };
    83	  }
    84	
    85	  const contradictoryEvidence = contradictoryRuntimeEvidence(snapshot);
    86	  if (contradictoryEvidence.length > 0) {
    87	    return {
    88	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
    89	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
    90	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
    91	      reasons: contradictoryEvidence
    92	    };
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  const duplicates = snapshot.duplicate_process_count ?? 0;
    96	  const observedExcessProcesses = Math.max(0, snapshot.active_worker_count - snapshot.expected_worker_count);
    97	  if (duplicates !== observedExcessProcesses) {
    98	    return {
    99	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   100	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
   101	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   102	      reasons: [
   103	        `OpenClaw process telemetry is inconsistent: observed ${observedExcessProcesses} excess worker process(es) but duplicate_process_count reports ${duplicates}.`
   104	      ]
   105	    };
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  if (snapshot.provider_auth_ok === false) {
   109	    return {
   110	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   111	      incident_class: "ROB_REQUIRED",
   112	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   113	      reasons: ["Provider authentication is invalid or unavailable; credentials require owner action."]
   114	    };
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  if (snapshot.provider_reachable === false) {
   118	    return {
   119	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   120	      incident_class: "BLOCKED_EXTERNAL",
   121	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   122	      reasons: ["Configured provider is unreachable; do not rewrite a healthy local runtime for an external outage."]
   123	    };
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  const nativeNeo = snapshot.control_plane_mode === "native_neo";
   127	
   128	  if (!nativeNeo && !snapshot.gateway_service_installed) {
   129	    reasons.push("Legacy OpenClaw gateway service is not installed.");
   130	  }
   131	  if (snapshot.browser_required && snapshot.browser_available === false) {
   132	    reasons.push("Required browser capability is unavailable.");
   133	  }
   134	  if (snapshot.active_worker_count < snapshot.expected_worker_count) {
   135	    reasons.push(`Expected ${snapshot.expected_worker_count} worker(s), found ${snapshot.active_worker_count}.`);
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  if (reasons.length > 0) {
   139	    return {
   140	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   141	      incident_class: "CAPABILITY_GAP",
   142	      safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   143	      reasons
   144	    };
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  if ((!nativeNeo && (!snapshot.gateway_running || !snapshot.health_ready)) || duplicates > 0) {
   148	    if (!nativeNeo && !snapshot.gateway_running) reasons.push("Legacy OpenClaw gateway is not running.");
   149	    if (!nativeNeo && !snapshot.health_ready) reasons.push("Legacy OpenClaw gateway readiness/health check is failing.");
   150	    if (duplicates > 0) reasons.push(`Detected ${duplicates} duplicate managed process(es).`);
   151	
   152	    return {
   153	      schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   154	      incident_class: "AUTO_REPAIR_SAFE",
   155	      safe_to_auto_repair: true,
   156	      reasons
   157	    };
   158	  }
   159	
   160	  return {
   161	    schema_version: "geminx.openclaw-diagnostic.v1",
   162	    incident_class: "HEALTHY",
   163	    safe_to_auto_repair: false,
   164	    reasons: [nativeNeo
   165	      ? "GeminX-native NEO is authoritative; OpenClaw gateway presence is optional."
   166	      : "OpenClaw legacy gateway runtime state is healthy."]
   167	  };
   168	}

    18	function ensureDirectory(targetPath: string): void {
    19	  mkdirSync(targetPath, { recursive: true });
    20	}
    21	
    22	function readJsonFile<T>(filePath: string): T | null {
    23	  try {
    24	    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as T;
    25	  } catch (error) {
    26	    const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
    27	    if (code === "ENOENT") return null;
    28	
    29	    throw new Error(
    30	      `Runtime state is unreadable or corrupt at ${filePath}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
    31	    );
    32	  }
    33	}
    34	
    35	function atomicWrite(filePath: string, content: string): void {
    36	  ensureDirectory(path.dirname(filePath));
    37	  const tempPath = `${filePath}.${process.pid}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
    38	
    39	  try {
    40	    writeFileSync(tempPath, content, { encoding: "utf8", flag: "wx" });
    41	    renameSync(tempPath, filePath);
    42	  } catch (error) {
    43	    rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
    44	    throw error;
    45	  }
    46	}
    47	
    48	function assertSafeRuntimeId(label: string, value: string): string {
    49	  const hasWindowsUnsafeFilenameCharacter = /[<>:"|?*\u0000-\u001f]/u.test(value);
    50	  if (
    51	    value.length === 0 ||
    52	    value === "." ||
    53	    value === ".." ||
    54	    value.includes("/") ||
    55	    value.includes("\\") ||
    56	    hasWindowsUnsafeFilenameCharacter
    57	  ) {
    58	    throw new Error(`${label} contains an unsafe runtime path identifier.`);
    59	  }
    60	  return value;
    61	}

    63	function assertStoredIdentity(label: string, requestedId: string, storedId: unknown, filePath: string): void {
    64	  if (typeof storedId !== "string" || storedId !== requestedId) {
    65	    throw new Error(
    66	      `Runtime state identity mismatch at ${filePath}: expected ${label} ${requestedId}, found ${String(storedId)}`
    67	    );

    71	function readIndexedRecord<T extends { id: string }>(root: string, entry: string, label: string): T | null {
    72	  const filePath = path.join(root, entry);
    73	  const record = readJsonFile<T>(filePath);

    75	  const requestedId = entry.slice(0, -".json".length);

   120	export class GeminxRuntimeStore {
   121	  readonly runtimeRoot: string;
   122	  readonly runsRoot: string;
   123	  readonly receiptsRoot: string;
   124	  readonly logsRoot: string;
   125	  readonly locksRoot: string;
   126	  private readonly memoryStore: DurableMemoryStore;
   127	
   128	  constructor(runtimeRoot = process.env.GEMINX_RUNTIME_ROOT ?? path.join(process.cwd(), "runtime")) {
   129	    this.runtimeRoot = path.resolve(runtimeRoot);
   130	    this.runsRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "runs");
   131	    this.receiptsRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "receipts");
   132	    this.logsRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "logs");
   133	    this.locksRoot = path.join(this.runtimeRoot, "locks");
   134	    this.memoryStore = new DurableMemoryStore({ root: this.runtimeRoot });
   135	
   136	    ensureDirectory(this.runtimeRoot);
   137	    ensureDirectory(this.runsRoot);
   138	    ensureDirectory(this.receiptsRoot);
   139	    ensureDirectory(this.logsRoot);
   140	    ensureDirectory(this.locksRoot);
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  runPath(runId: string): string {
   144	    return path.join(this.runsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.json`);
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  receiptPath(receiptId: string): string {
   148	    return path.join(this.receiptsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("receiptId", receiptId)}.json`);
   149	  }
   150	
   151	  logPath(runId: string): string {
   152	    return path.join(this.logsRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.log`);
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  attemptLogPath(runId: string, receiptId: string): string {
   156	    return path.join(
   157	      this.logsRoot,
   158	      `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.${assertSafeRuntimeId("receiptId", receiptId)}.log`
   159	    );
   160	  }
   161	
   162	  private mutationLockPath(runId: string): string {
   163	    return path.join(this.locksRoot, `${assertSafeRuntimeId("runId", runId)}.lock`);
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  private currentProcessOwnsMutationLock(runId: string): boolean {
   167	    try {
   168	      const current = JSON.parse(readFileSync(this.mutationLockPath(runId), "utf8")) as { pid?: number };
   169	      return current.pid === process.pid;
   170	    } catch {
   171	      return false;
   172	    }
   173	  }
   174	
   175	  private tryAcquireMutationLock(lockPath: string, nowMs: number): boolean {
   176	    try {
   177	      writeFileSync(
   178	        lockPath,
   179	        JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, acquired_at: isoNow(nowMs) }),
   180	        { encoding: "utf8", flag: "wx" }
   181	      );
   182	      return true;
   183	    } catch (error) {
   184	      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw error;
   185	      return false;
   186	    }
   187	  }
   188	
   189	  private withRunMutationLock<T>(runId: string, operation: () => T): T {
   190	    const lockPath = this.mutationLockPath(runId);
   191	    const nowMs = Date.now();
   192	    let acquired = this.tryAcquireMutationLock(lockPath, nowMs);
   193	
   194	    if (!acquired) {
   195	      let acquiredAt = Number.NaN;
   196	      try {
   197	        const existing = JSON.parse(readFileSync(lockPath, "utf8")) as { acquired_at?: string };
   198	        acquiredAt = existing.acquired_at ? Date.parse(existing.acquired_at) : Number.NaN;
   199	      } catch {
   200	        acquiredAt = Number.NaN;
   201	      }
   202	      const stale = !Number.isFinite(acquiredAt) || nowMs - acquiredAt > MUTATION_LOCK_STALE_MS;
   203	      if (!stale) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_MUTATION_LOCK_BUSY");
   204	
   205	      rmSync(lockPath, { force: true });
   206	      acquired = this.tryAcquireMutationLock(lockPath, nowMs);
   207	      if (!acquired) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_MUTATION_LOCK_BUSY");
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    try {
   211	      return operation();
   212	    } finally {
   213	      rmSync(lockPath, { force: true });
   214	    }
   215	  }
   216	
   217	  private writeRun(record: GeminxRunRecord): string {
   218	    const filePath = this.runPath(record.id);
   219	    atomicWrite(filePath, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   220	    this.memoryStore.append({
   221	      stream_type: "task",
   222	      stream_id: record.task_id,
   223	      task_id: record.task_id,
   224	      run_id: record.id,
   225	      event_type: "RUN_STATE",
   226	      timestamp: record.updated_at ?? record.created_at,
   227	      actor: record.caller,
   228	      status: record.status,
   229	      summary: record.goal,
   230	      record_ref: `runs/${path.basename(filePath)}`,
   231	      record_sha256: durableMemoryRecordHash(record),
   232	      protected_action_refused: record.protected_action_refused === true,
   233	      idempotency_key: `run:${record.id}:${durableMemoryRecordHash(record)}`
   234	    });
   235	    return filePath;
   236	  }
   237	
   238	  appendTaskDiaryEvent(input: {
   239	    task_id: string;
   240	    run_id: string;
   241	    event_type: string;
   242	    timestamp?: string;
   243	    actor?: string | null;
   244	    status?: string | null;
   245	    summary?: string | null;
   246	    record_ref?: string | null;
   247	    idempotency_key?: string | null;
   248	  }): void {
   249	    this.memoryStore.append({
   250	      stream_type: "task",
   251	      stream_id: input.task_id,
   252	      task_id: input.task_id,
   253	      run_id: input.run_id,
   254	      event_type: input.event_type,
   255	      timestamp: input.timestamp,
   256	      actor: input.actor ?? null,
   257	      status: input.status ?? null,
   258	      summary: input.summary ?? null,
   259	      record_ref: input.record_ref ?? null,
   260	      idempotency_key: input.idempotency_key ?? null
   261	    });
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  saveRun(record: GeminxRunRecord): string {
   265	    const candidateLease = record.recovery?.status === "leased" ? record.recovery.lease : null;
   266	    if (!candidateLease || this.currentProcessOwnsMutationLock(record.id)) {
   267	      return this.writeRun(record);
   268	    }
   269	
   270	    return this.withRunMutationLock(record.id, () => {
   271	      const persisted = this.getRun(record.id);
   272	      if (!persisted) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${record.id}`);
   273	      if (persisted.task_id !== record.task_id) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_TASK_IDENTITY_MISMATCH");
   274	      assertLeaseOwner(recoveryState(persisted), candidateLease.lease_id, candidateLease.generation, Date.now());
   275	      return this.writeRun(record);
   276	    });
   277	  }
   278	
   279	  saveReceipt(record: GeminxReceiptRecord): string {
   280	    record.escalation = classifyGeminxEscalation({
   281	      status: record.status,
   282	      protectedActionRefused: record.protected_action_refused,
   283	      blockers: record.blockers,
   284	      commands: record.commands
   285	    });
   286	
   287	    const filePath = this.receiptPath(record.id);
   288	    atomicWrite(filePath, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));
   289	    const durableStreamId = String(record.task_id ?? record.run_id ?? record.id).trim();
   290	    this.memoryStore.append({
   291	      stream_type: "task",
   292	      stream_id: durableStreamId,
   293	      task_id: record.task_id ?? record.run_id,
   294	      run_id: record.run_id,
   295	      event_type: "RECEIPT",
   296	      timestamp: record.created_at,
   297	      actor: record.caller,
   298	      status: record.status,
   299	      summary: record.summary || record.goal,
   300	      record_ref: `receipts/${path.basename(filePath)}`,
   301	      record_sha256: durableMemoryRecordHash(record),
   302	      protected_action_refused: record.protected_action_refused === true,
   303	      escalation_class: record.escalation?.kind ?? null,
   304	      idempotency_key: `receipt:${record.id}`
   305	    });
   306	    return filePath;
   307	  }
   308	
   309	  saveLog(runId: string, content: string): string {
   310	    const canonicalPath = this.logPath(runId);
   311	    const filePath = existsSync(canonicalPath)
   312	      ? this.attemptLogPath(runId, randomUUID())
   313	      : canonicalPath;
   314	    atomicWrite(filePath, content);
   315	    return filePath;
   316	  }
   317	
   318	  saveAttemptLog(runId: string, receiptId: string, content: string): string {
   319	    const filePath = this.attemptLogPath(runId, receiptId);
   320	    atomicWrite(filePath, content);
   321	    return filePath;
   322	  }
   323	
   324	  getRun(runId: string): GeminxRunRecord | null {
   325	    const filePath = this.runPath(runId);
   326	    const record = readJsonFile<GeminxRunRecord>(filePath);
   327	    if (record === null) return null;
   328	    assertStoredIdentity("run id", runId, record.id, filePath);
   329	    return record;
   330	  }
   331	
   332	  getReceipt(receiptId: string): GeminxReceiptRecord | null {
   333	    const filePath = this.receiptPath(receiptId);
   334	    const record = readJsonFile<GeminxReceiptRecord>(filePath);
   335	    if (record === null) return null;
   336	    assertStoredIdentity("receipt id", receiptId, record.id, filePath);
   337	    return record;
   338	  }
   339	
   340	  acquireRecoveryLease(
   341	    runId: string,
   342	    owner: string,
   343	    options: { ttl_ms?: number; max_retries?: number; now_ms?: number } = {}
   344	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   345	    if (!owner.trim()) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_LEASE_OWNER_REQUIRED");
   346	    const nowMs = options.now_ms ?? Date.now();
   347	    const ttlMs = Math.max(1_000, options.ttl_ms ?? DEFAULT_RECOVERY_LEASE_TTL_MS);
   348	    const configuredMaxRetries = Math.max(1, options.max_retries ?? DEFAULT_RECOVERY_MAX_RETRIES);
   349	
   350	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   351	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   352	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   353	      const current = recoveryState(record, configuredMaxRetries);
   354	      if (current.status === "cancelled") throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_CANCELLED");
   355	      if (current.status === "completed") return record;
   356	
   357	      const activeLease = current.lease && Date.parse(current.lease.expires_at) > nowMs ? current.lease : null;
   358	      if (activeLease) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_LEASE_ACTIVE");
   359	
   360	      const maxRetries = current.max_retries || configuredMaxRetries;
   361	      if (current.retry_count >= maxRetries) throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_RETRY_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED");
   362	
   363	      const generation = current.generation + 1;
   364	      const acquiredAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   365	      const lease: GeminxRecoveryLease = {
   366	        lease_id: randomUUID(),
   367	        owner,
   368	        generation,
   369	        acquired_at: acquiredAt,
   370	        heartbeat_at: acquiredAt,
   371	        expires_at: isoNow(nowMs + ttlMs)
   372	      };
   373	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   374	        ...record,
   375	        updated_at: acquiredAt,
   376	        recovery: {
   377	          ...current,
   378	          status: "leased",
   379	          generation,
   380	          retry_count: current.retry_count + 1,
   381	          max_retries: maxRetries,
   382	          lease,
   383	          cancelled_at: null,
   384	          cancellation_reason: null
   385	        }
   386	      };
   387	      this.saveRun(next);
   388	      return next;
   389	    });
   390	  }
   391	
   392	  heartbeatRecoveryLease(
   393	    runId: string,
   394	    leaseId: string,
   395	    generation: number,
   396	    options: { ttl_ms?: number; now_ms?: number } = {}
   397	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   398	    const nowMs = options.now_ms ?? Date.now();
   399	    const ttlMs = Math.max(1_000, options.ttl_ms ?? DEFAULT_RECOVERY_LEASE_TTL_MS);
   400	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   401	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   402	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   403	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   404	      const lease = assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   405	      const heartbeatAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   406	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   407	        ...record,
   408	        updated_at: heartbeatAt,
   409	        recovery: {
   410	          ...current,
   411	          lease: {
   412	            ...lease,
   413	            heartbeat_at: heartbeatAt,
   414	            expires_at: isoNow(nowMs + ttlMs)
   415	          }
   416	        }
   417	      };
   418	      this.saveRun(next);
   419	      return next;
   420	    });
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  saveRecoveryCheckpoint(
   424	    runId: string,
   425	    leaseId: string,
   426	    generation: number,
   427	    checkpoint: Omit<GeminxRecoveryCheckpoint, "at"> & { at?: string },
   428	    nowMs = Date.now()
   429	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   430	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   431	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   432	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   433	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   434	      assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   435	      if (current.checkpoint && checkpoint.sequence <= current.checkpoint.sequence) {
   436	        throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_CHECKPOINT_SEQUENCE_STALE");
   437	      }
   438	      const at = checkpoint.at ?? isoNow(nowMs);
   439	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   440	        ...record,
   441	        updated_at: at,
   442	        recovery: {
   443	          ...current,
   444	          checkpoint: {
   445	            sequence: checkpoint.sequence,
   446	            label: checkpoint.label,
   447	            at,
   448	            ...(checkpoint.evidence ? { evidence: [...checkpoint.evidence] } : {})
   449	          }
   450	        }
   451	      };
   452	      this.saveRun(next);
   453	      return next;
   454	    });
   455	  }
   456	
   457	  releaseRecoveryLease(runId: string, leaseId: string, generation: number, nowMs = Date.now()): GeminxRunRecord {
   458	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   459	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   460	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   461	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   462	      assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   463	      const updatedAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   464	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   465	        ...record,
   466	        updated_at: updatedAt,
   467	        recovery: { ...current, status: "idle", lease: null }
   468	      };
   469	      this.saveRun(next);
   470	      return next;
   471	    });
   472	  }
   473	
   474	  completeRecovery(
   475	    runId: string,
   476	    leaseId: string,
   477	    generation: number,
   478	    completionReceiptId: string,
   479	    nowMs = Date.now()
   480	  ): GeminxRunRecord {
   481	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   482	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   483	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   484	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   485	      if (current.status === "completed") {
   486	        if (current.completion_receipt_id === completionReceiptId) return record;
   487	        throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
   488	      }
   489	      assertLeaseOwner(current, leaseId, generation, nowMs);
   490	      const updatedAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   491	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   492	        ...record,
   493	        updated_at: updatedAt,
   494	        recovery: {
   495	          ...current,
   496	          status: "completed",
   497	          lease: null,
   498	          completion_receipt_id: completionReceiptId
   499	        }
   500	      };
   501	      this.saveRun(next);
   502	      return next;
   503	    });
   504	  }
   505	
   506	  cancelRecovery(runId: string, reason: string, nowMs = Date.now()): GeminxRunRecord {
   507	    return this.withRunMutationLock(runId, () => {
   508	      const record = this.getRun(runId);
   509	      if (!record) throw new Error(`GEMINX_RECOVERY_RUN_NOT_FOUND:${runId}`);
   510	      const current = recoveryState(record);
   511	      if (current.status === "completed") throw new Error("GEMINX_RECOVERY_ALREADY_COMPLETED");
   512	      if (current.status === "cancelled") return record;
   513	      const cancelledAt = isoNow(nowMs);
   514	      const next: GeminxRunRecord = {
   515	        ...record,
   516	        updated_at: cancelledAt,
   517	        recovery: {
   518	          ...current,
   519	          status: "cancelled",
   520	          lease: null,
   521	          cancelled_at: cancelledAt,
   522	          cancellation_reason: reason.trim() || "cancelled"
   523	        }
   524	      };
   525	      this.saveRun(next);
   526	      return next;
   527	    });
   528	  }
   529	
   530	  listRuns(): GeminxRunRecord[] {
   531	    return readdirSync(this.runsRoot)
   532	      .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(".json"))
   533	      .map((entry) => readIndexedRecord<GeminxRunRecord>(this.runsRoot, entry, "run id"))
   534	      .filter((entry): entry is GeminxRunRecord => entry !== null)
   535	      .sort((left, right) => right.created_at.localeCompare(left.created_at));
   536	  }
   537	
   538	  listReceipts(): GeminxReceiptRecord[] {
   539	    return readdirSync(this.receiptsRoot)
   540	      .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(".json"))
   541	      .map((entry) => readIndexedRecord<GeminxReceiptRecord>(this.receiptsRoot, entry, "receipt id"))
   542	      .filter((entry): entry is GeminxReceiptRecord => entry !== null)
   543	      .sort((left, right) => right.created_at.localeCompare(left.created_at));
   544	  }
   545	}

    44	  acknowledged_at?: string;
