    65	export class CommsStore {
    66	  readonly commsRoot: string;
    67	  readonly runtimeRoot: string;
    68	  private readonly memoryStore: DurableMemoryStore;
    69	
    70	  constructor(runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()) {
    71	    const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(runtimeRoot);
    72	    this.runtimeRoot = resolvedRoot;
    73	    this.commsRoot = path.join(resolvedRoot, "comms");
    74	    this.memoryStore = new DurableMemoryStore({ root: resolvedRoot });
    75	    mkdirSync(this.commsRoot, { recursive: true });
    76	  }
    77	
    78	  private hydrateObjective(msg: GeminxMessage, previous: GeminxMessage | null = null): GeminxMessage {
    79	    const payload = msg.payload && typeof msg.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(msg.payload)
    80	      ? msg.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
    81	    if (msg.type !== "founder_autonomous_job" && payload.command !== "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1") return msg;
    82	    const incoming = msg.objective_contract;
    83	    const prior = previous?.objective_contract;
    84	    const retained = prior && (!incoming || (prior.updated_at > incoming.updated_at)) ? prior : incoming;
    85	    const criteria = Array.isArray(payload.acceptance_criteria)
    86	      ? payload.acceptance_criteria.filter((x): x is string => typeof x === "string" && x.trim().length > 0)
    87	      : [];
    88	    const proposedSpend = payload.spend_ceiling_cents;
    89	    const explicitSpend = typeof proposedSpend === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(proposedSpend) && proposedSpend >= 0
    90	      ? proposedSpend : null;
    91	    const contract = ensureDurableObjective(retained,
    92	      String(payload.objective ?? payload.intent ?? payload.task ?? ""), criteria, payload.read_only === true, explicitSpend);
    93	    if (prior) {
    94	      // A stale worker snapshot must never rewind verified receipts, counted
    95	      // recovery attempts, or a previously applied lower spend ceiling.
    96	      contract.recovery_attempts = Math.min(2, Math.max(contract.recovery_attempts, prior.recovery_attempts));
    97	      const existing = new Map(contract.acceptance_criteria.map(c => [c.id, c]));
    98	      for (const old of prior.acceptance_criteria) {
    99	        const item = existing.get(old.id);
   100	        if (!item) { contract.acceptance_criteria.push(old); continue; }
   101	        if (old.status === "VERIFIED" && old.evidence_receipt &&
   102	          (item.status !== "VERIFIED" || !item.evidence_receipt)) {
   103	          Object.assign(item, old);
   104	        }
   105	      }
   106	      const ceilings = [contract.spend_ceiling_cents, prior.spend_ceiling_cents]
   107	        .filter((v): v is number => typeof v === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(v) && v >= 0);
   108	      contract.spend_ceiling_cents = ceilings.length ? Math.min(...ceilings) : null;
   109	      contract.objective_id = prior.objective_id;
   110	      contract.raw_founder_intent = prior.raw_founder_intent;
   111	      if (contract.recovery_attempts > (incoming?.recovery_attempts ?? 0)) {
   112	        contract.current_stage = prior.current_stage;
   113	        contract.next_action_pointer = prior.next_action_pointer;
   114	        contract.updated_at = prior.updated_at;
   115	      }
   116	    }
   117	    msg.objective_contract = contract;
   118	    return msg;
   119	  }
   120	
   121	  private getFilePath(messageId: string): string {
   122	    const safeId = messageId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_");
   123	    return path.join(this.commsRoot, `${safeId}.json`);
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  /** Preserve a receipt reference before a terminal Founder task is resumed.
   127	   * This is NOT a new worker or a new spend ledger.
   128	   */
   129	  preserveTerminalReceiptForResume(message: GeminxMessage): void {
   130	    const receiptId = String(message.receipt_id ?? "").trim();
   131	    const payload = message.payload && typeof message.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(message.payload)
   132	      ? message.payload as Record<string, unknown> : {};
   133	    const verified = payload.verified_current_state && typeof payload.verified_current_state === "object" && !Array.isArray(payload.verified_current_state)
   134	      ? payload.verified_current_state as Record<string, unknown> : {};
   135	    const receipts = Array.isArray(verified.prior_terminal_receipts)
   136	      ? verified.prior_terminal_receipts.filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object") : [];
   137	    if (receiptId && !receipts.some((item) => (item as Record<string, unknown>).receipt_id === receiptId)) {
   138	      receipts.push({ receipt_id: receiptId, status: message.status, archived_at: new Date().toISOString() });
   139	    }
   140	    const nextEpoch = Math.max(0, Number(message.lease_epoch || 0)) + 1;
   141	    message.lease_epoch = nextEpoch;
   142	    message.payload = {
   143	      ...payload,
   144	      verified_current_state: { ...verified, prior_terminal_receipts: receipts.slice(-12) },
   145	      supervisor_resume_fenced: true,
   146	      supervisor_resume_epoch: nextEpoch,
   147	    };
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  /** Atomic create-only enqueue. A duplicate command may never overwrite a running
   151	   * or completed receipt. Works with the existing persistent comms volume and does
   152	   * not introduce another queue. The deployment must use a shared persistent store.
   153	   */
   154	  createMessageIfAbsent(msg: GeminxMessage): {created:boolean;message:GeminxMessage} {
   155	    this.hydrateObjective(msg);
   156	    const filePath = this.getFilePath(msg.message_id);
   157	    // Write a complete temporary file, then link atomically without clobbering
   158	    // an existing receipt. Readers never observe a half-written JSON message.
   159	    const tempPath = filePath + "." + process.pid + "." + randomUUID() + ".tmp";
   160	    try {
   161	      writeFileSync(tempPath, JSON.stringify(msg, null, 2), { encoding:"utf8", flag:"wx" });
   162	      try {
   163	        linkSync(tempPath, filePath);
   164	      } catch (error: unknown) {
   165	        if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw error;
   166	        const existing = this.getMessage(msg.message_id);
   167	        if (!existing) throw new Error("DISPATCH_EXISTING_MESSAGE_UNREADABLE");
   168	        return {created:false,message:existing};
   169	      }
   170	    } finally {
   171	      rmSync(tempPath, {force:true});
   172	    }
   173	    this.recordDurableLifecycle(null, msg, filePath);
   174	    return {created:true,message:msg};
   175	  }
   176	
   177	  withMessageLock<T>(messageId: string, operation: () => T, timeoutMs = 10_000): T {
   178	    const filePath = this.getFilePath(messageId);
   179	    const lockDir = filePath + ".update-lock";
   180	    const start = Date.now();
   181	    let acquired = false;
   182	
   183	    while (!acquired) {
   184	      try {
   185	        mkdirSync(lockDir);
   186	        const ownerPath = path.join(lockDir, "owner.json");
   187	        writeFileSync(ownerPath, JSON.stringify({ pid: process.pid, createdAt: Date.now() }), "utf8");
   188	        acquired = true;
   189	      } catch (err: unknown) {
   190	        if ((err as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "EEXIST") {
   191	          try {
   192	            const ownerPath = path.join(lockDir, "owner.json");
   193	            if (existsSync(ownerPath)) {
   194	              const info = JSON.parse(readFileSync(ownerPath, "utf8"));
   195	              if (info?.pid && !isPidAlive(info.pid)) {
   196	                rmSync(lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   197	                continue;
   198	              }
   199	            } else if (Date.now() - statSync(lockDir).mtimeMs > timeoutMs) {
   200	              rmSync(lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   201	              continue;
   202	            }
   203	          } catch {}
   204	
   205	          if (Date.now() - start > timeoutMs) {
   206	            throw new Error(`MESSAGE_LOCK_TIMEOUT_${messageId}`);
   207	          }
   208	          const waitBuffer = new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4));
   209	          Atomics.wait(waitBuffer, 0, 0, 15);
   210	        } else {
   211	          throw err;
   212	        }
   213	      }
   214	    }
   215	
   216	    try {
   217	      return operation();
   218	    } finally {
   219	      try { rmSync(lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {}
   220	    }
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  /** Atomic-on-shared-volume QUEUED claim. The short-lived mkdir lock serialises
   224	   * claims across backend processes that use the SAME persistent CommsStore volume.
   225	   * Does NOT steal active claim locks from live processes even if time has elapsed.
   226	   * Requires provable stale ownership (verified dead PID) for safe recovery.
   227	   */
   228	  claimQueuedMessage(messageId: string, claimantId: string, fenced = false): { claimed: boolean; message: GeminxMessage | null; reason?: string } {
   229	    const filePath = this.getFilePath(messageId);
   230	    const lockPath = filePath + ".claim-lock";
   231	    const owner = String(claimantId || "").trim();
   232	    if (!/^[a-zA-Z0-9._:-]{1,128}$/.test(owner)) return { claimed: false, message: null, reason: "INVALID_CLAIMANT" };
   233	
   234	    // Provable stale claim recovery: only recover if lock holder PID is verified dead
   235	    try {
   236	      if (existsSync(lockPath)) {
   237	        const ownerFile = path.join(lockPath, "claim-owner.json");
   238	        let canRecover = false;
   239	        if (existsSync(ownerFile)) {
   240	          try {
   241	            const info = JSON.parse(readFileSync(ownerFile, "utf8"));
   242	            if (info?.pid && !isPidAlive(info.pid)) {
   243	              canRecover = true;
   244	            }
   245	          } catch {
   246	            canRecover = Date.now() - statSync(lockPath).mtimeMs > 60_000;
   247	          }
   248	        } else if (Date.now() - statSync(lockPath).mtimeMs > 60_000) {
   249	          canRecover = true;
   250	        }
   251	
   252	        if (canRecover) {
   253	          rmSync(lockPath, { recursive: true, force: true });
   254	        }
   255	      }
   256	    } catch {}
   257	
   258	    try {
   259	      mkdirSync(lockPath);
   260	      try {
   261	        writeFileSync(
   262	          path.join(lockPath, "claim-owner.json"),
   263	          JSON.stringify({ claimantId: owner, pid: process.pid, claimedAt: Date.now() }),
   264	          "utf8"
   265	        );
   266	      } catch {}
   267	    } catch (error: unknown) {
   268	      if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "EEXIST") {
   269	        return { claimed: false, message: this.getMessage(messageId), reason: "CLAIM_LOCK_BUSY" };
   270	      }
   271	      throw error;
   272	    }
   273	    try {
   274	      const current = this.getMessage(messageId);
   275	      if (!current) return { claimed: false, message: null, reason: "MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND" };
   276	      if (current.status !== "QUEUED") {
   277	        return { claimed: false, message: current, reason: "ALREADY_CLAIMED_OR_NOT_QUEUED" };
   278	      }
   279	      current.status = "ACKNOWLEDGED";
   280	      current.acknowledged_at = new Date().toISOString();
   281	      if (fenced || current.lease_epoch !== undefined) {
   282	        current.lease_epoch = Math.max(0, Number(current.lease_epoch || 0)) + 1;
   283	        current.claim_owner = owner;
   284	      }
   285	      this.saveMessage(current);
   286	      return { claimed: true, message: current };
   287	    } finally {
   288	      rmSync(lockPath, { recursive: true, force: true });
   289	    }
   290	  }
   291	
   292	  saveMessage(msg: GeminxMessage): GeminxMessage {
   293	    return this.withMessageLock(msg.message_id, () => {
   294	      return this.saveMessageInternal(msg);
   295	    });
   296	  }
   297	
   298	  private saveMessageInternal(msg: GeminxMessage): GeminxMessage {
   299	    const filePath = this.getFilePath(msg.message_id);
   300	    const previous = existsSync(filePath)
   301	      ? (() => {
   302	          try { return JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as GeminxMessage; }
   303	          catch { return null; }
   304	        })()
   305	      : null;
   306	
   307	    // Terminal protect gate: A stale worker or delayed heartbeat may NEVER revert
   308	    // an already terminal message (COMPLETED, BLOCKED, FAILED, CANCELLED, EXPIRED)
   309	    // back to an active executing state (RUNNING, ACKNOWLEDGED, DELIVERED) or resurrect
   310	    // it through an unauthorized QUEUED transition.
   311	    // Stale workers also CANNOT append misleading terminal activity or overwrite
   312	    // terminal payload, receipts or accepted follow-ups.
   313	    const terminalStates = new Set(["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"]);
   314	    if (previous && terminalStates.has(previous.status)) {
   315	      const incomingPayload = msg.payload && typeof msg.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(msg.payload)
   316	        ? (msg.payload as Record<string, unknown>)
   317	        : {};
   318	      const isSupervisorAuthorizedResume =
   319	        (msg.status === "QUEUED" &&
   320	        (incomingPayload.supervisor_resume_fenced === true ||
   321	         incomingPayload.resume_same_objective === true ||
   322	         incomingPayload.resume_after_internal_repair === true ||
   323	         incomingPayload.resume_after_provider_restore === true ||
   324	         incomingPayload.resume_after_connector_result === true ||
   325	         (Array.isArray(msg.activity) && msg.activity.some(e => e.phase === "resume" || e.tool === "chrome_status" || e.tool === "browser_resume" || e.phase === "connector-return")))) ||
   326	        (msg.status === "BLOCKED" && incomingPayload.founder_reset_fenced === true);
   327	
   328	      if (!isSupervisorAuthorizedResume) {
   329	        // Disallow unauthorized resurrection or status transition from terminal state
   330	        msg.status = previous.status;
   331	        msg.completed_at = previous.completed_at;
   332	        msg.receipt_id = previous.receipt_id;
   333	        msg.reply_payload = previous.reply_payload;
   334	        msg.payload = previous.payload; // Preserve canonical terminal payload
   335	        msg.activity = previous.activity; // FREEZE activity! Stale worker cannot append misleading activity
   336	        msg.followups = previous.followups;
   337	        msg.claim_owner = previous.claim_owner;
   338	        msg.lease_epoch = previous.lease_epoch;
   339	      } else {
   340	        // Legitimate explicit supervisor recovery:
   341	        // Consume one-time supervisor fencing token so workers cannot replay it
   342	        delete incomingPayload.supervisor_resume_fenced;
   343	        delete incomingPayload.founder_reset_fenced;
   344	      }
   345	    }
   346	
   347	    // Monotonic version assignment
   348	    msg.version = Math.max(Number(msg.version || 0), Number(previous?.version || 0)) + 1;
   349	
   350	    this.hydrateObjective(msg, previous);
   351	    const tempPath = `${filePath}.${process.pid}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
   352	    const content = JSON.stringify(msg, null, 2);
   353	
   354	    try {
   355	      writeFileSync(tempPath, content, { encoding: "utf8", flag: "w" });
   356	      renameSync(tempPath, filePath);
   357	    } catch (error) {
   358	      rmSync(tempPath, { force: true });
   359	      throw error;
   360	    }
   361	
   362	    this.recordDurableLifecycle(previous, msg, filePath);
   363	    return msg;
   364	  }
   365	
   366	  /**
   367	   * Versioned conditional update: update the message only if its current stored version
   368	   * matches expectedVersion (optimistic concurrency control).
   369	   * Fully atomic across processes via file locking.
   370	   */
   371	  updateMessageVersioned(
   372	    messageId: string,
   373	    expectedVersion: number,
   374	    updater: ((current: GeminxMessage) => GeminxMessage) | Partial<GeminxMessage>
   375	  ): { updated: boolean; message: GeminxMessage | null; reason?: string } {
   376	    return this.withMessageLock(messageId, () => {
   377	      const current = this.getMessage(messageId);
   378	      if (!current) return { updated: false, message: null, reason: "MESSAGE_NOT_FOUND" };
   379	      const currentVersion = Number(current.version || 0);
   380	      if (currentVersion !== expectedVersion) {
   381	        return { updated: false, message: current, reason: "VERSION_CONFLICT" };
   382	      }
   383	      const updated = typeof updater === "function" ? updater(current) : { ...current, ...updater };
   384	      const saved = this.saveMessageInternal(updated);
   385	      return { updated: true, message: saved };
   386	    });
   387	  }
   388	
   389	
   390	  private recordDurableLifecycle(previous: GeminxMessage | null, msg: GeminxMessage, filePath: string): void {
   391	    const previousActivity = Array.isArray(previous?.activity) ? previous.activity.at(-1) : null;
   392	    const latestActivity = Array.isArray(msg.activity) ? msg.activity.at(-1) : null;
   393	    const activityChanged = previousActivity?.event_id !== latestActivity?.event_id;
   394	    const stateChanged = previous === null || previous.status !== msg.status;
   395	    const receiptChanged = previous?.receipt_id !== msg.receipt_id;
   396	    if (!stateChanged && !receiptChanged && !activityChanged) return;
   397	
   398	    const payload = msg.payload && typeof msg.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(msg.payload)
   399	      ? msg.payload as Record<string, unknown>
   400	      : {};
   401	    const reply = msg.reply_payload && typeof msg.reply_payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(msg.reply_payload)
   402	      ? msg.reply_payload as Record<string, unknown>
   403	      : {};
   404	    const objective = String(payload.objective ?? payload.goal ?? reply.summary ?? reply.message ?? latestActivity?.label ?? msg.type);
   405	    const timestamp = latestActivity?.at ?? msg.completed_at ?? msg.last_heartbeat_at ?? msg.created_at;
   406	    const recordRef = `comms/${path.basename(filePath)}`;
   407	    const recordHash = durableMemoryRecordHash(msg);
   408	    const sourceEvent = latestActivity?.event_id ?? msg.receipt_id ?? `${msg.status}:${timestamp}`;
   409	    const append = (eventType: string, status: string, summary: string, suffix: string, escalationClass: string | null = null) => {
   410	      this.memoryStore.append({
   411	        stream_type: "task",
   412	        stream_id: msg.message_id,
   413	        task_id: msg.message_id,
   414	        run_id: msg.message_id,
   415	        correlation_id: msg.correlation_id,
   416	        conversation_id: typeof payload.conversation_id === "string" ? payload.conversation_id : null,
   417	        event_type: eventType,
   418	        timestamp,
   419	        actor: msg.sender,
   420	        status,
   421	        summary,
   422	        record_ref: recordRef,
   423	        record_sha256: recordHash,
   424	        escalation_class: escalationClass,
   425	        idempotency_key: `comms:${msg.message_id}:${suffix}`
   426	      });
   427	    };
   428	
   429	    if (previous === null) {
   430	      append("ACCEPTED", msg.status, objective, "accepted");
   431	      append("PLANNED", "planned", `Founder objective accepted with durable execution contract: ${objective}`, "planned");
   432	    }
   433	
   434	    const phase = String(latestActivity?.phase ?? "").toLowerCase();
   435	    if (activityChanged && latestActivity) {
   436	      if (/verify|verification|test|proof/.test(phase)) {
   437	        append("VERIFICATION", latestActivity.status, latestActivity.label || "Verification activity recorded.", `verification:${sourceEvent}`);
   438	      } else if (/plan|planning/.test(phase)) {
   439	        append("PLANNED", latestActivity.status, latestActivity.label || objective, `planned:${sourceEvent}`);
   440	      } else if (!/queued|queue|cancel|human/.test(phase)) {
   441	        append("ACTION", latestActivity.status, latestActivity.label || objective, `action:${sourceEvent}`);
   442	      }
   443	    }
   444	
   445	    if (msg.status === "WAITING_HUMAN" && (stateChanged || activityChanged)) {
   446	      append("ESCALATION", msg.status, latestActivity?.label || objective, `escalation:${sourceEvent}`, "human_boundary");
   447	    }
   448	
   449	    const terminal = ["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"].includes(msg.status);
   450	    if (terminal && stateChanged) {
   451	      append("OUTCOME", msg.status, String(reply.summary ?? reply.message ?? objective), `outcome:${msg.status}:${sourceEvent}`);
   452	    }
   453	
   454	    if (msg.receipt_id && receiptChanged) {
   455	      append("RECEIPT", msg.status, String(reply.summary ?? reply.message ?? objective), `receipt:${msg.receipt_id}`);
   456	    }
   457	  }
   458	
   459	  getMessage(messageId: string): GeminxMessage | null {
   460	    const filePath = this.getFilePath(messageId);
   461	    if (!existsSync(filePath)) return null;
   462	
   463	    try {
   464	      return this.hydrateObjective(JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, "utf8")) as GeminxMessage);
   465	    } catch (error) {
   466	      console.error(
   467	        `[geminx-comms-store] failed to read message ${messageId}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknown"}`
   468	      );
   469	      return null;
   470	    }
   471	  }
   472	
   473	  latestWaitingHumanBrowserHandoff(options?: {
   474	    maxAgeMs?: number;
   475	    nowMs?: number;
   476	  }): {
   477	    message_id: string;
   478	    correlation_id: string;
   479	    session_id: string;
   480	    url: string | null;
   481	    challenge: string | null;
   482	    event_at: string;
   483	  } | null {
   484	    const configuredMaxAgeMs = Number(
   485	      options?.maxAgeMs ?? process.env.GEMINX_BROWSER_WAITING_HANDOFF_RECOVERY_MAX_AGE_MS ?? 30 * 60_000
   486	    );
   487	    const maxAgeMs = Number.isFinite(configuredMaxAgeMs)
   488	      ? Math.max(60_000, configuredMaxAgeMs)
   489	      : 30 * 60_000;
   490	    const nowMs = Number(options?.nowMs ?? Date.now());
   491	
   492	    const candidates = this.listMessages()
   493	      .filter(message => isPrimaryAgentRecipient(message.recipient) && message.status === "WAITING_HUMAN")
   494	      .map(message => {
   495	        const activity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
   496	        const humanEvent = [...activity].reverse().find(item => item.requires_human === true && Boolean(item.session_id));
   497	        if (!humanEvent?.session_id) return null;
   498	
   499	        const sessionEvents = activity.filter(item => item.session_id === humanEvent.session_id);
   500	        const latestSessionEvent = sessionEvents.at(-1);
   501	        if (!latestSessionEvent || latestSessionEvent.requires_human !== true) return null;
   502	
   503	        const eventAt = Date.parse(humanEvent.at || message.created_at);
   504	        if (!Number.isFinite(eventAt) || nowMs - eventAt > maxAgeMs) return null;
   505	
   506	        const ownsSession = activity.some(item => item.tool === "browser_session_start" && item.session_id === humanEvent.session_id);
   507	        return {
   508	          message,
   509	          event: humanEvent,
   510	          ownsSession
   511	        };
   512	      })
   513	      .filter((item): item is NonNullable<typeof item> => item !== null)
   514	      .sort((left, right) => {
   515	        if (left.ownsSession !== right.ownsSession) return left.ownsSession ? -1 : 1;
   516	        const leftAt = left.event.at || left.message.created_at;
   517	        const rightAt = right.event.at || right.message.created_at;
   518	        return rightAt.localeCompare(leftAt);
   519	      });
   520	
   521	    const selected = candidates[0];
   522	    if (!selected) return null;
   523	    return {
   524	      message_id: selected.message.message_id,
   525	      correlation_id: selected.message.correlation_id,
   526	      session_id: selected.event.session_id!,
   527	      url: selected.event.url ?? null,
   528	      challenge: selected.event.challenge ?? null,
   529	      event_at: selected.event.at || selected.message.created_at,
   530	    };
   531	  }
   532	
   533	  resumeWaitingHumanForBrowserSession(sessionIdRaw: string): string[] {
   534	    const sessionId = sessionIdRaw.trim();
   535	    if (!sessionId) return [];
   536	
   537	    const candidates = this.listMessages()
   538	      .filter(message => {
   539	        if (!isPrimaryAgentRecipient(message.recipient) || message.status !== "WAITING_HUMAN") return false;
   540	        const activity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
   541	        return activity.some(event => event.requires_human === true && event.session_id === sessionId);
   542	      })
   543	      .map(message => {
   544	        const activity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
   545	        const ownsSession = activity.some(event => event.tool === "browser_session_start" && event.session_id === sessionId);
   546	        return { message, ownsSession };
   547	      })
   548	      .sort((left, right) => {
   549	        if (left.ownsSession !== right.ownsSession) return left.ownsSession ? -1 : 1;
   550	        return left.message.created_at.localeCompare(right.message.created_at);
   551	      });
   552	
   553	    const selected = candidates[0]?.message;
   554	    if (!selected) return [];
   555	
   556	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
   557	    selected.status = "QUEUED";
   558	    delete selected.completed_at;
   559	    delete selected.receipt_id;
   560	    delete selected.reply_payload;
   561	    delete selected.next_attempt_at;
   562	    delete selected.last_error;
   563	    selected.last_heartbeat_at = now;
   564	    selected.activity = [...(Array.isArray(selected.activity) ? selected.activity : []), {
   565	      event_id: randomUUID(),
   566	      at: now,
   567	      phase: "human",
   568	      status: "done" as const,
   569	      label: "Human browser step completed · resuming the same task",
   570	      tool: "browser_resume",
   571	      session_id: sessionId,
   572	      requires_human: false
   573	    }].slice(-80);
   574	    this.saveMessage(selected);
   575	    return [selected.message_id];
   576	  }
   577	
   578	  resumeWaitingCreatorForConnectedChromeBridge(options?: {
   579	    maxAgeMs?: number;
   580	    nowMs?: number;
   581	  }): string[] {
   582	    const configuredMaxAgeMs = Number(
   583	      options?.maxAgeMs ?? process.env.GEMINX_CHROME_CREATOR_AUTORESUME_MAX_AGE_MS ?? 24 * 60 * 60_000
   584	    );
   585	    const maxAgeMs = Number.isFinite(configuredMaxAgeMs)
   586	      ? Math.max(60_000, configuredMaxAgeMs)
   587	      : 24 * 60 * 60_000;
   588	    const nowMs = Number(options?.nowMs ?? Date.now());
   589	
   590	    const candidates = this.listMessages()
   591	      .filter(message => {
   592	        if (!isPrimaryAgentRecipient(message.recipient)) return false;
   593	        const activity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
   594	        const replyText = JSON.stringify(message.reply_payload ?? {});
   595	        const recoverableChromeLock =
   596	          message.status === "BLOCKED" &&
   597	          /BROWSER_SESSION_HUMAN_CONTROL_LOCK/i.test(replyText);
   598	        if (message.status !== "WAITING_HUMAN" && !recoverableChromeLock) return false;
   599	
   600	        const latestHuman = [...activity].reverse().find(event => event.requires_human === true);
   601	        const latestEventAt = activity.at(-1)?.at ?? message.completed_at ?? message.created_at;
   602	        const eventAt = Date.parse(latestHuman?.at || latestEventAt);
   603	        if (!Number.isFinite(eventAt) || nowMs - eventAt > maxAgeMs) return false;
   604	
   605	        const searchable = [
   606	          JSON.stringify(message.payload ?? {}),
   607	          replyText,
   608	          ...activity.flatMap(event => [event.label, event.url ?? "", event.challenge ?? "", event.tool ?? ""])
   609	        ].join(" ");
   610	        return /\broblox\b|game\s+creator|geminx\s+creator|creator\s+(?:run|game|publish|play|repair|test)/i.test(searchable);
   611	      })
   612	      .map(message => {
   613	        const activity = Array.isArray(message.activity) ? message.activity : [];
   614	        const latestHuman = [...activity].reverse().find(event => event.requires_human === true);
   615	        return {
   616	          message,
   617	          eventAt: latestHuman?.at || activity.at(-1)?.at || message.completed_at || message.created_at,
   618	          priority: message.priority.toUpperCase() === "P0" ? 0 : 1
   619	        };
   620	      })
   621	      .sort((left, right) => left.priority - right.priority || right.eventAt.localeCompare(left.eventAt));
   622	
   623	    const selected = candidates[0]?.message;
   624	    if (!selected) return [];
   625	
   626	    const now = new Date(nowMs).toISOString();
   627	    selected.status = "QUEUED";
   628	    delete selected.completed_at;
   629	    delete selected.receipt_id;
   630	    delete selected.reply_payload;
   631	    delete selected.next_attempt_at;
   632	    delete selected.last_error;
   633	    selected.last_heartbeat_at = now;
   634	    selected.activity = [...(Array.isArray(selected.activity) ? selected.activity : []), {
   635	      event_id: randomUUID(),
   636	      at: now,
   637	      phase: "human",
   638	      status: "done" as const,
   639	      label: "Founder Chrome Bridge connected · autonomously resuming the same Creator task",
   640	      tool: "chrome_status",
   641	      requires_human: false
   642	    }].slice(-80);
   643	    this.saveMessage(selected);
   644	    return [selected.message_id];
   645	  }
   646	
   647	  listMessages(): GeminxMessage[] {
   648	    const files: GeminxMessage[] = [];
   649	    try {
   650	      const entries = readdirSync(this.commsRoot);
   651	      for (const entry of entries) {
   652	        if (entry.endsWith(".json")) {
   653	          const msg = this.getMessage(entry.slice(0, -5));
   654	          if (msg) files.push(msg);
   655	        }
   656	      }
   657	    } catch (error) {
   658	      console.error(
   659	        `[geminx-comms-store] failed to list messages: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknown"}`
   660	      );
   661	    }
   662	    return files;
   663	  }
   664	}
